Conclusion: TFM虽然在各单变量预测任务上表现优异，但其自回归构造的多变量联合分布不忠实于真实分布。提出的两个一致性要求为评估表格预测模型的联合分布质量提供了不依赖真实后验的可操作检验框架，提示未来需要开发真正能生成一致联合分布的模型。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) are currently the best approach to tabular prediction problems. They are constructed as transformers that approximate the Bayesian posterior predictive distribution based on a pre-training prior. These univariate predictors can be converted into multivariate ones autoregressively by sampling one target and adding it to the features.
  However, the faithfulness of the resulting joint has not been investigated. Furthermore, TFMs cannot be evaluated against the posterior itself, at least not on real-world datasets, because the ground-truth distribution is unknown. We therefore propose asking a different question: could a model's predictions result from any joint distribution? To answer this question, we pose two requirements that any such model must satisfy. The first is marginalization consistency, which demands that marginalized conditionals are equal to directly predicted marginals. The second is factorization consistency, which demands that different factorization orders result in equal joint distributions. Every TFM that we evaluate violates both of these requirements for both classification and regression across all datasets.

</details>


### [67] [ProDVI: Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06015)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProDVI框架利用代码生成型大语言模型产生可执行的Python函数作为环境动态的粗略假设，生成合成转移数据来预训练actor-critic框架中价值网络的状态-动作编码器，从而为在线强化学习提供动态感知的归纳偏置，无需预收集数据集、高保真模拟器或元学习即可提升无模型RL的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习样本效率极低，部分原因是RL智能体通常从零初始化，需要通过在线交互获取任务相关知识。现有方法依赖预收集数据集、高保真模拟器或跨任务元学习，但这些前提条件可能难以获得甚至不可用。因此需要一种不依赖这些资源即可获取有效初始化的方法。

Method: ProDVI包含两个阶段：首先，提示代码生成型大语言模型生成可执行的Python函数，编码对环境动态的粗略假设；然后利用这些函数合成转移数据，构建辅助动态预测目标来预训练actor-critic架构中价值网络的状态-动作编码器。生成的程序仅用于表征预训练，不要求精确模拟目标环境，其偏差可通过在线学习从真实转移和奖励中修正。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control Suite任务上的实验表明，ProDVI能有效提升无模型RL算法的样本效率。

Conclusion: ProDVI证明了将LLM编码的常识与领域知识作为程序化动态先验来初始化价值网络表示是可行的，为缺乏数据、模拟器或相关任务资源的场景提供了一种新的RL初始化路径。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) is notoriously sample inefficient. One contributing factor is that RL agents are typically initialized from scratch, forcing them to acquire task-relevant knowledge through online interaction. Existing approaches obtain informative initializations through pre-collected datasets, high-fidelity simulators, or meta-learning over related tasks, but these prerequisites may be difficult to access or even unavailable. In this paper, we propose Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization (ProDVI), a framework that leverages the commonsense and domain knowledge encoded in large language models to initialize RL agents without relying on these resources. Specifically, ProDVI prompts a code-generating language model to produce executable Python functions that encode coarse hypotheses about environment dynamics. These functions are then used to generate synthetic transitions. Based on these transitions, we construct an auxiliary dynamics prediction objective to pretrain the state-action encoder of the value network in an actor-critic framework. The learned representation provides dynamics-aware inductive biases before online RL begins. Importantly, the generated programs are used only for representation pretraining and are not required to faithfully simulate the target environment. While the generated programs may be inaccurate, their induced initialization can be corrected through online learning from real transitions and rewards. Experiments on OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control Suite tasks show that ProDVI can effectively improve the sample efficiency of model-free RL algorithms.

</details>


### [68] [BioKD: Selective Physiology-to-Video Knowledge Distillation via Reliability Gate for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06023)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Hongjun Liu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BioKD框架，利用生理信号作为特权信息在训练阶段指导视频学生模型学习情感表征，推理时仅依赖非侵入式视频输入，通过样本级可靠性感知门控机制和渐进式蒸馏策略抑制不可靠生理监督带来的负迁移，在DEAP和AMIGOS数据集上的一致优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频情感识别在面对模糊或社会性掩饰的行为线索时存在局限，而生理信号虽更可靠但部署性差（需要额外传感设备和多模态同步）。现有生理到视频的知识蒸馏面临生理教师监督噪声大、不稳定等问题，包括受试者间差异、信号伪影和时间不一致性，这些因素可能导致负迁移。

Method: BioKD框架的核心包括：（1）以生理信号作为特权信息（privileged information）在训练阶段指导视频学生模型；（2）样本级可靠性感知门控机制，用于自适应调节知识迁移的强度；（3）渐进式蒸馏策略，实现更稳定的跨模态蒸馏。推理阶段仅使用视频输入，不增加额外开销。

Result: 在DEAP和AMIGOS数据集上，BioKD在试次级（trial-wise）和受试者级（subject-wise）评估协议下均一致优于代表性基线。具体地，DEAP试次级唤醒度达到68.01%，更具挑战性的受试者级设置下达到65.29%。此外，BioKD有效缓解了过度自信的教师错误，优于仅使用熵加权的策略，且推理时无额外开销。

Conclusion: BioKD通过显式建模生理监督的可靠性，有效解决了跨模态蒸馏中不可靠监督导致的负迁移问题，在保持推理阶段非侵入式和零额外开销的前提下实现了优越的情感识别性能，验证了显式建模监督可靠性的重要性。

Abstract: To address the limitations of video-based emotion recognition under ambiguous or socially masked behavioral cues, as well as the poor deployability of physiological signals, this paper proposes a reliability-aware physiology-to-video knowledge distillation framework, termed BioKD. The proposed framework leverages physiological signals as privileged information during training to guide a video-based student model in learning deep affective representations, while relying solely on non-intrusive video inputs at inference time. To cope with the high noise and instability of physiological teacher supervision caused by inter-subject variability, signal artifacts, and temporal inconsistency, BioKD incorporates a sample-wise reliability-aware gating mechanism together with a progressive distillation strategy. By adaptively regulating the strength of knowledge transfer, the framework suppresses negative transfer induced by unreliable physiological supervision and enables more stable cross-modal distillation. Experiments on DEAP and AMIGOS show that BioKD consistently outperforms representative baselines under both trial-wise and subject-wise evaluation protocols for valence and arousal recognition. For example, BioKD achieves 68.01\% on DEAP (trial-wise arousal) and 65.29\% under the more challenging subject-wise setting, demonstrating improved performance under a subject-independent evaluation setting. Further analyses show that BioKD effectively mitigates overconfident teacher errors and outperforms an entropy-only weighting strategy, confirming the importance of explicitly modeling supervision reliability. In addition, BioKD introduces no additional inference-time overhead relative to the same video student architecture and removes the need for physiological sensing and multimodal synchronization.

</details>


### [69] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoThread是一种混合自适应线程调优方法，利用PINO（物理信息神经算子）预测线程数，结合有限源M/M/1排队模型约束预测，并在线细调以匹配资源分配，缓解RL推理中的仿真器瓶颈。实验显示平均加速18.4%，吞吐量达到XGBoost的1.7倍和Reinforcer的1.8倍，执行时间最多减少83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL推理中仿真器端执行开销成为瓶颈，工作负载高度动态且对运行时线程配置敏感。现有多线程策略难以在执行前或执行中匹配线程资源，导致资源争用、调度开销增加和吞吐量降低。

Method: 通过经验分析识别任务执行时间与调度时间之比是决定最优线程数的关键因素。提出AutoThread，包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用PINO作为线程数预测器；(2) 引入有限源M/M/1排队模型约束和引导预测，在动态工作负载下实现快速准确估计；(3) 执行负载感知的在线微调以补偿预测误差并优化资源分配。

Result: AutoThread相比静态策略平均加速18.4%；平均吞吐量达到XGBoost的1.7倍、Reinforcer的1.8倍；与最先进方法相比执行时间最多减少83.8%。

Conclusion: AutoThread通过结合预测模型和排队理论，在动态RL推断工作负载下实现了高效、准确的线程资源自适应配置，显著优于现有静态与启发式方法，证明了该混合方法的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [70] [Dynamic Graph Prompting via Topology-Routed Mixed-Curvature Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06031)
*Quanxin Wang,Xuanting Xie,Bingheng Li,Xingtong Yu,Shuo Wang,Ruiyi Fang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurvPrompt，一种面向动态图的拓扑路由几何提示框架。现有动态图提示方法在单一固定嵌入空间中操作，但作者揭示局部聚类和度异质性的时间偏移会重组边曲率谱，即最优表示几何随局部拓扑动态演化。CurvPrompt维护一组曲率多样化的黎曼专家库，每个专家配有可学习提示，通过拓扑感知门控将每个节点-时间实例动态路由到稀疏专家子集，构建个性化混合曲率表示。预训练阶段采用软路由构建连续拓扑-几何映射，下游适应阶段切换为均匀权重的硬Top-K路由。实验表明该方法在四大多个基准数据集的少样本链接预测和节点分类任务上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图提示方法在单一固定嵌入空间中操作，未能意识到动态图中局部聚类与度异质性的时间演化会改变边曲率谱，即最优表示几何应随局部拓扑动态变化。作者将此未解决的不匹配问题形式化为"几何欠适应"（geometry under-adaptation）。

Method: CurvPrompt框架包含：(1) 曲率多样化的黎曼专家库，每位专家配备可学习提示；(2) 拓扑感知门控机制，将每个节点-时间实例动态路由到稀疏专家子集，构建个性化混合曲率表示；(3) 预训练阶段使用软路由建立连续拓扑-几何映射，下游适应阶段切换为均匀权重的硬Top-K路由以保障参数效率和训练稳定性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，CurvPrompt在少样本链接预测任务上显著优于基线方法，并在节点分类任务上提供了一致且稳定的性能提升，验证了几何适应性提示的必要性。

Conclusion: 动态图中的最优表示几何并非固定不变，而是随局部拓扑结构动态演化。CurvPrompt通过曲率多样化的黎曼专家和拓扑感知路由机制，成功实现了几何自适应的动态图提示学习，在标签稀缺场景下显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Dynamic graph prompting freezes a pre-trained temporal backbone and adapts it to label-scarce downstream tasks using lightweight prompts. However, existing methods operate within a single, fixed embedding space. In this work, we reveal that temporal shifts in local clustering and degree heterogeneity actively reorganize the edge curvature spectrum---indicating that the optimal representation geometry dynamically evolves with local topology over time. We formalize this unaddressed mismatch as geometry under-adaptation. To overcome this limitation, we propose CurvPrompt, a topology-routed geometry prompting framework for dynamic graphs. Instead of relying on a single space, CurvPrompt maintains a bank of curvature-diverse Riemannian experts, each paired with a learnable prompt. A topology-aware gate dynamically routes each node--time instance to a sparse subset of experts, constructing a personalized mixed-curvature representation. To ensure parameter efficiency and training stability under extreme label scarcity, CurvPrompt employs soft routing during pre-training to build a continuous topology--geometry mapping, and transitions to hard Top-K routing with uniform weights during downstream adaptation. Extensive experiments across four benchmark datasets show that CurvPrompt significantly advances few-shot link prediction while delivering strong, consistent performance on node classification tasks, validating the necessity of geometry-adaptive prompting.

</details>


### [71] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种逆强化学习（IRL）奖励模型在北极航运AI导航中的应用，基于3186条AIS航行记录、202艘船、9个北极航运季的数据。非线性奖励模型比线性基线在保留似然度上提升50.9%，但添加船级潜在上下文变量反而使性能下降16.5%。系统分析表明，表面上的船级行为差异主要由可观测的航线与环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船级因素。此外，预测精度、路线保真度和奖励迁移三个指标产生不同的模型排名，说明单一指标不足以评估习得奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助导航对北极航运适应快速变化的海冰条件至关重要，但其可靠部署需要可解释且对环境变化鲁棒的奖励模型。现有meta-IRL方法引入潜在上下文变量以捕捉行为异质性，但尚不清楚这些潜在表征是否真正恢复隐藏偏好，还是仅仅重新编码了观测状态中已有的信息。

Method: 在3186条来自202艘船的AIS航行轨迹数据上（覆盖9个北极航运季），构建受控评估实验，比较三种模型：线性共享奖励模型、非线性共享奖励模型、以及基于相同非线性架构并添加潜在上下文的模型。通过行为分析、上下文探测和预注册的特征隐藏消融实验系统评估各模型。

Result: 非线性奖励较线性基线在保留似然度上提升50.9%，但添加船级潜在上下文后性能下降16.5%。特征隐藏消融实验证明表面上的船级差异主要源于可观测的航线与环境条件。预测精度、路线保真度和奖励迁移三个评价指标产生不同的模型排名。

Conclusion: 在添加每船潜在上下文之前，应先测试已有观测的路线、环境和船级特征是否能解释行为变异性。单一评估指标不足以判断习得奖励的质量，需要多维度评估以确保AI在安全关键领域（如北极航运导航）的可信部署。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [72] [Is Self-Pretraining really useful to improve diagnosis in medical Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06122)
*Omar Coser,Antonio Orvieto,Paolo Soda,Loredana Zollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自预训练（SPT）对医疗时间序列任务中Transformer模型性能的影响，覆盖面广（多模态、多变量乃至单变量），在三个基准数据集上验证了SPT能稳定提升分类准确率0-6个百分点，且模型越深收益越大，表明SPT是一种无需修改架构即可通用提升性能的简单策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有证据表明Transformer架构在长上下文基准测试中受益于自预训练（SPT），但该优势是否能推广到多样的医疗时间序列（多模态、多变量乃至简单的单变量）尚不明确。尤其是在数据有限的临床场景下，评估SPT对模型性能与可扩展性的影响具有重要价值。

Method: 在三个代表性医疗时间序列任务（康复机器人Camargo、压力检测Non-EEG Stress、帕金森检测Gait Parkinson's）上评估Transformer架构。模型分别从零训练或通过SPT训练，SPT采用四种基于掩码的目标以促进时间与跨模态表征学习，并系统改变模型深度以考察容量与预训练收益的交互作用。

Result: SPT在所有数据集和配置下稳定提升分类准确率0-6个百分点，收益依赖于掩码策略、数据集和架构；不仅多变量设置下有效，在仅限单变量输入时也有增益；模型越深，从预训练中学到的丰富时间表征获益越大。

Conclusion: SPT是一种简单且通用的策略，无需任务特定的架构改动即可增强Transformer在医疗时间序列任务上的性能，有望在数据受限的临床环境中提升模型的鲁棒性与准确性。

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [73] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级扩展算法，在无需先验流量知识的情况下，通过在线估计请求到达强度来适应时变到达率，克服了传统LLM调度算法假设泊松到达的局限性，并在低到达率漂移场景下相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量且延迟相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM调度算法（如WAIT）通常假设请求到达服从恒定速率的泊松过程，但真实世界流量具有突发性和动态变化特性，这一假设难以反映实际场景。因此需要设计一种能自适应时变到达率且无需先验流量知识的高效调度算法。

Method: 在SOTA的WAIT算法基础上提出轻量级扩展：利用观测到的请求间隔时间进行在线请求强度估计，从而动态适应时变到达率。采用基于Markov Modulated Poisson Process（MMPP）的合成工作负载进行仿真评估。

Result: 在评估的低到达率漂移场景中，所提方法相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量，同时保持了与这些方法相当的延迟水平。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了通过在线估计请求强度来适配真实动态流量模式的有效性，为LLM推理调度在非平稳到达流量下的性能提升提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [74] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillTFM是一种无需训练的表格基础模型（TFM）自适应系统，通过将TFM的参数更新转变为代理技能的门控演化，利用可验证和可扩展的技能库来应对分布偏移、异构特征语义和任务特定模式等问题。在模拟边界环境和真实电力价格预测中，AUC提升了0.128-0.142，非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在科学、工业、金融、医疗和公共服务等领域广泛存在且至关重要。表格基础模型（TFMs）虽为通用表格学习提供了有前景的范式，但其实际部署受限于分布偏移、异构特征语义以及难以在无昂贵微调或额外标注数据情况下捕获的任务特定模式。

Method: SkillTFM的核心是一个可验证且可扩展的技能库，它结合了两部分机制：(1) 边界证据识别，用于刻画任务结构和基础模型的失败模式；(2) 门控技能演化，在显式验证的前提下检索和扩展可复用技能。整个系统将TFM自适应从参数更新转变为代理技能的门控演化过程。

Result: 在模拟边界设置和真实世界电力价格预测中，SkillTFM将AUC提升了0.128-0.142，将非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。跨不同TFM骨干网络的实验进一步验证了SkillTFM的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SkillTFM通过在无需训练的情况下用技能演化的方式替代参数更新，有效解决了TFM在分布偏移和复杂任务模式下的自适应难题，并在多个设置和骨干网络上展现了显著的性能提升与良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [75] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对二值神经网络的后训练早期停止机制，利用累加过程中部分和的符号可预测性，提前终止不必要的累加运算，无需重新训练模型参数。在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深卷积层去除了86.6%的累加项（精度仅下降0.37点），三个最深卷积层同时使用时减少全网络25%的算术运算（精度下降1.36点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络虽然将点积转化为带符号的加法/减法，但运算数量并未减少——每个神经元仍需累加全部输入，而许多累加运算对最终输出符号没有贡献。随着累加进行，部分和常常远离零点，最终符号在很早之前就已可预测，后续贡献仅改变和值而不改变输出激活，造成计算浪费。

Method: 提出一种后训练早期停止机制：在训练数据集上刻画累加过程中运行部分和的行为特征，利用该统计信息尽可能早地预测最终符号，从而提前终止累加。该方法不重新训练任何模型参数，并在理想化权重排序下统计节省的操作数。

Result: 在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深卷积层去除了86.6%的累加项，精度仅下降0.37点；同时应用于三个最深卷积层时，减少全网络25%的算术运算，精度下降1.36点。

Conclusion: 通过后训练统计分析实现对二值神经网络累加过程的早期终止是有效的，能够在不重新训练模型的情况下大幅减少计算量，同时保持较低的精度损失。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [76] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment) 是一种解析器引导的偏好优化框架，用依赖解析器监督替代人工偏好标注，通过delta-DPO、复合奖励和奖励黑客监控，在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威博克马尔语等低资源语言上显著提升语法质量，无需人类偏好标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化方法有效但依赖昂贵的人工标注，而形态丰富、低资源语言的偏好标注尤为稀缺，亟需无需人工标注的替代方案来提升生成质量。

Method: SAGA框架包含四个核心组件：1) 将解析器判断转换为delta-DPO的偏好对；2) 结合解析器质量与词汇多样性的复合奖励；3) 基于奖励差距准则过滤低信息量对；4) 监控奖励黑客以维护可靠的监督信号。

Result: 在GPT-SW3-1.3B上，丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升至93.8%；冰岛语在独立Stanza评估上提升+4.5个百分点（三均值+3.3），且母语者在80%成对比较中偏好SAGA输出；挪威博克马尔语提升+28个百分点。

Conclusion: 在高品质依赖解析器可用的低资源语言中，解析器派生的监督信号是人工偏好标注的实际可行替代方案，可用于语法对齐。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [77] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"何时、如何、何地"三个维度系统审视了持续学习（CL）从参数中心式学习向系统级适应的范式转变，涵盖off-policy/on-policy及超越梯度的优化机制（How）、预训练/后训练/推理时阶段（When）、以及内部参数与外部结构约束（Where）三个轴，并综述了代表性方法、当前转型趋势、关键挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习主要聚焦于参数中心的机制（如训练策略、架构设计、权重适应），但新兴范式正在重塑CL的边界：on-policy学习拓宽了更新机制、测试时训练将CL从训练阶段延伸到推理阶段、外部harness组件（记忆、技能库、交互协议）将模型能力的进化边界拓展到静态参数空间之外。作者认为这些发展标志着从参数中心学习向系统级适应的转变，需要一个统一框架来刻画和系统梳理这一转型。

Method: 作者提出一个三轴分析框架：How维度涵盖off-policy、on-policy和超越梯度的优化机制；When维度捕捉预训练、后训练和推理时三个阶段的演变；Where维度划分内部参数更新与外部结构约束两类。基于这一框架，作者系统综述了各种代表性方法，并追踪持续学习从参数中心到系统级适应的转型轨迹。

Result: 通过三轴框架系统梳理了持续学习领域的代表性方法，揭示了持续学习从传统的参数中心式模型适应向更广泛的系统级适应演进的总体趋势，并识别了这一范式转变带来的关键挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 持续学习正经历从参数中心式学习向系统级适应的根本性范式转变。通过When、How、Where三轴框架，该文为理解这一转型提供了系统化的视角，同时指出新范式带来的挑战与机遇，为未来持续学习研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [78] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3是一个轻量级概率全球天气预报模型（约1.34亿参数），通过显式多时间步推理解决了预测时间步长选择的权衡问题：单一权重集可在推理时配置时间步长，同时混合时间步长训练提升了滚动稳定性，实现了接近SOTA的中期概率预报能力与稳定的延伸期滚动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖固定且预定的自回归时间步长，面临根本性权衡：短时间步长（1-6小时）能够精细解析日内周期内的大气动力学，但会增加误差累积；长时间步长（24小时）减少误差累积，但限制了亚日可预测性高的短程预报的可用性。作者旨在通过显式多时间步推断来解决这一矛盾。

Method: GEM-3采用邻域注意力Transformer架构，基于等矩形网格，参数约1.34亿。核心创新在于：(1) 显式多时间步推断，使用单组训练权重即可在推理时配置模型时间步长；(2) 混合时间步长训练，相比时间步长专门化模型显著提升滚动稳定性；(3) 在其前身GEM-2基础上引入多项架构改进。

Result: GEM-3实现了接近SOTA的中期概率预报技能，具备稳定的延伸期滚动能力，同时保持高效的训练和推理过程，并能提供决策相关的诊断信息。

Conclusion: GEM-3提供了一个实用的天气预报系统，有效耦合了接近SOTA的中期概率预报技能、稳定的延伸期滚动、高效的训练与推理，以及决策相关的诊断能力，在预报时间步长的可预测性与可用性之间实现了动态平衡。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [79] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇综述全面梳理了后训练适应（post-training adaptation）领域的文献，提出了一个六维分类法（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），区分了微调、检索增强、提示等常被混淆的概念，并展示了适应策略从传统机器学习到深度学习和多模态大语言模型的演化过程。文章还映射了技术间的关系（继承、取代、混合、分层部署堆栈），并为技术文档、模型变更追踪和治理分析提供了统一的词汇表，最后指出了评估、可复现性、推理时适应、去学习、多模态适应和治理感知工作流等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练适应技术在现代机器学习实践中日益重要（包括微调、检索增强、模型编辑、去学习、校准等），但相关文献分散在不同技术族、模型类别和部署场景中，缺乏统一的框架来比较不同方法或描述训练后模型的修改过程，导致术语混乱和评估困难。

Method: 通过系统性地综合后训练适应文献，提出一个六维分类法（mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, model type），区分易混淆的术语（如微调、检索增强和提示），梳理适应策略从传统ML到深度学习和多模态大模型的历史演化，并映射技术间的关系（继承、取代、混合、分层部署）。

Result: 提出了一个统一的六维taxonomy和共享词汇表，能够支持技术文档编写、模型变更追踪和治理分析；揭示了技术间的关系图谱，包括继承、取代、混合和分层部署堆栈；识别了评估、可复现性、持久化推理时适应、去学习、多模态适应和治理感知工作流等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 后训练适应领域需要一个统一的分类框架来整合分散的文献，本文提出的六维分类法和关系映射为实现这一目标奠定了基础，同时也指出了该领域未来需要解决的多个关键开放问题。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [80] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了MetaboLLM（代谢组学专用大语言模型）和MetaboLLM-GIN（将生成的生化描述转化为代谢物图用于患者级预测的图同构网络），在代谢组学知识与预测任务上显著优于基线和医学适配模型，展示了领域专用语言模型组织异质生化知识为可预测、可解释代谢物图表示的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为预测性表示。现有通用或医学适配的语言模型缺乏代谢组学领域的专门知识组织能力，无法有效支持患者层面的代谢物预测任务。

Method: MetaboLLM通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索构建；MetaboLLM-GIN将MetaboLLM生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图，并利用图同构网络（GIN）进行患者级预测。模型评估覆盖四种骨干模型家族，并在外部公共基准上进行迁移验证。

Result: MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上优于对应基线和医学适配模型，并可迁移到外部基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉旁路移植术后应激性高血糖预测中取得最高AUC（0.8616），在绝经后激素治疗方案分类中取得最高AUC（0.8123），优于传统模型、替代图构建方法及未适配/非检索配置生成的图。模型解释还产生生物学上有意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域专用语言模型能够将异质生化知识组织为可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，为代谢组学驱动的临床预测和生物学发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [81] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RxnCLF，一种基于缩合反应图（CRG）的自监督对比学习框架，用于反应表示学习。在170万Pistachio反应上预训练后，该模型在多个产率预测基准上超越图与序列基线，R2指标达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标注数据稀缺、反应空间组合爆炸且稀疏的问题，现有基于字符串、指纹和图的不同反应编码仅部分捕捉化学转化信息，导致复杂底物反应预测不准确，亟需更具泛化能力的反应表示学习方法。

Method: RxnCLF采用自监督对比学习框架，构建缩合反应图（CRG）将反应物和产物信息统一到单一图中，使模型学习显式且富集的转化结构。模型在Pistachio约170万反应上预训练，学习紧凑且连续的表征空间，同时捕捉反应中心特征与侧链上下文信息。

Result: 在Buchwald-Hartwig偶联、Pd催化的BH偶联以及专有的HTE C-N偶联和酰胺化数据集等多个产率预测基准上，RxnCLF一致超越图与序列基线，R2指标得到提升并取得整体最优性能。

Conclusion: RxnCLF作为基于CRG的可扩展反应基础模型具有较大潜力，有望在更大反应空间内实现泛化，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等多种下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [82] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应查询与非自适应查询之间的差距。在有限结果空间上，证明了可区分性当且仅当两个分布类的成对条件概率存在正分离；当分离为零时，任何有限查询预算下的最坏情况误差恰好为1/2。对于T-查询自适应策略，构造了使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对查询的随机非自适应过程，其模拟转录与自适应转录的全变差距离在ρ以内。最终证明最坏情况固定误差自适性差距为Θ_ε(N²)，表明交互可将所需测试数量减少二次方因子，但不会产生指数级查询优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估中一个核心问题是：评估者是否可以基于早期响应选择后续测试，还是必须预先固定所有测试。理解自适应与非自适应查询之间在能力上的根本差距，对评估协议的样本效率和理论极限具有基础性意义。

Method: 在条件查询模型下，对有限结果空间上定义分布类别对的可区分性问题进行分析。首先建立可学习性与成对条件概率正分离之间的等价关系；其次构造随机化非自适应过程，通过O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对查询在总变差距离ρ内模拟任意T查询自适应策略的转录；最后构造匹配的分布族，证明自适应查询复杂度为常数而非自适应复杂度为Ω_ε(N²)。

Result: 可区分性当且仅当分布类间的成对条件概率分离为正；零分离时最坏情况误差恰为1/2。非自适应模拟自适应查询的代价为O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对查询，且存在匹配的下界Ω_ε(N²)，最终固定误差自适性差距为Θ_ε(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互式评估可将所需测试数量减少二次方因子（Θ(N²)），但不会像一般自适应查询理论中常见的那样产生指数级优势。这揭示了在模型评估场景中，自适应交互的收益是多项式而非指数的。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [83] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BaKron是一个高效的神经网络量化求解器，用于两测Kronecker分解Hessian近似的自适应舍入。通过结合反对角线并行和递归分治构造，将总计算量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，与GPTQ的三次方复杂度匹配，同时利用了更丰富的曲率信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ风格的自适应舍入通常只利用来自输入激活的单侧信息，而两测Kronecker分解Hessian近似可以捕获输出坐标间的相关性，但直接在向量化权重域中应用GPTQ计算成本过高。因此需要一个高效的求解器来利用两测曲率信息。

Method: BaKron建立在BoA和YAQA使用的两测自适应舍入公式之上，结合反对角线并行与递归分治构造。对于m×n权重矩阵，使用O(m+n)顺序步骤，将总工作量从O(m²n²)降低到O(mn(m+n))，匹配GPTQ的三次方扩展。该方法是模块化的，可与不同的基础量化器和Hessian估计器组合。

Result: 提供了实用基准测试，考虑了BaKron可搭配的各种Hessian，找到了高效计算这些Hessian的技术，并进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: BaKron实现了与GPTQ同等的计算复杂度，同时利用了两测Kronecker分解的Hessian近似中更丰富的曲率信息，在效率和精度之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [84] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出U-OPSD方法，利用模型自身生成和内部一致性实现无监督在线自我蒸馏，不依赖任何外部监督信号（如真实标签、环境反馈或更大模型的指导），在多个数学推理基准上超越或匹敌依赖真实标签的有监督方法（OPSD、GRPO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy self-distillation方法仍依赖外部监督（ground-truth标签、环境反馈或大模型指导），并非真正意义上的"自我"蒸馏，限制了其在无外部信号场景下的适用性。

Method: U-OPSD首先对模型生成进行多次采样，通过多数投票并设置自一致性阈值构建伪解决方案；然后基于最短伪方案构建教师分布，蒸馏到模型的最长错误补全前缀上，使模型在确定性错误的位置进行自我纠正。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500和AMC23基准上，U-OPSD在Qwen3非思考模式4B和8B规模下分别比基模型提升8.5%和10.7%，平均超过OPSD 3.2%和2.3%；在思考模式下与OPSD持平（4B超0.9%，8B持平），并分别超过GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD证明仅依靠模型自身生成的内部一致性即可实现有效的在线自我蒸馏，在无任何外部监督的情况下达到甚至超越有监督方法的性能，是真正的"无监督自我蒸馏"。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [85] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC)方法，解决生成式奖励模型在强化学习中因比较性评分与标量奖励范式不匹配而无法发挥潜力的问题。RRC通过自竞争排序和锚定引导排序两种策略，从相对偏好排序中构建有效的RL学习信号，在开放对话和推理基准上显著提升生成式奖励模型的RL训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型在响应排序方面表现优异，但由于其本质是比较性的（输出偏好排序），而现有RL算法采用标量评分范式，二者之间存在不匹配，导致生成式奖励模型在RL中未能充分发挥潜力。

Method: RRC提出了两种互补的策略：(1) 自竞争排序（self-competitive ranking），利用采样响应之间的相互比较来构建奖励；(2) 锚定引导排序（anchor-guided ranking），通过少量参考响应实现可扩展的基于排序的奖励构建。

Result: 在开放对话和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC显著改善了使用生成式奖励模型的RL训练效果，相比现有奖励构建方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RRC有效弥合了生成式奖励模型的比较性本质与RL标量奖励范式之间的鸿沟，为生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的应用提供了更有效的奖励信号构建方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [86] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一个自主终端任务合成系统，通过对抗性求解器校准来根据已验证的求解器行为修订候选任务，构建了5,431个校准终端任务，在多个基准上显著提升了模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端代理需要可执行且可验证的任务，但现有的可执行性验证仅确认任务可行性，无法揭示任务相对于给定求解器设置的难度是否合适。作者希望构建一个具备"求解器相对可学习区"的任务集合，即任务不仅要可行，还要对目标求解器具有恰当的挑战性。

Method: CalibForge通过两类校准策略来自主修订候选任务：(1) 多求解器校准，针对异构求解器池内的分歧；(2) 对比求解器校准，针对指定的强通/弱败关系。两种策略都将可证明的可解性作为锚点，建立一个求解器相对的"可学习区"。系统据此生成5,431个校准终端任务。

Result: 消融实验表明两种校准策略比单纯作者编写加验证或普通单求解器反馈提供更有效的监督。在完整集合上训练的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%，相比对应基座模型的最大提升在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达24.71个百分点，SWE-bench Pro上达27.68个百分点，Doc2Repo上达30.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对可学习性作为构建有效且可迁移的代理训练数据的实用目标取得了显著成效，验证了使用求解器行为校准任务合成的有效性。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [87] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为有限VC维二元分类问题构造了一个达到统计最优风险界的学习器，其风险上界在常数因子内匹配已知下界，证毕了agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度问题（在固定最小风险L*下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决agnostic PAC学习中样本复杂度的精确刻画：此前下界已知（Devroye等），但缺少在常数因子内匹配下界的最优上界构造。本文的目标是构造一个学习器，使其风险界在固定L*下与已知下界匹配，从而完成该问题的最优性证明。

Method: 构造了一个针对有限VC维假设类H⊆{-1,+1}^X的学习器。关键创新在于设计一种学习算法，使得其泛化误差能够以L*√((d+log(1/δ))/n)+(d+log(1/δ))/n的形式进行刻画，其中常数项为7×10^8，从而与Devroye-Györfi-Lugosi的下界（常数因子内）精确匹配。

Result: 所构造的学习器以至少1-δ概率达到风险上界 L(ĥ)≤L*+7×10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n)+(d+log(1/δ))/n)，该界在固定L*下与现有下界匹配，证毕agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文在常数因子内完全解决了有限VC维下agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度问题，填补了最优上界与下界之间的空白，说明该框架下的学习风险界已达到统计最优。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>

### [88] [The prediction of extreme uncertainty-production events in three-dimensional Navier-Stokes turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05208)
*Jin Ge,Joran Rolland,John Christos Vassilicos*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究三维Navier-Stokes湍流中不确定性能量的指数增长，重点关注不确定性生产和放大的间歇性与高度局域化特征。通过在Q-R平面上分析应变率、涡度和涡变形等关键场量的动力学，并结合不同雷诺数下直接数值模拟（DNS）的全程时空域提交函数估计，证实了应变率在驱动不确定性中的核心作用，并发现当应变率与涡度过接近其空间均值时，仅凭所考虑场量难以实现稳定的概率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解湍流系统的可预测性是流体力学中的核心挑战。不确定性在湍流中的增长往往呈现间歇性和局域化特征，传统平均意义上的分析无法捕捉极端事件的发生机制。因此需要从Navier-Stokes方程中识别驱动不确定性增长的关键场量，并量化罕见极端事件的发生概率，以提升湍流可预测性研究的深度。

Method: 1) 从Navier-Stokes方程推导并识别影响不确定性生产项P_Δ增长/衰减的关键场量（应变率、涡度、涡变形）；2) 在速度梯度张量第二、第三不变量构成的Q-R平面上考察这些场量的动力学行为；3) 在不同雷诺数下的周期域DNS中，估计整个时空域上的提交函数（committor function）；4) 以不确定性能量、应变率、涡度和涡变形为条件，估计局部不确定性生产项罕见的极端事件的概率。

Result: 估计结果表明，应变率在驱动不确定性增长中起主导作用。当应变率和涡度偏离其空间平均值较远时，能够以较高概率预测极端不确定性事件；但当二者接近空间平均值时，仅基于所考虑的场量无法实现稳定的概率预报。

Conclusion: 研究确认了应变率是驱动湍流不确定性的关键物理量，同时揭示了流场处于"平均状态"附近时可预测性的根本局限——此时局域不确定性生产事件的概率预报不可靠，需要引入更高阶或更丰富的场量信息来改善预测能力。

Abstract: We investigate the exponential growth of uncertainty energy in 3D Navier-Stokes turbulence, emphasising the intermittent and highly localized amplification/production of uncertainty, a critical factor in understanding the predictability of turbulent systems. From the Navier-Stokes equations one can identify some key fields contributing to the growth/decay of uncertainty-production term $P_Δ$: strain rate, vorticity, and vortex deformation. The dynamics of these fields are examined in the $Q-R$ plane, where $Q$ and $R$ are the second and third invariants of the velocity gradient tensor, to understand their role in the evolution of uncertainty-production term $P_Δ$. We proceed by estimating committor functions across the entire spatiotemporal domain of direct numerical simulations (DNS) of turbulence in a periodic domain at different Reynolds numbers. Our estimates of the probability of rare extreme events of local uncertainty-production term as a function of uncertainty energy, strain rate, vorticity, and vortex deformation confirm the role of strain rate in driving uncertainty. Where strain rate and vorticity are too close to their space-average values, stable probabilistic forecasts appear impossible solely on the basis of the fields considered here.

Abstract: Viscoelastic filament thinning is classically described by elastocapillary dynamics in extensional flows, yet in confined microchannels the combined effects of wall-induced shear, elasticity, and capillarity remain poorly understood. Here, we experimentally investigate the breakup of a shear-thinning viscoelastic liquid coflowing with an immiscible Newtonian fluid in a rectangular microchannel, focusing on the formation, stretching, instability, and breakup of the thin filament connecting the primary droplet to the upstream liquid. Four regimes: stable coflow, squeezing, dripping, and jetting are identified and mapped using the capillary numbers of the dispersed and continuous phases and an elastocapillary parameter. Although elasticity weakly affects the onset of primary droplet formation, it strongly alters later filament dynamics by delaying capillary breakup and stabilising long-lived filaments. Scaling analyses based on capillary, viscous, and elastic force balances predict the primary droplet size, critical filament thickness at instability onset, maximum filament length, and critical jet length. Particle tracking shows that confinement creates a non-uniform wall-induced shear field along the inclined filament, producing spatial variations in interfacial velocity and initiating the first bead-on-a-string instability at the location of maximum shear. A Rayleigh-Plateau analysis incorporating an effective viscosity derived from the Oldroyd-B model predicts the instability wavelength and growth rate to the correct order of magnitude. These results show that confined viscoelastic breakup is governed not solely by classical elastocapillary thinning, but by a coupled wall-shear-elasticity mechanism controlling filament stretching, instability, and secondary droplet formation, thereby providing a predictive framework for filament-mediated breakup in confined viscoelastic multiphase flows.

Method: 作者在4种流量和4种河道坡度共16组条件下开展实验室实验，利用非侵入式、可顺流测量的Stream Tomography技术连续测绘河床地形。采用Landau方程描述沙坝高度的S形增长曲线，以此稳健估算增长率和平衡高度。同时基于实测河床地形进行数值流动模拟，分析不同流量条件下水流偏转、泥沙输移与局部冲刷的耦合机制。

Result: 沙坝高度呈现S形增长且可由Landau方程良好描述；无量纲增长率随流量减小而增大，但其敏感性弱于单一交替沙坝模态的稳定性理论预测，且在低流量下偏差更显著，反映出线性化的局限性和多模态相互作用的影响。数值模拟表明，低流量条件下沙坝高度-水深比更大，水流更容易偏转，导致沉积区下游泥沙输移受限、沙坝迁移受限，水流和泥沙输移集中于冲刷区，加剧局部冲刷并促进沙坝高度增长。

Conclusion: 该研究为沙坝稳定性理论提供了基准约束条件，有助于改进沙坝高度对流量变化响应的预测能力。

Abstract: Alternate bars are ubiquitous bedforms in alluvial rivers, and excessive bar-height growth can increase flood risk and disrupt ecosystems. Predicting bar-height variability requires quantifying the dependence of bar growth rate on water discharge, yet this has rarely been achieved because conventional flume measurements interrupt the flow and cannot continuously capture bar evolution. Here we conducted laboratory experiments under four discharges and four channel slopes (16 conditions) and continuously mapped bed topography using Stream Tomography, a nonintrusive, flow-through measurement technique. Bar height exhibits sigmoidal growth and is well described by the Landau equation, allowing robust estimation of the growth rate and equilibrium height. The inferred dimensionless growth rate tends to increase as discharge decreases, but its sensitivity is weaker than that predicted by stability theory for a single alternate-bar mode. This discrepancy becomes more pronounced at lower discharge, indicating limitations of linearization and the influence of interactions among multiple bar modes. Furthermore, numerical flow simulations over the measured bed topography reveal that, during bar development, lower-discharge conditions produce a relatively larger bar height-to-depth ratio, making the flow more prone to deflection. In such cases, sediment transport is restricted downstream of depositional areas, thereby limiting bar migration. Consequently, flow and sediment transport become concentrated in the scour zones, enhancing local scour and promoting the growth of bar height. These results provide benchmark constraints for bar stability theories and help improve predictions of how bar height responds to changes in discharge.

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维均匀各向同性湍流在绝对平衡态（大尺度）下，速度环量满足严格的面积极则或周界规则，并引入特征平衡尺度l_eq来划分两种统计规律适用的区域，为二维环量方程提供了新的稳态解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 速度环量的面积极则传统上与湍流惯性区相关联但并非精确成立，作者希望在二维湍流的绝对平衡态中寻找严格满足的环量统计规律，并探索新的几何不变量。

Method: 通过涡度等分（enstrophy equipartition）和能量等分（energy equipartition）分别对应大尺度绝对平衡态，从二维无粘环量方程推导出周界规则，并分析中间状态由特征尺度l_eq划分的不同统计行为。

Result: 在涡度等分的绝对平衡态下，面积极则严格成立；在能量等分的绝对平衡态下，周界规则严格成立。中间状态下，当l远小于l_eq时面积极则主导，l远大于l_eq时周界规则主导。该规律对极端长宽比（低至0.03）的环路依然成立。

Conclusion: 这些发现为二维环量方程提供了新的稳态解，并暗示存在更多未发现的解，为探索湍流的几何不变量开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the area rule of velocity circulation -- traditionally associated with the turbulent inertial range but shown not to be exact -- is strictly satisfied in the large-scale absolute equilibrium of two-dimensional (2D) homogeneous isotropic turbulence under enstrophy equipartition. We also derive a novel perimeter rule from the 2D inviscid loop equation, which posits that the probability distribution function (PDF) of velocity circulation depends solely on the loop perimeter rather than its area. This perimeter rule holds strictly in the large-scale absolute equilibrium characterized by energy equipartition. At the intermediate states determined by both enstrophy and energy, these two regimes are separated by a characteristic equilibrium scale $l_\text{eq}$: the area rule governs loop statistics when $l\ll l_\text{eq}$, while the perimeter rule emerges for $l\gg l_\text{eq}$. These statistical laws remain robust even for loops with extreme aspect ratios as low as $0.03$, a value that inertial-range studies never achieved. Our findings provide a new steady-state solution to the 2D loop equation and suggest additional solutions, paving the way for exploring previously undiscovered geometric invariants of turbulence.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三相EHD数值研究多依赖现象学模型，缺乏热力学一致性和对表面电荷对流等关键机制的准确描述，亟需一个严格热力学一致的理论与数值框架。

Method: 从Onsager变分原理严格推导三相EHD模型，内在保证热力学一致性；开发介观格子玻尔兹曼方法求解该模型，自然捕捉界面演化和电荷输运。

Result: 通过电渗流、三相液透镜铺展、静态复合液滴平衡及均匀电场下复合液滴变形等多个基准案例验证了数值框架的准确性，并成功模拟了电场下双液滴聚并与分离及EHD与剪切流耦合下的液滴行为。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个鲁棒且热力学可靠的三相EHD数值工具，可用于探索多相EHD系统的高度非线性行为。

Abstract: Multiphase electrohydrodynamic (EHD) flows play a crucial role in various engineering applications. However, existing numerical studies on three-phase electrohydrodynamic systems predominantly rely on phenomenological models, often neglecting thermodynamic consistency and critical surface charge convection mechanisms. To address these fundamental gaps, this paper proposes a thermodynamically consistent three-phase EHD model derived strictly from the Onsager variational principle. This theoretical framework intrinsically guarantees thermodynamic consistency and accurately captures complex multiphysics interactions without requiring a priori assumptions. Furthermore, a mesoscopic lattice Boltzmann method is developed to solve the proposed model, enabling the natural capture of interfacial evolution and charge transport. The accuracy of the numerical framework are rigorously validated against several benchmark cases, including electroosmotic flow in microchannels, the spreading of a three-phase liquid lens, the equilibrium of static compound droplet, and the deformation of compound droplet under uniform electric field. Using this validated framework, we investigate EHD applications, specifically simulating the complex dynamics of double droplet coalescence and separation under electric field, as well as the behavior of droplets subjected to combined EHD and shear flow. Overall, this work provides a robust, thermodynamically reliable numerical tool for exploring the highly nonlinear behaviors of multiphase EHD systems.

</details>

*Rossen Ivanov,Calin-Iulian Martin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究三层分层不可压无粘流体中二维非线性水波运动方程，建立了基于Dirichlet-Neumann算子的Hamiltonian变分框架，导出了线性色散关系的双三次方程并给出解的上下界与近似公式，同时分析了刚性盖子（平坦表面）情形下的弱非线性Boussinesq近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三层分层流体的非线性运动方程在湖泊、海洋内波以及实验室三层流体实验中具有重要应用。然而，这类系统的Hamiltonian结构尚未被系统建立，线性与弱非线性动力学行为的完整刻画也缺乏统一框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为该类问题提供严格的理论基础。

Method: (1) 利用Dirichlet-Neumann (DN)算子对三层流体（底部平坦、自由表面、两个界面）逐层建立Hamiltonian公式化；(2) 线性化得到小振幅扰动方程，推导出色散关系的双三次方程；(3) 分析双三次方程六个实数解（三个正、三个负）的传播速度及其上下界，并给出近似解析公式；(4) 对平坦表面（刚性盖子）模型单独用DN算子展开，推导线性与弱非线性Boussinesq方程；(5) 验证极限情况下退化为两层自由表面模型。

Result: 成功建立了三层分层流体非线性运动方程的Hamiltonian结构，色散关系由一双三次方程给出，六种传播速度（三个右传波、三个左传波）的模具有不同的数量级，获得了各传播速度的上下界估计及近似公式。刚性盖子模型下同样得到了DN算子表达的非线性演化方程、线性方程及Boussinesq近似方程，结果可退化到两层自由表面模型。

Conclusion: 本文建立的三层分层流体Hamiltonian框架与DN算子方法为该类系统提供了统一的理论工具，线性色散关系的完整分析与近似公式为实际应用（湖泊/海洋内波、实验室三层流体实验）提供了可操作的定量预测手段。

Abstract: The object of investigation in this paper are the nonlinear equations of motion for two-dimensional inviscid water flows with piecewise constant density stratification in a three-layer fluid with a flat bottom, a free surface and two interfaces. We establish a Hamiltonian formulation for the nonlinear governing equations in this setup. The Hamiltonian of the system and the equations of motion of the surface and of the interfaces are expressed with the help of the Dirichlet-Neumann (DN) operators, which are introduced for each of the layers. Then, the linear equations for small amplitudes of the elevation of the surface and of the interfaces in the leading order are derived from which a bi-cubic equation for the dispersion relation is obtained, whose solutions are analysed. The six real solutions for the possible propagation speeds (three positive, related to right-moving waves and three negative, related to left-moving waves) have magnitudes of different order. Upper and lower bounds for the previously mentioned roots are also given in terms of the coefficients of the equation. Subsequently, approximate formulae for the propagation speeds are derived. The importance of the DN operators is further illustrated in a separate analysis of the three-layer model with flat surface (rigid lid). The full nonlinear evolution equations are expressed again in terms of the DN operators, and the equations in the linear regime and the weakly nonlinear propagation regime (the Boussinesq approximation) are derived by a proper expansion of the DN operators. Limits to the two-layer free surface model are obtained as well. The obtained results are applicable to internal waves in lakes and in the ocean as well as to laboratory experiments with three superimposed fluid layers.

Result: 在穿透阈值以下无电荷产生；低Weber数下回缩射流产生高单位质量电荷，中等Weber数下回缩射流和冲击诱导射流共同贡献电荷，高Weber数下煎饼弹跳抑制回缩射流、电荷由冲击诱导穿透主导并趋于饱和；更小的网格孔径提升电荷-质量比，导电网格通过耗散残余表面电荷实现重复冲击下的稳定带电。

Abstract: Charge separation during the breakup of water drop has been recognized since the early studies of waterfall and spray electrification, yet the role of controlled drop fragmentation at structured liquid-repellent surfaces remains unclear. Here we show that water drops impacting superhydrophobic meshes generate charged secondary droplets as liquid penetrates and fragments through the mesh pores. By combining Faraday-cup charge measurements with high-speed imaging, we identify how the charge depends on the Weber number and on the pathway of spray formation. No measurable charge is detected below the penetration threshold. At low Weber numbers, recoil jet formation gives a high charge per unit spray mass, whereas at intermediate Weber numbers both recoil jets and impact-induced jets contribute to charging. At higher Weber numbers, pancake bouncing suppresses recoil jet formation, so charging is dominated by impact induced penetration and the total charge approaches a saturated value. We further show that smaller mesh pores enhance the charge-to-mass ratio and that conductive meshes provide stable charging under repeated impacts by dissipating residual surface charge. These findings provide design principles for superhydrophobic mesh platforms for spray charging and rain-driven energy harvesting.

</details>


### [95] [Magnetic Dynamo Driven by Inertial Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06086)
*A. Mishra,G. Mamatsashvili,M. Le Bars,A. J. Barker,F. Stefani*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速旋转行星和恒星内部中，大尺度地转涡旋通常伴随惯性波湍流并耗散波能，其强阻尼会抑制发电机效应。本文旨在研究在涡旋受控阻尼条件下，惯性波湍流本身能否驱动稳健的磁发电机作用。

Method: 通过数值模拟研究进动驱动的惯性波流动，引入对地转涡旋的受控阻尼项，在运动学阶段考察磁发电机增长率、临界磁Prandtl数、临界Poincaré数及其随Reynolds数的变化，并进行动能螺旋度相关性和磁能增长的谱分析。

Result: 结果表明，即使微小的涡旋阻尼也能显著提升惯性波发电机的增长率，使其能在Pm低至10⁻³、Po低至0.025时维持发电机作用；临界Po和Pm随Reynolds数增大而下降；发电机的起始和增长与动能螺旋度的相干涨落相关；磁能增长主要来自惯性波在宽尺度范围内的感应作用。

Conclusion: 惯性波被确立为快速旋转低磁Prandtl数流动中高效产生磁场放大的机制，对理解行星和恒星内部磁场的产生具有重要意义。

Abstract: We demonstrate, by studying precession-driven flows, that inertial wave hydrodynamic turbulence can drive a robust magnetic dynamo action. Motivated by the stronger damping of large-scale geostrophic vortices in rapidly rotating planetary and stellar interiors, we introduce a controlled damping of the vortices, which usually accompany inertial wave turbulence and feed on wave energy. It is shown that even a small vortex damping results in a significant increase of the growth rate of the dynamo due to inertial waves in the kinematic regime, allowing it to persist for magnetic Prandtl numbers as low as $Pm \sim 10^{-3}$ and Poincaré numbers $Po\sim 0.025$. These critical values of $Po$ and $Pm$ for the dynamo onset decrease with increasing Reynolds number. The onset and growth of the dynamo appear to correlate with the coherent fluctuations of kinetic helicity. Spectral analysis shows that magnetic energy growth is primarily due to inertial-wave-induced induction over a broad range of scales. These results establish inertial waves as an efficient mechanism for magnetic field amplification in rapidly rotating low-$Pm$ flows relevant to planetary and stellar interiors.

</details>


### [96] [Wave scattering around a submerged vertical permeable breakwater](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06089)
*Jeongin Kim,Yong Sung Park*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对无限水深条件下，淹没式可渗透垂直板式防波堤在线性单色波作用下的波浪速度场，通过摄动法和Riemann-Hilbert问题求解，获得了解析解，并给出了应用实例及能量恒等式验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可渗透防波堤在工程中具有消波和透水双重功能，但此前缺乏针对淹没式可渗透垂直板结构的精确解析解，需要建立理论模型来描述波浪与可渗透结构的相互作用。

Method: 采用二维势流理论，假设流体不可压缩、无粘、无旋。可渗透边界条件为非线性，通过以小参数表示渗透性的摄动法将其展开至一阶：零阶项代表不可渗透防波堤的散射波，一阶项给出可渗透修正。各级速度势通过约化方法求解，零阶问题化为齐次Riemann-Hilbert问题，一阶问题化为非齐次Riemann-Hilbert问题。

Result: 详细讨论了波长、防波堤长度和渗透性条件对反射系数和透射系数的影响；推导出精确的能量恒等式，用于验证一阶解并给出展开有效范围的闭式边界ε_max(kb)。

Conclusion: 该解析解可准确描述波浪被淹没式可渗透垂直板防波堤散射的物理过程，能量恒等式不仅验证了解的正确性，还明确了摄动展开的适用范围。

Abstract: An analytical solution for a wave velocity field scattered by a submerged permeable vertical plate-type breakwater under the linear monochromatic wave is obtained and the applications of the solution are presented. The water has an infinite depth, and the flow is assumed to be incompressible, inviscid, and irrotational, which leads to the two-dimensional potential wave theory. The permeable breakwater vertically occupies a finite interval beneath the water surface and the water flows through the breakwater. The resulting nonlinear boundary condition is resolved by the perturbation method with a small parameter representing the permeability. The solution was expanded up to the first order so that the leading-order term can represent the wave scattered by the impermeable breakwater and the first-order term can give the correction to the solution considering the wave scattered by the permeable breakwater. Each order of the wave velocity potential is determined by a reduction method and this leads to the homogeneous Riemann-Hilbert problem for the leading-order problem and the nonhomogeneous Riemann-Hilbert problem for the first-order problem. \rev{The effects} of wavelength, breakwater length, and breakwater permeability conditions on the reflection and transmission coefficients are discussed in detail as an illustrative example of the application of the solution. \rev{An exact energy identity is also derived; it verifies the first-order solution and yields a closed-form boundary $\varepsilon_{\max}(kb)$ of the validity range of the expansion.

</details>


### [97] [From expansion to collapse: Bubble and continuum multiscale modeling in open-system magmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06181)
*Janine Birnbaum,Fabian B. Wadsworth,Anthony Lamur,Jackie E. Kendrick,Yan Lavallee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多尺度耦合模型（MVFFIN），将微观气泡内挥发分扩散的力学模型与宏观热演化和岩浆悬浮体流体流动耦合，以统一框架解析岩浆中气泡膨胀与收缩、输运与碎裂的复杂反馈机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅酸盐熔体中气泡生长会引发显著的体积膨胀，对岩浆输运动力学具有一阶控制作用。当岩浆暴露于外部环境时，表面热量和挥发分流失会使气泡收缩，导致扩散、流变和流动之间产生复杂反馈。现有模型难以同时捕捉气泡尺度微观过程与大尺度岩浆流动之间的双向耦合，因此需要统一框架来解析多尺度脱气过程。

Method: 将微观力学模型（单个气泡内挥发分扩散）与宏观尺度热演化及周围岩浆悬浮体流体流动方程双向耦合。这种双向耦合同时追踪气泡尺寸、熔体粘度和压力梯度的协同演化，使气泡生长和再吸收均能从局部条件自然涌现。模型在灵活、模块化的数值框架（MVFFIN）中实现。

Result: 识别出由以下因素主导的不同动力学机制：（i）受粘性阻力或气泡尺度扩散限制的气泡生长，（ii）悬浮体的粘性输运，（iii）通过渗透性多孔网络和暴露的岩浆-流体界面的脱气，以及（iv）热淬火。发现由温度和挥发分浓度梯度形成的薄的高粘度边界层在调节流动和气泡演化中起核心作用。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个统一框架用于理解多尺度脱气及其对岩浆输运和碎裂的影响，可推广到管道流、火山碎屑演化等多种系统，通过解析内部气泡动力学与外部边界条件之间的相互作用实现。

Abstract: Bubble growth in silicate melts drives significant volume expansion, which has a first order control on magma transport dynamics. When magmas are exposed to external environments, heat and volatile loss at free surfaces can reverse bubble growth, leading to shrinkage and complex feedbacks between diffusion, rheology, and flow. To resolve how magma flow controls, or is controlled by, bubble expansion, we couple a micro-mechanical model for volatile diffusion into individual bubbles, with a macro-scale thermal evolution and fluid flow of the surrounding magmatic suspension. This two-way coupling captures the co-evolution of bubble size, melt viscosity, and pressure gradients, allowing both growth and resorption to emerge naturally from local conditions. We identify distinct dynamical regimes governed by (i) bubble growth limited by (a) viscous resistance or (b) diffusion at the bubble scale, (ii) viscous transport of the suspension, (iii) outgassing through permeable porous networks and exposed magma-fluid interfaces, and (iv) thermal quenching. Across these regimes, thin, high-viscosity boundary layers arising from temperature and volatile concentration gradients play a central role in modulating flow and bubble evolution. The model is implemented in a flexible, modular numerical framework (Multiscale Vesiculation, Fluid flow, Failure, and Interaction Nonlinear model: MVFFIN) enabling extension to a wide range of systems and applications, including conduit flow and pyroclast evolution. By resolving the interplay between internal bubble dynamics and external boundary conditions, this approach provides a unified framework for understanding multiscale degassing and its impact on magmatic transport and fragmentation.

</details>


### [98] [VENUSS: a unified finite-element model of solidifying lava](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06199)
*Janine Birnbaum,Einat Lev,Marc W. Spiegelma,Jackie E. Kendrick,Yan Lavallee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VENUSS (Viscous-Elastic Numerically Unified Solver for Solidifying flows) 这一新数值模型，用于模拟冷却和凝固的自由表面流动。该模型将粘性流体内部与随冷却增长的弹性外壳耦合，并演示了弹性外壳相比高粘性外壳会导致更显著的横向扩张和更少的垂向隆升，表明固体层中应力横向传递对准确预测穹顶变形的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔岩流和穹顶表面形成固体硬壳（外壳）会导致变形机制从粘性主导转变为弹性或塑性主导，这一转变显著影响流动的速率和方式。特别是在水下、冰下和地外环境中冷却加速，需要专门针对这些环境校准的模型。现有模型未能充分耦合粘性流体内部与弹性外壳的相互作用。

Method: 作者开发了一个新的数值模型VENUSS，该模型将粘性流体内部与弹性外壳耦合，弹性外壳的厚度随冷却过程增长。模型将弹性壳与基底地形相耦合，用于模拟从底部供料的穹顶状流动的变形行为。

Result: 演示结果表明，带有弹性外壳且耦合基底地形的穹顶状形状比具有可比的高粘性外壳的穹顶产生更多的横向扩张和更少的垂向隆升。这一差异说明了固体层中应力横向传递的重要性。

Conclusion: 在流动模型中纳入凝固外壳（弹性壳）是准确解释和预测穹顶变形的必要条件，因为固体层的横向应力传递对变形模式有显著影响。VENUSS模型适用于冷却加速环境（如水下、冰下和地外环境）中的熔岩流建模。

Abstract: The development of a solid rind or carapace at the surface of lava flows and domes results in a transition in deformation mechanism from dominantly viscous to elastic or plastic. This transition has a significant impact on the rate and style of emplacement, including on the construction of channelized flows, over-steepened margins, and flow advance due to lava breakouts. These processes are particularly important in subaqueous, subglacial, and extraterrestrial environments in which cooling is accelerated, requiring models specifically calibrated for these environments. We present a new numerical model, Viscous-Elastic Numerically Unified Solver for Solidifying flows (VENUSS), for cooling and solidifying free surface flows. The model couples a viscous fluid interior with an elastic shell whose thickness grows in response to cooling. As a demonstration of the impact of including a solidified crust in the flow model, we show that a dome-like shape fed from below with an elastic shell coupled to the basal topography results in more lateral expansion and less vertical uplift than a comparable highly-viscous rind, demonstrating the need for lateral transfer of stress in solid layers to accurately interpret and predict dome deformation.

</details>
