# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Nucleation beyond Equilibrium: Fronts Control Invasion in Bistable Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05251)
*Victor Lequin,Giulio Biroli,Camille Scalliet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了非保守向量序参量反应-扩散系统的成核理论，将经典成核理论（CNT）推广到生态动力学等不满足细致平衡条件的非平衡系统，并证明CNT的数学结构在非平衡状态下依然成立，只需将能量量替换为前沿的动力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典成核理论依赖于细致平衡（微观可逆性）条件，在平衡态下有效。然而生态动力学通常违反细致平衡，且其序参量是多个耦合的非保守丰度组成的向量，而非单一标量场，因此缺乏通用的成核理论来描述空间扩展系统中一个状态对另一个状态的入侵。

Method: 针对具有非保守向量序参量的反应-扩散系统推导新的成核理论，同时适用于接近双节线（binodal，通过传播前沿入侵）和接近旋节线（spinodal，亚稳态失稳）两种情形。通过大量数值计算拟势（quasipotential）来验证理论，并应用于两种群的Lotka-Volterra模型。

Result: 理论在两种机制下均得到数值验证。理论证明CNT的数学结构在非平衡下仍然成立：前沿速度扮演体相自由能差的角色，扩散率扮演表面张力的角色。在Lotka-Volterra模型中，强种间竞争会在前沿内产生显著的丰度耗竭区，总丰度远低于承载能力，且这种向量结构使入侵难度随竞争增强而指数增加。

Conclusion: 该理论为生态学中的双稳态系统提供了可检验的预测框架：竞争强度增加（在固定竞争优势下）会使入侵呈指数性变难，该预测可在微生物系统中进行实验验证。同时表明只基于物种频率的标量描述无法捕捉这些动力学效应。

Abstract: Bistability, the existence of two alternative stable states with distinct basins of attraction, is common across ecology and many other biological, chemical, and physical systems. In spatially extended systems, the invasion of one state by the other proceeds through nucleation, the fluctuation-driven growth of a droplet beyond a critical size. Classical Nucleation Theory (CNT) quantifies this process using the energy landscape, but this description relies on detailed balance, the condition of microscopic reversibility that holds at equilibrium. Ecological dynamics generally violate detailed balance and are described not by a single scalar field but by several coupled, non-conserved abundances--a vector order parameter--for which no general nucleation theory exists. Here we derive such a theory for reaction-diffusion systems with a non-conserved vector order parameter, valid both close to the binodal, where invasion proceeds through propagating fronts, and close to the spinodal, where the metastable state loses stability. Extensive numerical computations of the quasipotential confirm the theory in both regimes. We show that the mathematical structure of CNT survives out of equilibrium, once energetic quantities are replaced by dynamical properties of fronts: the front speed plays the role of the bulk free-energy difference between phases, and the diffusivity that of the surface tension. Applied to the two-species Lotka-Volterra model, an archetypal system of bistable ecological antagonism, our theory shows that strong interspecific competition generates a pronounced depletion region within fronts, where the total abundance falls well below carrying capacity. This vectorial structure, invisible to a scalar description based on species frequency alone, makes invasion exponentially harder as competition strengthens at fixed competitive advantage--a prediction testable in microbial systems.

</details>


### [100] [Superfluid helium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05354)
*Charles Radin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个数学上可解的玩具模型，用于描述氦-4超流体的正常液相与超流相之间的相变，并展示了如何由此获得超流体的序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建模自然界中常见同位素氦-4的超流现象，并需要一个可精确求解的理论框架来理解超流相变的本质以及如何定义和提取序参量。

Method: 构建了一个数学上可解的玩具模型，模拟液氦-4从正常液相到超流相的相变过程，并基于该模型推导超流体的序参量。

Result: 该玩具模型可用于阐明相变机制，并展示如何从模型中提取超流体的序参量。

Conclusion: 通过这个可解的玩具模型，可以为超流体氦-4的相变提供理论洞见，并为定义序参量提供可行途径。

Abstract: We are interested in modelling superfluid helium-4, the common isotope of helium, of atomic number 4. We present a mathematically solvable toy model of the phase transition between the normal liquid and superfluid phases and use it to show how an order parameter might be obtained for the superfluid.

</details>


### [101] [Theory of Measurement-Altered Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05289)
*Kabir Khanna,Sara Murciano,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出弱测量下的Tomonaga-Luttinger液体理论，通过复制instantron计算研究Born平均观测量的测量诱导随机性，发现测量相关时密度和相位关联函数以含对数修正的普适幂律衰减，并刻画了关联函数矩的完整多重分形谱，揭示了后测量态系综中的强非高斯涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部测量能够改变无带隙量子物质中的长程关联，但此前对测量诱导的随机性如何影响临界量子态缺乏系统的理论框架。作者旨在建立弱测量下Tomonaga-Luttinger液体（一维量子临界态）的理论，以理解测量记录的内在随机性如何改变临界标度行为。

Method: 1) 提出弱测量的Tomonaga-Luttinger液体理论框架；2) 发展复制instantron（replica instanton）计算方法处理Born平均观测量，以应对测量记录的内在随机性；3) 结合矩阵乘积态（MPS）数值计算验证解析结果。

Result: 1) 当测量为相关（relevant）时，平均密度和相位关联函数在长距离下表现为含对数修正的普适幂律衰减，该特征被论证为测量诱导随机性所特有；2) 刻画了关联函数矩的完整多重分形谱，揭示后测量态系综中存在宽广且强非高斯的涨落；3) MPS数值计算支持解析结果。

Conclusion: 物理测量改变临界量子态的方式超出了受迫测量和常规临界标度的范畴，为1+1维共形场论描述的基态提供了测量改变临界性的一般图景。

Abstract: Local measurements can alter long-range correlations in gapless quantum matter. We propose a theory of weakly-monitored Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids, a broad class of quantum critical states in one dimension. In order to address the intrinsic randomness of the measurement record, we develop a replica instanton calculation to study Born-averaged observables. We find that when measurements are relevant, average correlators of density and phase fluctuations decay at long distances as universal power laws with logarithmic corrections, a feature we argue is peculiar to measurement-induced randomness. We characterize the full multifractal spectrum of moments of correlations functions, revealing broad, strongly non-gaussian fluctuations across the ensemble of post-measurement states. We support these analytic results with matrix-product-state calculations, and provide a general picture of measurement-altered criticality for ground states described by 1+1d conformal field theories. Our results establish that physical measurements alter critical quantum states in a manner that lies beyond both forced measurements and conventional critical scaling.

</details>


### [102] [Morphology of frozen labyrinths from irreversible threshold dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05496)
*Daniel Richard Levy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一种不可逆的多数阈动力学：每个代理通过高斯核交互，最多只能翻转一次（从局部加权少数派翻出）。该规则使二值场冻结在平衡的非共识迷宫图案中，而非可逆的MBO型阈动力学所驱动的共识或条纹。图案区域出现在初始自旋组成接近均等的窗口内，随交互范围增大而收窄，由标准化偏差控制。冻结形态是停滞的粗化：双连续、特征宽度次线性增长。通过干预实验证明粗观域布局由初始条件决定，更新顺序仅起到次要的首达竞争作用来微调壁位置。壁由挫败（agent冻结在自身局域场对面）标记，这在可逆弛豫中完全不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不可逆性如何改变多数阈动力学的结果，特别是探究从可逆的MBO型动力学（界面由平均曲率运动）转向一次性翻转规则后，系统从随机初始条件演化出的冻结图案及其形成机制。

Method: 在格点上采用高斯核加权交互，研究最多一次翻转的多数阈动力学。通过理论分析确定图案出现的参数窗口（基于标准化偏差），结合干预实验（固定初始条件而变化更新顺序）来区分确定性布局与随机细调的作用，并分析冻结壁上的挫败信号。

Result: 不可逆的翻转规则从随机初始条件冻结出平衡的非共识迷宫（labyrinth）图案，可逆弛豫则驱散这些图案。图案窗口围绕等量自旋组成，随交互范围增长而收窄；其起始由标准化偏差控制，尺度无关性跨越四倍范围。冻结形态为停滞的粗化，双连续且特征宽度次线性增长。更新顺序仅影响壁位置的次级首达竞争，粗观布局由初始条件确定性决定。挫败标记几乎纯界面性，不承载图案的大尺度形状。

Conclusion: 不可逆性是产生冻结迷宫图案的根本原因，其粗观结构由初始条件决定，随机更新顺序只在局部微调层面起作用；挫败是冻结界面的标志性特征，与可逆弛豫形成鲜明区分。

Abstract: Majority threshold dynamics, in which each agent adopts the dominant state in a weighted neighborhood, relaxes a binary field toward consensus or stripes. We study what happens when this rule is made irreversible: each agent, interacting through a Gaussian kernel on a lattice, may flip out of its local weighted minority at most once. The reversible form is threshold dynamics of Merriman-Bence-Osher type, an exactly solvable calibration in which interfaces move by mean curvature with closed-form lattice pinning and mobility. Irreversibility changes the outcome. From random initial conditions, the one-flip rule freezes balanced non-consensus labyrinths that reversible relaxation drives away. The patterned regime is a window of initial spin compositions around equal balance, narrowing as the interaction range grows, controlled by a standardized bias whose onset is independent of scale over a fourfold range. The frozen morphology is arrested coarsening: bicontinuous at balance, with a feature width that grows sublinearly and falls below the interaction range at large scales. We determine the mechanism by intervention. Fixing the initial condition while varying the update order shows that the coarse domain layout is deterministic, while the update order enters only in a secondary first-passage race that fine-tunes the wall positions the deterministic dynamics has already set. Those walls are marked by frustration: agents frozen against their own local field. This signature is identically absent from any reversible relaxation, is overwhelmingly interfacial, and carries almost none of the pattern's large-scale shape.

</details>


### [103] [Entanglement Scaling and Full Counting Statistics in Excited States of Two-Dimensional Rotating Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05722)
*Priyangshu Goswami,Abhishek Dhar,Satya N. Majumdar,Anupam Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维谐振势阱中旋转费米子的N粒子激发态在圆盘区域内的纠缠熵（Rényi熵）与粒子数累积量，解析计算发现面积律标度对激发态仍成立，验证了非相互作用费米子纠缠熵的级数展开，并推导出中心累积量生成函数，证明概率分布与基态结果具有相同的标度性质（仅相差一个变量平移），最后将结果推广到环形区域，发现环内Rényi熵和粒子数累积量可分解为两个边界圆盘相应量之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人研究多集中于基态纠缠熵的性质，而激发态（尤其是旋转系统中多粒子激发态）的纠缠结构尚不清楚。作者希望探究激发态的纠缠熵是否仍满足面积律、级数展开等已知性质，以及能否将基态的结果系统推广到激发态和更一般的几何区域（如环形区域）。

Method: 通过构造由特定N个单粒子能级填充的激发态，在二维谐振势阱旋转角频率为Ω的条件下，解析计算了半径为r的圆盘内的q阶Rényi熵和粒子数涨落的累积量；并推导中心累积量生成函数，进而分析了概率分布函数的标度行为；最后将计算推广到环形几何区域，证明环形区域的Rényi熵和粒子数累积量可以分解为内外两个边界圆盘相应量之和。

Result: (1) 面积律标度对这类激发态仍然成立；(2) 验证了非相互作用费米子纠缠熵关于粒子数累积量的级数展开；(3) 中心累积量生成函数表明圆盘内概率分布与基态结果具有相同标度，仅相差一个变量平移；(4) 在环足够宽时，环的Rényi熵与粒子数累积量等于内外两个圆盘相应量之和（可加性关系）。

Conclusion: 旋转费米子激发态的纠缠熵保持了基态的面积律性质，且离散几何（圆盘、环形）下的纠缠量和粒子数涨落具有系统的可分解结构，为理解非基态（激发态）纠缠结构及其几何分解提供了完整的解析框架。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement entropy of a class of $N$-particle excited state of fermions confined in a two-dimensional harmonic trap rotating at an angular frequency $Ω$. The excited state is constructed by filling a particular set of $N$ single-particle energy levels. We analytically compute the Rényi entropies of order $q$ in a disc of radius $r$ around the centre of the trap, and the cumulants corresponding to number fluctuations of fermions within the disc. We found that the area law scaling of entanglement entropy holds even for a class of excited states. We also verified the well-known series expansion of entanglement entropy in terms of the particle number cumulants for non-interacting fermions. We further derive the centered cumulant generating function, demonstrating that the associated probability distribution function in the disc has identical scaling properties, up to a variable shift, to the known ground-state result. Finally, we extend our result to an annular region, showing that both the Rényi entropy and particle number cumulants of the annulus decompose into sums of the corresponding quantities of the two bounding discs. These additive relations hold as long as the width of the annulus is sufficiently large.

</details>


### [104] [Computing Shear Viscosities from Molecular Dynamics Simulation: Comparing the OrthoBoXY Approach with the Green-Kubo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05845)
*Marcel Brandt,Ralf Ludwig,Dietmar Paschek*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文使用OrthoBoXY方法从平衡分子动力学模拟中计算了15种纯分子液体的剪切粘度，并与Green-Kubo方法结果进行了验证。研究发现粘度不受有限尺寸效应影响（小至250个分子），标准误差也几乎与系统尺寸无关。作者提出：优先运行小系统长时长而非大系统短时长模拟，并根据系统粘度对OrthoBoXY的block长度推荐值进行缩放调整，可最高减少24倍计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OrthoBoXY是一种高效的分子液体粘度计算方法，但对其最佳计算参数（如系统尺寸、运行时长、block长度）缺乏系统的指导，可能导致计算效率低下或结果不准确。作者旨在系统研究这些参数的影响，提供优化的计算策略。

Method: 使用OrthoBoXY方法对15种纯分子液体进行平衡MD模拟计算剪切粘度，并与Green-Kubo方法交叉验证。通过多系统尺寸模拟检查有限尺寸效应，通过多运行时长模拟检验block长度τ_block的选取策略，并分析粘度标准误差与系统尺寸的关系。

Result: 两种方法结果高度一致；粘度不受有限尺寸效应影响（可小至250个分子）；粘度标准误差几乎与系统尺寸无关（因自扩散系数精度与系统尺寸相关加权补偿）；推荐小系统长模拟；高粘度系统τ_block可缩小8倍，低粘度系统可缩小4倍，高流动性系统不宜缩小；结合250分子系统，计算成本最多降低24倍。

Conclusion: 研究者应优先选择较小系统配合较长模拟时间以最大化效率；根据系统粘度特性可安全地缩放OrthoBoXY的block长度，显著降低计算资源需求，且不牺牲数值精度。

Abstract: We calculated shear viscosities of 15 neat molecular liquids from equilibrium molecular dynamics (MD) simulations using the OrthoBoXY approach and compare them to viscosities calculated via the Green-Kubo method. Data from both methods agree very well. Here, we show how to avoid pitfalls while computing the OrthoBoXY-data to obtain optimal results. From simulations of multiple system sizes, we could verify that the viscosity of molecular liquids is not influenced by finite size effects down to systems as small as 250 molecules. Moreover, we demonstrate that also the standard error of the viscosity is nearly independent of the system size. This is shown to be a consequence of a compensation effect of an increasing accuracy of the self-diffusion coefficients with increasing systems-size and the system-size dependent weighting according to the OrthoBoXY-equation. As a consequence, we suggest that it is preferable to run simulations of smaller systems with longer simulation times rather than larger systems with shorter simulation runs. In addition, we discuss a refinement of the recently introduced "recipe" for OrthoBoXY simulations block-lengths $τ_\mathrm{block}$. Based on data from simulations with varying run-lengths, we suggest the following modification: for highly viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/8$, significantly reducing the computational resources needed. For less viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/4$. For systems with high fluidity, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ should not be scaled down in order to achieve reliable results. When using a smaller system size of 250 molecules, these refinements are leading up to a 24-fold reduction in computational cost compared to the previous recommended set-up without sacrificing numerical accuracy.

</details>


### [105] [Dynamic scaling behavior in the presence of a periodic magnetic driving across Ising continuous transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05936)
*Andrea Pelissetto,Ettore Vicari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在临界温度下，二维Ising模型受到周期性磁场驱动时的临界动力学行为，发现了由时间t、振幅A和周期P之间的非平凡相互作用引起的特殊动力学标度行为，其标度变量为τ=t/P和σ=AP^κ（κ=y_h/z≈0.865）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解周期性外部驱动对经典铁磁系统临界动力学的影响，特别是周期性磁场如何与临界涨落产生非平凡的相互作用，从而揭示超越传统静态标度框架的新动力学标度规律。

Method: 以二维Ising模型为范例，在临界温度下施加周期性磁场h(t)=-A cos(2πt/P)，采用纯弛豫动力学（如Metropolis或Glauber动力学）模拟演化，通过标度分析识别出关键的标度变量τ=t/P和σ=AP^κ，其中κ=y_h/z由磁场临界维数y_h和动态指数z决定。

Result: 发现磁化强度和键能密度的动力学标度行为围绕一条光滑曲线呈现振荡，并在大τ极限下趋近于平稳行为。标度变量的形式为τ=t/P和σ=AP^κ，其中κ=y_h/z，对二维Ising模型κ≈0.865。

Conclusion: 周期性驱动会在临界系统中产生特殊的动力学标度行为，该行为由时间、振幅和周期的非平凡耦合决定。此外，还简要讨论了零磁场下周期性变温驱动Ising系统穿越临界点的动力学行为，为更广泛的周期性驱动临界现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the critical dynamics arising from a time-dependent periodic homogenous source coupled to the order-parameter field, which drives a classical ferromagnetic system across a continuous transition. For this purpose, we consider the paradigmatic two-dimensional (2D) Ising model in the presence of a periodic magnetic field $h(t)=-A\, \cos (2πt/P)$, evolving under a purely relaxational dynamics at the critical temperature. We show that the periodic driving gives rise to a peculiar dynamic scaling behavior in the thermodynamic limit, arising from a nontrivial interplay among the time $t$, the amplitude $A$ and period $P$ of $h(t)$. The relevant scaling variables are $τ=t/P$ and $σ=A P^κ$, with $κ= y_h/z$, where $y_h=(d+2-η)/2$ is the critical dimension of the magnetic field, and $z$ is dynamic exponent for the critical relaxational dynamics ($κ\approx 0.865$ for the 2D Ising model). The dynamic scaling behaviors of the magnetization and bond-energy density show an oscillatory behavior around a smooth curve which approaches a large-$τ$ stationary behavior. We also briefly discuss the dynamic behavior of an Ising system driven across the critical point by a periodic time-varying temperature at zero magnetic field.

</details>


### [106] [Measurement-induced entanglement Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06006)
*Viktor Eisler,Erik Tonni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究无限跳跃链基态在占据数基下进行部分投影测量后的纠缠哈密顿量。对于被两个测量区域与链条其余部分隔开的线段，作者证明其约化密度矩阵可通过共形映射和规范变换与巨正则态关联，纠缠哈密顿量由局部逆温度描述，该逆温度在端点处以平方根方式趋零且与测量结果无关；相反，局部化学势与线段内诱导电荷密度相关，因此后选择态的纠缠哈密顿量比熵包含更多关于测量结果的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解投影测量对量子系统纠缠结构的影响，特别是探究后选择态中纠缠哈密顿量的性质，以及其与测量结果之间的信息关联。

Method: 采用共形场论方法，通过对无限跳跃链基态在占据数基上进行部分投影测量，利用共形映射和规范变换建立约化密度矩阵与巨正则态之间的联系。

Result: 纠缠哈密顿量由局部位置依赖的逆温度描述，该逆温度在测量区域端点处以平方根形式消失且独立于具体测量结果；而局部化学势则与测量诱导的电荷密度相关，反映了测量结果的信息。

Conclusion: 后选择态的纠缠哈密顿量包含远多于纠缠熵的关于测量结果的信息，其中逆温度部分普适且与测量结果无关，而化学势部分编码了测量结果的具体信息。

Abstract: We study the entanglement Hamiltonian of an infinite hopping chain in its ground state, after partial projective measurements in the occupation basis. For a segment separated by two measurement regions from the rest of the chain, we show that the reduced density matrix can be related to a grand-canonical state via a conformal mapping and a gauge transformation in the underlying field-theory description. The entanglement Hamiltonian is then described by a local inverse temperature that vanishes as a square root around the endpoints and is independent of the particular measurement outcome. In sharp contrast, the local chemical potential is shown to be related to the induced charge density in the segment. Hence the entanglement Hamiltonian of a post-selected state contains much more information on the measurement outcome than the respective entropy.

</details>


### [107] [Thermodynamic statistics of given names in USA and France](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06048)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文利用美国和法国超过100年的官方政府数据集，分析了人名的出现频率/流行度分布，发现这些分布可通过Lorenz曲线和Pareto曲线表征，Gini系数稳定在0.85-0.95范围内。研究表明人名分布与财富不平等类似，可用Rayleigh-Jeans（RJ）热化与凝聚现象很好地描述，其中RJ热化源于能量和概率范数守恒两个积分不变量。时间相关性分析显示人名间的相关性在20世纪中叶前保持稳定，此后发生显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将物理学中研究财富不平等的统计力学方法（Lorenz曲线、Pareto曲线、RJ热化）应用到人名分布分析中，探索人名流行度分布是否遵循类似物理系统的统计规律，并揭示其长期时间演化特征。

Method: 使用美国和法国的官方政府数据集，对超过100年时间尺度上的人名出现频率/流行度分布进行分析。采用Lorenz曲线和Pareto曲线（财富不平等分析的经典工具）刻画分布特征，计算Gini系数；同时引入Rayleigh-Jeans（RJ）热化与凝聚理论进行建模，类比物理系统中能量和概率范数守恒的积分不变量。此外还计算了人名间的时间相关性。

Result: 人名分布曲线在超过100年的时间尺度上保持稳定，Gini系数维持在0.85-0.95的窄范围内。人名分布与财富不平等分布相似，可以很好地用RJ热化与凝聚现象描述。人名间的时间相关性在20世纪中叶之前保持稳定，之后发生显著变化。

Conclusion: 人名流行度分布遵循与财富不平等类似的统计物理规律，可通过RJ热化理论统一描述，表明人名分布可能具有普适的统计特征。时间相关性的阶段性变化反映了社会文化因素的变迁对人名选择行为的影响。

Abstract: Using official government data sets of USA and France we analyze the occurrence/frequency/popularity distributions of given names on a time scale of more than 100 years. These distributions are characterized through the Lorenz and Pareto curves broadly used in the analysis of wealth inequality in the world. These curves remain stable during the considered time period with the Gini coefficient remaining in the narrow range 0.85-0.95. As for the case of wealth inequality, we show that the distributions of names are well described by the Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) thermalization and condensation phenomenon well studied in various physical systems. The RJ thermalization results from two integrals of motion being analogous to energy and probability norm conservation in physical systems with energy states corresponding to popularity levels of names. Time correlations between names are also determined showing their stability until the middle of the twentieth century and a significant change after that.

</details>


### [108] [Collective search-and-capture under competing assignment policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06084)
*Néstor Sepúlveda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个最小晶格模型下的主动搜索-捕获问题，其中持续随机游走者通过有限范围的互斥分配规则不可逆地捕获静止目标。研究发现完成时间T_c关于重定向率α是非单调的，在中等持久性处存在最小值。关键发现是T_c由捕获过程的极端晚期尾部主导，而非典型的平均捕获时间。更重要的是，分配策略（最大基数匹配）对捕获时间的控制可以比行走者的持续性更强，在中等搜索半径下可将T_c降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多智能体搜索-捕获问题的集体动力学机制，特别是持续随机游走者的持久性、搜索半径和分配策略如何共同影响整体完成时间。

Method: 使用最小晶格模型进行数值模拟：持久随机游走者通过有限范围、互斥的分配规则捕获静止目标；系统比较三种分配策略——单轮贪心分配、级联再分配和候选图上的最大基数匹配；测量完成时间T_c作为重定向率α和搜索半径R的函数。

Result: T_c(α)呈非单调性，在中等持久性处有最小值，且该最小值随R增大而减弱。捕获动力学显示T_c由捕获过程晚期尾部的极端事件所控制，该尾部主要由自由探索阶段主导。最大基数匹配在中等R下即可产生强烈加速，在大R下可将T_c降低数倍，在中等R下可降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 在集体耗竭耦合的搜索问题中，分配策略对捕获时间的控制能力可能比行走者的持久性更强。两种贪心策略在小R下表现一致，但最大基数匹配在中等及较大R下具有显著优势，表明优化匹配结构是加速集体搜索-捕获的关键杠杆。

Abstract: We study a minimal lattice model of active search-and-capture in which persistent random walkers locate and irreversibly capture immobile targets through a finite-range, mutually exclusive assignment rule. We measure the collective completion time $T_c$ as a function of the walkers' reorientation rate $α$ and the search radius $R$. The dependence $T_c(α)$ is non-monotonic, with a minimum at intermediate persistence whose depth decreases as $R$ grows. Capture kinetics show that $T_c$ is not a typical capture time but is governed by the extreme, late-time tail of the capture process, while the bulk of targets are captured much earlier; this tail is controlled mainly by the free-exploration phase rather than by the final directed approach. We then compare the baseline single-round assignment rule with cascading reassignment and with maximum-cardinality matching on a candidate graph. The two greedy policies (single-round and cascading) agree at very small $R$, whereas maximum-cardinality matching already produces a strong speedup at moderate $R$: improved matching reduces $T_c$ by factors of several at large $R$, and by more than an order of magnitude at moderate $R$. Thus, in this collective, depletion-coupled search problem, the assignment policy can control the capture time more strongly than the walkers' persistence.

</details>


### [109] [Transport coefficients in hard-sphere fluids: thermodynamic versus kinetic descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06143)
*Miguel Hoyuelos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从Onsager矩阵的一般表达式出发，发展了一种硬球流体输运系数的热力学理论，该理论预测输运系数与稀薄气体值的比值仅依赖于平衡热力学性质，可通过状态方程直接计算。该理论获得了热导率、粘度和自扩散系数的紧凑表达式，并在几乎整个流体范围内定量复现了模拟数据，其中自扩散系数的预测显著优于Enskog动力学理论，证明了输运系数可在纯热力学框架内被精确描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的输运系数计算通常依赖动力学理论（如Enskog理论），但这些方法在某些方面（如自扩散系数）预测精度有限。本文探索是否可以从热力学框架出发，仅利用平衡热力学性质来准确描述输运系数，从而建立更简洁且更精确的理论。

Method: 从Onsager矩阵的一般表达式出发推导输运系数的热力学理论，预测σ/σ_id比值仅由平衡热力学性质决定，可通过状态方程直接计算；针对热导率、粘度和自扩散系数分别推导出紧凑的解析表达式。

Result: 所得到的表达式在几乎整个流体范围内定量复现了模拟数据；在自扩散系数方面，该理论的预测显著优于Enskog动力学理论。

Conclusion: 输运系数可以被精确地描述在一个纯粹的热力学框架内，无需依赖详细的动力学信息。

Abstract: A thermodynamic theory for transport coefficients in hard-sphere fluids is developed from a general expression for the Onsager matrix. The theory predicts the ratio $σ/σ_{\rm id}$, where $σ$ is a transport coefficient and $σ_{\rm id}$ denotes its dilute-gas value. This ratio depends exclusively on equilibrium thermodynamic properties and can therefore be computed directly from the equation of state. Compact expressions are obtained for the thermal conductivity, viscosity, and self-diffusion coefficient. These expressions quantitatively reproduce simulation data over almost the entire fluid range and, in the case of self-diffusion, significantly improve upon the predictions of Enskog kinetic theory. The results demonstrate that transport coefficients can be accurately described within a purely thermodynamic framework.

</details>


### [110] [A Universal Control Budget for First-Passage Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06368)
*Shiling Liang,Ruicheng Bao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现有限马尔可夫链的平均首次通过时间对任意速率的对数灵敏度绝对值不超过1，且所有灵敏度之和等于-1，这两条定律构成一个保守的控制预算，限制了通过改变速率来调控反应完成时间的程度，并进一步约束了动力学校对（kinetic proofreading）的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次通过时间是反应完成的核心可观测物理量，但此前缺乏关于其均值如何响应速率变化的通用约束。作者希望找到一种普适的、不依赖具体系统细节的规律来刻画速率改变对反应完成时间的影响。

Method: 对任意有限马尔可夫链，推导平均首次通过时间对任意速率的对数灵敏度，证明其绝对值上界为1且所有速率灵敏度之和为-1；基于这两条守恒定律构建控制预算，并将其应用于动力学校对等场景分析。

Result: 证明了两个普适定律：(1) 每个速率的对数灵敏度绝对值不超过1；(2) 所有速率的灵敏度求和为-1。由此导出控制预算，说明加速某些转变必须以其他转变减慢为代价；同时该预算将动力学校对的判别能力上限定在检查站数量，并给出了其关于底物浓度的灵敏度价格。

Conclusion: 平均首次通过时间对速率变化遵循一个普适的守恒预算规律，这不仅统一了散在的灵敏度结果，还为反应网络调控和动力学校对的性能边界提供了严格的定量约束。

Abstract: The first-passage time is the natural observable of reaction completion, yet how its mean responds to a rate change has lacked a general constraint. We show that the logarithmic sensitivity of the mean first-passage time of any finite Markov chain to any rate is bounded by one in magnitude, and that these sensitivities sum to -1. Together the two laws form a conserved control budget: speeding completion through some transitions must be paid for by others, and a coordinated change shifts the completion time only as far as the budget allows. Raising an activation barrier or shifting the depth of a well moves many rates at once, yet neither can shift the completion time further than a single rate could. The budget caps kinetic-proofreading discrimination at the checkpoint count, and prices it in sensitivity to substrate concentration.

</details>

### [111] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轮廓积分方法（CIM），用于高效计算控制形式参数化线性系统在指定时间区间内、达到用户给定精度的输出。CIM通过对改进积分轮廓应用求积规则来近似逆拉普拉斯变换，并与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）有效结合，通过贪心算法基于推导的误差估计构建投影空间，同时自然满足Hermite插值条件，大幅降低参数域内输入-输出关系的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化线性系统的输入-输出关系计算在控制工程中至关重要，但直接求解成本高昂。现有方法在处理参数域、时间区间、初始条件及各类输入函数时效率有限，需要一种既能保证精度又能显著降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 1) 采用轮廓积分方法（CIM），通过沿改进积分轮廓的求积规则近似逆拉普拉斯变换；2) 将该方法与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）集成，利用贪心算法根据推导出的误差估计构建投影空间；3) 证明该投影框架自然满足Hermite插值条件；4) 在基准非参数化和参数化控制系统上与最先进的投影MOR方法进行对比评估。

Result: 该方法在非参数化和参数化基准控制系统上均展示了良好的精度与效率，相比最先进的投影MOR方法，在跨越参数域计算输入-输出关系时显著降低了成本，同时适用于广泛的输入函数和由低维子空间良好捕捉的初始条件。

Conclusion: CIM与贪心投影MOR结合的方法为参数化线性控制系统的高效输出计算提供了一种通用、精确且计算经济的新框架，其Hermite插值性质的天然满足进一步增强了解析能力，是该领域相比现有SOTA方法的有力替代方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [112] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了异质多盒搜索代价博弈（Hider在两盒中藏球，Searcher自适应开盒直至找到两球）的精确解。博弈值为三个显式有理函数的最大值；依据参数区域不同，最优Hider策略为限制在三种、五种或全部六种放置位形上的乘积形式分布。所有非浪费确定性Searcher策略与72棵基本决策树收益等价，仅产生42个不同开盒计数剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质多目标搜索博弈是经典搜索理论的推广，此前同类问题仅在同质或单目标的假设下被解决。本文旨在针对三盒异质搜索代价这一最小的非平凡情形，给出精确封闭形式的博弈值和完全刻画的最优策略，填补该方向的理论空白。

Method: 方法包括：（1）将博弈建模为零和线性规划，证明所有非浪费确定性Searcher策略可归并为72棵基本决策树，进而得到42个不同的开盒计数剖面；（2）按参数区域划分三种情形，分别用解析论证、两个混合策略和四个混合策略求解；（3）利用精确有理Bernstein基证书验证参量化混合策略在整个代价区域上的可行性；（4）通过独立实现复现策略集、零和线性规划值以及154个二叉节点。

Result: 博弈值被精确刻画为三个显式有理函数的最大值。最优Hider策略在三个参数区域分别退化为限制于3、5或6种放置位形的乘积形式分布。精确的Bernstein基证书证明了所有混合策略在完整代价区域上的可行性，且独立实现完全复现了策略集、博弈值和全部154个被检验的节点。

Conclusion: 本文为异质三盒双球搜索博弈提供了完全精确的解，证明了可以通过有限决策树归并和有理Bernstein基证书来严格求解此类博弈，为更一般多盒、多目标异质搜索博弈的精确求解建立了方法论基础。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [113] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）中带Stiefel流形约束与非凸二次约束的离散优化问题，提出子空间分裂重构和单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法（DASSP），并首次为CDFT计算提供严格收敛保证的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT是描述电子激发态和电荷局域态的强有力框架，但其离散优化问题由于同时包含Stiefel流形约束和非凸二次约束而难以求解。现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构面临收敛问题，亟需一种兼具高可行精度、稳健性和效率的求解方法。

Method: 首先利用系统固有的旋转不变性并引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂（subspace-splitting）重构公式，将两组约束解耦。基于该重构，提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法（Damped ADMM），称为DASSP。每次迭代包含三个高效可实现的步骤：谱最小化步骤、投影梯度步骤和阻尼对偶上升步骤。

Result: 在合成和真实CDFT问题上的数值实验表明，DASSP达到高可行精度，并且在不牺牲稳健性的前提下表现出良好的计算效率。这是首个对CDFT计算具有严格收敛保证的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为可靠、高效的大规模CDFT应用铺平了道路，填补了CDFT优化算法在收敛理论保障方面的空白。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [114] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对野火灾害中弱势群体（如医院患者和长期护理居民）的疏散问题，提出了一种两阶段随机优化模型，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径，并采用基于逻辑的Benders分解方法（结合组合Benders割和逻辑不等式）来解决NP难问题。通过在科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据验证，该方法相比替代策略显著提升了避难所选址、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火灾害中弱势群体的疏散支持是一个关键但研究不足的领域。医院患者和长期护理居民等特殊人群在疏散过程中面临严格的时间窗口约束和资源限制，现有方法难以在可接受的计算时间内获得高质量解，亟需一种能够综合考虑设施选址、车队规模和车辆路由的优化框架。

Method: 构建两阶段随机优化模型，集成设施选址、车队规模确定和带严格时间窗的车辆路径优化；针对模型的NP难特性，提出基于逻辑的Benders分解（Logic-Based Benders Decomposition）求解方法，采用组合Benders割（Combinatorial Benders Cuts）和基于逻辑的不等式来加速收敛。

Result: 通过大量数值实验和科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据验证，所提出的方法能获得高质量解，在避难所选址、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率方面均显著优于其他替代策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段随机优化模型与逻辑Benders分解方法能有效解决野火场景下弱势群体的疏散规划问题，为实际应急管理提供了更优的决策支持工具。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [115] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望代价（被积函数可能非光滑、非凸）与下半连续、近端有界函数之和。该方法基于非光滑局域化下降引理，通过逐步细化的样本平均逼近期望代价，并采用Armijo型线搜索确保充分下降（容许由样本近似引起的随机误差）。在无需正则项（弱）凸性、无需随机预言机方差一致有界的条件下，建立了函数值序列几乎必然收敛及所有聚点平稳性的收敛保证；借助KL性质进一步升级为整个轨迹收敛到单一平稳点，并针对指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长的样本量给出显式多项式收敛速率（至多差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机近端方法在处理期望代价与非光滑非凸正则项之和的问题时，通常需要正则项的（弱）凸性假设以及随机预言机方差的均匀上界，限制了其适用性。作者旨在建立更广泛的框架，同时覆盖光滑Lipschitz梯度损失与"光滑损失减凸函数"这类差分结构，并在更松弛的假设下给出收敛性保证。

Method: 提出近端随机次梯度方法：(1) 用逐步细化的样本平均替代期望代价；(2) 采用Armijo型线搜索选择近端次梯度步长，确保充分下降性质（容许样本近似引入的随机误差）；(3) 对被积函数施加非光滑、局域化的下降引理变体这一结构假设；(4) 借助KL性质提升子序列收敛到全轨迹收敛；(5) 对指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长的样本量推导显式收敛速率。

Result: 在样本量序列仅需非递减且无界（无需指定增长率）的松弛条件下，建立了函数值序列几乎必然收敛以及轨迹所有聚点平稳性的结果；该结果即使在光滑情形下也是新的。借助KL性质，将子序列收敛升级为整个轨迹收敛到单一平稳点。对指数型KL函数和多项式增长样本量，导出了函数值和迭代点的显式多项式收敛速率（至多差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法适应更广泛的问题类型，不要求正则项（弱）凸性，也不要求随机预言机方差的一致上界。在光滑情形下收敛性保证也是全新的，并且通过KL分析获得了强化的全轨迹收敛与显式速率结果。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [116] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带两阶段自适应动量的惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL⁺-TP），用于求解一类多块非凸非光滑优化问题，该方法在理论和实验上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛存在于震前异常分析和机器学习等应用中，现有方法在收敛速度和参数选择灵活性方面存在局限，需要一种更高效的求解方法。

Method: 提出IBPL⁺-TP方法，包含三个关键创新：(1) 两阶段自适应动量策略用于有效更新外推参数；(2) 允许使用两个不同的外推点加速收敛；(3) 两个外推点的外推参数独立于且不受其他参数约束。方法采用惯性块近端线性化框架，并对外推参数进行自适应调整。

Result: 证明了目标函数的单调收敛性，以及迭代序列全局收敛到临界点，并建立了收敛速率。在稀疏非负矩阵分解（ℓ₀约束）和稀疏非负CP分解（ℓ₀约束）两个非凸非光滑机器学习问题上的数值实验表明，该方法优于多种最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的IBPL⁺-TP方法在保持理论收敛保证（单调收敛、全局收敛到临界点、收敛速率）的同时，通过两阶段自适应动量和双外推点策略显著提升了求解多块非凸非光滑优化问题的效率，数值实验验证了其优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [117] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的迭代收敛框架，用于分析Bregman近端方法（BPMs）在非凸目标函数下的迭代序列收敛性，并首次在没有凸性假设的条件下证明了连续时间BPM（镜面流）的轨迹收敛到稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端方法（BPMs）的迭代收敛性长期未解，尤其是在非凸目标函数情形下。已有工作仅对Shannon熵核和线性约束问题建立了收敛性，缺乏适用于更广泛核和复合目标函数的统一框架。

Method: 扩展了已有工作（chen2026skl）中的解析工具，特别是缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质。通过引入依赖于核的参数化函数，证明了扩展SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立（当核具有闭定义域时），并验证标准BPMs在温和正则条件下满足框架假设，从而建立迭代收敛性。对于连续时间情形，利用参数化函数证明镜面流在o-极小可定义目标函数下收敛到稳定点。

Result: 建立了适用于广泛核和复合目标函数的统一BPM迭代收敛框架；证明了扩展SKŁ性质对连续子解析函数成立；验证了标准BPMs在此框架下的迭代收敛性；首次证明了镜面流对o-极小可定义目标函数的轨迹收敛到稳定点，无需凸性假设或稳定点孤立性假设。

Conclusion: 离散和连续时间两方面的收敛结果共同为BPMs提供了一套统一的轨迹收敛理论，填补了非凸情形下BPM迭代收敛性的空白。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [118] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出重定域Yau-Yau滤波器（RD-YYF），通过在最新状态估计为中心的固定大小局部窗口内求解前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）来改进非线性目标跟踪。该方法结合离线物理信息神经网络（PINNs）生成FKE解快照、主成分分析构建低维密度演化表示，以及轻量残差代理在线预测密度演化。实验表明其在两个几何约束目标跟踪任务中的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波，同时保持高效的单步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器将非线性状态估计重构为前向Kolmogorov方程控制的概率密度传播问题，但将其应用于目标跟踪时，需要在有限计算域上高效近似FKE。传统方法在全局域上求解计算开销大且数值分辨率难以集中在高概率区域，因此需要一种既能高效近似FKE又能保持跟踪精度的求解方案。

Method: RD-YYF采用三阶段设计：(1) 离线阶段，在固定大小的局部窗口内使用PINNs求解FKE生成密度演化快照，窗口以最新状态估计为中心；(2) 通过主成分分析（PCA）对密度演化进行低维表示，并训练轻量残差代理网络将初始条件系数和域中心映射到终端解系数；(3) 在线阶段，利用预训练代理预测重定域窗口内的密度演化，随后执行观测更新和状态估计。

Result: 在两个几何约束目标跟踪示例上的实验表明，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和粒子滤波（PF），且每时间步推理保持高效。消融实验显示，域重定心策略改善了高概率区域的密度逼近精度，并加速了离线PINN的收敛。

Conclusion: RD-YYF展示了将物理信息神经网络与低维代理模型结合用于高效非线性目标跟踪的潜力，为Yau-Yau滤波器在实际跟踪场景中的应用提供了可行且高效的实现路径。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [119] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源的Benders型分解算法软件包，用于求解多阶段随机整数规划问题，该算法结合了ReLU割以提供一般混合整数状态变量的收敛保证，并实现了基于归一化和正则化的割选择策略以及交替割准则来优化求解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划的求解需要收敛保证，但标准的Benders割和Lagrangian割在处理一般混合整数状态变量时可能无法保证收敛。此外，用于生成ReLU割的对偶问题往往存在多个最优解，不同解产生割的质量差异很大，需要有效的割选择策略来提升算法性能。

Method: 该软件包实现了具有收敛保证的Benders型分解算法，包含多种割族（Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割和ReLU割）。针对ReLU割对偶问题多最优解的问题，提出了两种割选择策略：基于归一化和基于正则化的方法；同时提出了一种交替割准则，在便宜的Benders割有效时使用之，仅在必要时才调用更昂贵的紧割。

Result: 在四类多阶段随机整数规划基准问题上进行了计算实验，验证了所提出方法的有效性，并揭示了问题结构对算法实际性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该开源软件包为多阶段随机整数规划提供了实用的分解求解工具，通过割选择策略和交替割准则在收敛保证与计算效率之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [120] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭凸集变分不等式上的连续时间投影神经网络，针对一类既非Hessian生成也非标量的状态相关矩阵度量，证明了投影在参数与度量上的联合正则性，并给出局部与全局指数收敛速率及周期性轨道环的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影神经网络的收敛性分析仅覆盖Hessian生成的逆度量与标量状态相关度量，尚缺乏对一般矩阵度量的理论保障。本文旨在填补这一空白，为更广泛的矩阵度量下的投影动力学提供正则性、收敛性与极限行为的严格分析。

Method: 通过状态相关正定矩阵对算子进行预条件处理，其逆矩阵定义投影度量。利用投影在参数与度量上的联合正则性，结合谱界改进Euclidean Lipschitz估计（从平方界改进为界本身）。针对Lipschitz强单调算子与Lipschitz度量证明局部指数收敛（给出显式速率和半径），在紧可行集上利用度量变分界证明全局指数收敛。对二次连续可微、一致正定度量与强单调线性算子，分析周期轨道的环形结构及逆度量的可积性。

Result: 证明了非Hessian、非标量矩阵度量下投影的联合正则性；Euclidean Lipschitz估计较以往平方界有显著改进；建立了局部与全局指数收敛的显式速率和半径；发现强单调线性算子情形下存在周期轨道环，且该环上逆度量违反Hessian可积性。确定性数值实验验证了解析公式并量化了收敛证书的松弛度。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性建立了通用矩阵度量下投影神经网络的收敛理论，扩展了既有分析范围，揭示了度量不可积性下仍可保证指数收敛，并刻画了特殊的周期解结构，为投影神经网络的设计与分析提供新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [121] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆优化方法来同时学习决策者的偏好（感知成本）和决策规则，通过三个案例研究（轮班分配、生产计划、实际路由问题）验证了该方法在捕捉和复现专家决策方面优于仅建模单一要素的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家决策策略往往隐含多年经验积累，难以显式表达。理解和迁移这种决策知识对于培训新手、提升人机系统性能以及实现全自主系统具有重要意义，因此需要从专家观察到的决策行为中学习可解释的决策模型。

Method: 开发一种逆优化方法，联合学习决策者的感知成本（偏好）和决策规则。通过构建数学模型从专家观察决策中反向推导其隐含的偏好参数和决策约束规则，三者案例研究验证了方法的通用性。

Result: 在三个案例（轮班分配、生产规划、实际路由问题）中，同时建模感知成本和决策规则比仅建模单一要素能产生更好的预测性能，验证了所提框架的有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 联合学习感知成本与决策规则的逆优化框架能够更全面地捕捉专家决策行为，为知识迁移和自主学习系统提供了可行且可解释的建模途径。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [122] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于Hilbert空间中可微（可能非凸）函数最小化的快速梯度算法。该算法将凸函数的干摩擦性质推广为非凸设定下的"类干摩擦"性质，并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数。算法在参数选择下可达到子序列收敛到临界点或全序列收敛到近似临界点，并在满足KL性质的优值函数条件下实现全序列收敛到临界点。该算法可退化为多种现有的含Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法，并可通过Moreau包络的变分性质扩展到弱凸非光滑优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的惯性梯度算法在非凸优化中面临收敛性证明困难、参数选择不够灵活等问题。干摩擦性质已被证明对凸函数的快速收敛有效，但尚未系统推广到非凸情形。因此，作者旨在将干摩擦概念扩展到非凸设定，并结合线搜索技术设计一种统一框架，既能自适应选择参数，又能覆盖现有多种算法，同时扩展到弱凸非光滑问题。

Method: 1. 将凸函数中的干摩擦性质扩展为非凸设定下的"类干摩擦"（dry-like friction）性质；2. 采用线搜索技术自适应更新每次迭代的参数；3. 利用KL性质建立优值函数的全序列收敛性分析；4. 利用Moreau包络的变分性质将算法推广到弱凸非光滑优化问题，并扩展到更广泛的（非凸、非连续）KL函数类。

Result: 算法在适当参数条件下可实现子序列收敛到临界点或全序列收敛到近似临界点；在优值函数满足KL性质时可实现全序列收敛到临界点。该算法能退化为多种现有惯性梯度算法，并通过数值仿真验证了算法的效率和结合类干摩擦、外推与线搜索的优势。

Conclusion: 作者成功设计了一种通用的、参数灵活的快速梯度算法框架，统一了多类现有惯性梯度算法，并在非凸和非光滑设定下建立了收敛性保证，为快速优化算法在更广泛问题中的应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [123] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究由Bregman损失定义的线性回归模型，发现即使生成势函数φ强凸，目标函数在θ上仍可能非凸，并通过Hessian的加权Gram矩阵表示给出了局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降条件线性收敛的充分条件。针对二次-四次势函数，导出了精确的标量凸性条件、负曲率区间及正曲率的局部和全局充分条件。数值实验表明标量条件可能过于保守，且梯度下降步长收敛范围随四次项参数增大而缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman回归中生成势函数的凸性并不保证拟合模型的优化目标是凸的，存在理论上的空白。需要揭示残差依赖的曲率如何与生成势函数及设计矩阵相互作用，从而为优化算法的收敛性提供理论保障。

Method: 将回归目标的Hessian表示为加权Gram矩阵，其中权重依赖于势函数的导数与当前残差；基于此表示推导局部强凸性、光滑性及梯度下降线性收敛的条件；对二次-四次势函数进行精确的标量分析，并结合数值实验验证。

Result: 得到了局部强凸性和光滑性条件；对二次-四次势函数给出了精确的标量凸性条件、负曲率区间和正曲率的充分条件；数值实验表明标量条件可能过保守，且梯度下降收敛的步长范围随四次参数增大而变窄。

Conclusion: 反向Bregman回归中残差依赖的曲率显著影响优化几何，生成势函数的凸性与目标函数凸性之间存在系统性缺口；所提加权Gram矩阵框架为理解该问题提供了统一的工具。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [124] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下的复杂度，引入图的"临界根"概念，并证明当二次根变化时，问题最优值是关于临界根的分段仿射凸函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是一个基础的图论优化问题，其目标是寻找给定图的子图，使得所有顶点上某个给定函数（在顶点的子图度上求值）之和最大化。此前对该问题的一般性研究较多，但对二次函数情形的复杂性及最优值的结构化性质缺乏深入理解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文针对二次函数情形引入"临界根"（critical roots）这一新的图论概念，通过分析这些临界根将参数空间划分为多个区间，并利用这些区间对不同二次根下问题的最优值进行刻画，证明最优值在这些区间上是凸的分段仿射函数。

Result: 证明了在二次函数情形下，随着二次根的变化，度序列优化问题的最优值在由图的临界根所定义的区间上是分段仿射且凸的。

Conclusion: 通过引入临界根这一概念，本文给出了二次度序列优化问题最优值的结构化刻画，揭示了其凸分段仿射性质，为该问题及其复杂度研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [125] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置不确定条件下最大化加权覆盖，并设计了PAROO算法来确定传感器朝向，实验表明其优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络在实际部署中面临传感器位置不确定性的挑战，同时不同监控区域的重要性存在差异，现有方法未同时考虑鲁棒性和优先级需求。

Method: 构建融合监控约束和RRF鲁棒公式的数学优化模型，并提出优先级感知鲁棒方向优化（PAROO）算法以最大化加权覆盖目标。

Result: 在机场模拟监控场景中，所提框架能有效将感知资源导向高优先级区域，加权覆盖性能优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在安全敏感环境中具有实际应用价值，能够在传感器位置不确定的情况下有效兼顾优先级与鲁棒性的覆盖需求。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [126] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层防御-攻击者模型（BDAM）提供了第一个精确最优解基准。通过下层对偶化将占主导的攻击者路径项转化为精确的单层混合整数规划，在之前基准的全部18个三行和五行配置上验证了最优性（其中17个在10秒内求解），并给出了15×30网格上未经认证的强解。同时揭示了供应规则在平局情形下的病态性，提出了平局不变的威胁走廊公式，并引入MILP-DA算法，在13/18个认证配置和全部18个更大规模配置上超越了此前发表的混合元启发式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层干预（bi-level interdiction）模型通常依靠元启发式方法求解，在测试规模上无法获得精确最优解来评估解质量。作者此前用混合元启发式求解BDAM，但缺乏精确对照基准，因此需要为BDAM提供精确最优解以评估和改进启发式方法。

Method: （1）利用BDAM中占主导的攻击者最短路径项，通过对下层问题进行对偶化，将其精确重构为单层混合整数规划（当弧长是防御者二进制决策的线性函数时成立）；（2）分析供应规则在最短路径存在平局时的病态性，提出平局不变的威胁走廊公式；（3）提出MILP-DA算法，将精确锚点（MILP）与走廊解码和可行性修复相结合。

Result: 精确MILP在全部18个三行/五行配置上获得认证最优解（17个在10秒内），在15×30网格上获得强未被认证的可行解。作者此前发表的元启发式平均值比该精确前沿低3.55%，差距随规模扩大。平局处理对目标函数影响小于10^(-3)，但可实现供应成本差异达0.83单位。MILP-DA在13个认证配置和全部18个更大配置上胜出，所有失败均出现在三行网格。

Conclusion: BDAM可通过下层对偶化获得精确单层重构，从而为元启发式提供可靠的质量评估基准；平局导致供应规则病态但被目标权重抑制；结合精确锚点与修复机制的MILP-DA显著优于此前发表的混合元启发式，但在狭窄（三行）网格上仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [127] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中模型无关策略迭代（PI）的初始化难题，提出了一种新颖的谱分配方法，通过利用Lyapunov型算子谱和逐步调节累积因子，从稳定的辅助系统逐步逼近原系统以获得初始稳定器，并设计无需初始稳定策略的模型无关算法实现最优控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代（PI）是求解最优控制问题的重要强化学习工具，但其初始化阶段（寻找初始稳定控制器）通常依赖完整模型信息，这严重限制了模型无关PI的初始化。由于乘性噪声的存在，随机系统的稳定性不由Hurwitz条件保证，而需满足包含漂移项和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式，使得模型无关PI的初始化更具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种新颖的谱分配方法：借助Lyapunov型算子的谱，从稳定的辅助系统出发，通过逐步调整累积因子来渐进逼近原始系统，从而获得稳定控制增益。进一步结合系统数据和累积因子调节，设计了一个不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提出的谱分配方法能够有效获得初始稳定控制器，且所设计的模型无关算法能够实现连续时间不定随机线性二次控制的最优控制。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中模型无关策略迭代初始化困难的问题，通过谱分配和累积因子调节策略，成功实现了无需初始稳定策略的模型无关最优控制，拓展了策略迭代在随机系统中的应用范围。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [128] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种新的分布式可诊断性条件及其验证方法，可处理传输延迟影响下的故障诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式诊断方法通常假设诊断信息可以实时、无延迟地传输，但在实际系统中传输延迟不可避免。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究存在传输延迟时系统能否在有限步内实现故障诊断。

Method: 提出了一种扩展的分布式可诊断性条件，将已有文献中的分散式可诊断性条件推广到分布式场景。通过构建一种名为delay recorder（延迟记录器）的新型结构以及定义一个新的诊断函数来验证该条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够成功判定故障是否可被诊断，即在存在传输延迟的情况下仍能确定系统是否满足分布式可诊断性。

Conclusion: 该工作为存在传输延迟的部分可观测离散事件系统提供了分布式故障诊断的理论基础和验证框架，扩展了现有分散式诊断方法的适用范围。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [129] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了5≤m≤9范围内的弱有限增广Zarankiewicz数z_{wL}(m,3)的精确值，均等于2m，并通过显式构造给出下界、对C_4-free基图的精确有限搜索给出上界，同时为z_{wL}(m,3)=2m的猜想提供了有力支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱有限增广Zarankiewicz数在m较小范围内的精确值，验证并支持z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5成立的猜想，并为BSR(m,3)的下界提供构造性证明。

Method: 下界通过显式的弱允许构造给出；上界（对m=7,8,9）通过对正则C_4-free基图进行精确有限搜索获得，并结合已有小尺寸结果进行整体分析。

Result: 得到z_{wL}(5,3)=10, z_{wL}(6,3)=12, z_{wL}(7,3)=14, z_{wL}(8,3)=16, z_{wL}(9,3)=18，即z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对所有5≤m≤9成立；并推出BSR(m,3)≥2m对这些m成立。

Conclusion: 这些结果与已知小尺寸情形一致，强烈支持z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5成立的猜想，并给出了BSR(m,3)≥2m的新下界。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [130] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题上的收敛行为，重点剖析了同样本SEG（S-SEG）与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）的本质差异，包括S-SEG对样本wise Lipschitz参数的敏感性、无界域上的高概率受限间隙收敛性，以及非对称双步长策略在S-SEG上的失效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外梯度算法在确定性单调变分不等式问题中的收敛理论已十分成熟，但其随机版本（尤其是同样本变体S-SEG）的理论理解仍存在明显空白。已有研究多聚焦于独立样本SEG（I-SEG），且通常要求域紧致或随机算子方差一致有界。本文旨在填补这些理论空白，深入理解S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛行为上的实质差异。

Method: 本文首先通过反例证明S-SEG对样本wise Lipschitz参数敏感，仅满足均值Lipschitz性和有界方差在紧致集上也不足以保证收敛。随后，在放宽的假设条件下（允许无界域），对两种SEG变体建立高概率受限间隙收敛结果，并证明这些结果在一般情况下无法根本改进。最后通过构造具体的随机单调VIP反例，证明已知可保证I-SEG几乎必然末次迭代收敛的非对称双步长策略在S-SEG上会失效（几乎必然发散）。

Result: (1) 证明S-SEG在紧致集上即使满足均值Lipschitz和有界方差也可能不收敛；(2) 在更宽松的假设下（允许无界域），为I-SEG和S-SEG建立了高概率受限间隙收敛界，并给出这些界的下界说明其最优性；(3) 构造反例表明非对称双步长策略对S-SEG无效，S-SEG在该策略下几乎必然发散。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了S-SEG与I-SEG在理论性质上的根本差异：S-SEG对样本结构更敏感、收敛条件更苛刻，且某些对I-SEG成立的加速或步长策略在S-SEG上不适用。这为理解随机外梯度算法的收敛理论提供了更全面的视角，也对实际应用中SEG变体的选择与步长设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [131] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从边际概率密度观测推断广义常微分方程组动力学的随机梯度方法，将不可逆随机过程转化为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆耗散流，利用特征线表示替代高维Liouville方程求解，并通过交叉U统计量构建无偏梯度估计，最终用于随机梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中我们通常只能观测到低维状态的边际概率密度函数，而底层动力学可能是不可逆或随机的（如扩散过程）。这些过程在低维观测下看起来是随机的，但若能够将观测与隐变量联合考虑，则可以转化为确定性的可逆流。因此需要一种方法，能够从边际观测推断底层确定性动力学的参数。

Method: 本文方法的核心在于：1) 将扩散和其他不可逆过程重写为增广状态空间上的确定性可逆流，联合密度满足双曲型Liouville方程；2) 利用特征线表示而非直接求解高维Liouville方程，粒子沿特征线从初始分布传输；3) 沿特征线传播参数灵敏度（Eulerian敏感性），使用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器；4) 结合随机梯度下降迭代优化参数。

Result: 通过四个实验验证方法有效性：三模态线性系统（从单模态边际观测恢复）；非线性Gompertz增长模型（含隐模态）；双稳态系统（隐模态使单峰边际变为双峰）；Stokes-Oseen阻力定律恢复（细胞流中粒子）。各实验均分析了收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将不可逆/随机动力学的推断问题转化为从边际观测恢复确定性ODE的问题，通过特征线表示与无偏梯度估计，实现了对广义ODE系统参数的可靠推断，并在多种复杂动力学场景中验证了有效性。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [132] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形（正交列矩阵流形）上的应用，发现其更新存在精确闭式解，据此提出Skewon算法，并提供一阶收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon作为矩阵感知优化方法在Stiefel流形上的现有扩展依赖启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率各异且缺乏精确性，需要更高效且理论上可靠的方法。

Method: 作者证明了Stiefel流形上的Muon更新存在精确闭式解，并基于此推导出Skewon算法，实现了高效的orthogonality-constrained优化实现，同时在光滑非凸环境下建立了Skewon的一阶收敛性保证。

Result: 所提出的Skewon算法相比现有Stiefel流形上的Muon扩展具有更高效的实现（得益于闭式解），并具有一阶收敛性保证。

Conclusion: Skewon为Stiefel流形上的正交约束优化提供了精确、高效且具有收敛性保证的实用算法。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [133] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界域上多维连续性方程（含源项）通过局部内通量反馈的镇定问题。反馈通过通量散度指定，使得误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程。在有限时间特征阻尼不等式的几何条件下，结合增益条件（局部阻尼与压缩放大之间的关系），证明误差在L2中指数稳定。Lyapunov型入口条件确保特征阻尼，加权Lyapunov泛函给出微分Lyapunov准则和对加性扰动的输入到状态（ISS）估计。文章还讨论了反馈通量的椭圆右逆实现，并将特征阻尼论证扩展至状态非线性依赖的速度场，最后用二维例子说明几何、增益和实现条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多维有界域上连续性方程（含源项）的镇定问题，传统方法通常依赖全局或边界控制，而本文旨在通过局部内部的通量反馈实现镇定，以降低控制实现的物理限制和工程代价。此外，需要处理压缩放大效应与阻尼之间的权衡，以及非线性速度场的扩展需求。

Method: 1) 通过通量散度指定局部内反馈，将误差方程化为带局部阻尼的输运方程；2) 提出有限时间特征阻尼不等式作为核心几何条件，要求特征线在时间范围上积累均匀阻尼；3) 结合增益条件（阻尼与压缩放大关系）证明L2指数稳定性；4) 引入Lyapunov型入口条件以在支持受限轨迹族上确保特征阻尼；5) 构造加权Lyapunov泛函，提供微分Lyapunov准则和ISS估计；6) 讨论通量反馈的椭圆右逆实现；7) 将论证推广至状态非线性依赖的速度场。

Result: 在有限时间特征阻尼不等式和适当增益条件下，证明了误差在L2范数意义下的指数稳定性；建立了加权Lyapunov泛函框架下的微分Lyapunov准则，并给出了对加性扰动的输入到状态（ISS）估计；实现了反馈通量的椭圆右逆构造，并将特征阻尼论证扩展到非线性速度场；通过二维数值例子验证了几何条件、增益条件和实现条件的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文为多维连续性方程在局部内通量反馈下的镇定提供了一套完整的理论框架，涵盖几何条件、稳定性和鲁棒性分析以及反馈通量的实际构造。该工作为输运方程族的分布式控制提供了新的Lyapunov工具和ISS分析方法，并可推广至非线性情形。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [134] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散事件系统中观测延迟情况下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了两种针对延迟观测的诊断与预测条件，并指出现有"可预测性(prognosability)"概念需修订以避免保守性。通过引入延迟观测器和新的验证函数来验证诊断性与可预测性条件，理论分析证明了该验证方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际离散事件系统中，观测数据往往存在延迟到达的情况，而现有故障诊断与预测的理论框架通常假设观测即时可获得。这种假设与实际情况不符，导致现有方法在延迟观测场景下可能过于保守（即无法充分利用可用信息），因此需要针对延迟观测场景重新审视和修订诊断与预测的条件。

Method: 论文提出两种针对延迟观测的故障诊断与预测条件，其中关键在于修订了已有的可预测性(prognosability)概念以避免保守性。为验证这些条件，作者引入了延迟观测器(delay observer)来建模观测延迟的影响，并设计了一个新的验证函数来检测系统是否满足诊断性和可预测性要求。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法能够有效处理观测延迟下的故障诊断与预测问题，验证了修订后的诊断性与可预测性条件在延迟观测场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了离散事件系统在延迟观测场景下故障诊断与预测的理论空白，通过修订可预测性概念并引入延迟观测器与新的验证函数，为实际系统中观测延迟情况下的故障处理提供了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [135] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在电力系统规划中采用集成方法，将稳定措施（惯量和电压稳定约束）与发电、输电和储能扩展同时考虑，结果表明集成方法可降低系统成本，并倾向于使用电网形成型电池储能系统的双重用途而非STATCOM等其他稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器型资源（IBR）在电力系统中渗透率不断提高，需要替代同步发电机在动态电压稳定性和惯量响应方面的贡献。然而，传统的电力系统规划方法是顺序式的，稳定措施仅在规划后期才被考虑，这种分离式方法可能无法实现最优规划方案。

Method: 提出一种集成的电力系统规划方法，将稳定措施（通过简化的惯量和电压稳定性约束表示）与发电、输电和储能系统的扩展同时进行优化，并与传统的顺序规划方法进行比较分析。

Result: 研究发现：（1）集成方法相比顺序方法能够降低系统成本；（2）电网形成型电池储能系统的双重用途（同时提供惯量和电压支撑）优于静态同步补偿器（STATCOM）等其他稳定措施。

Conclusion: 集成规划方法在处理高比例逆变器型资源电力系统的动态稳定问题时具有成本优势，同时电网形成型电池储能系统的双重用途功能应作为优先考虑的稳定措施。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>
