### [136] [On a two-dimensional Camassa-Holm-Zakharov-Kuznetsov equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05334)
*Xixuan Li,Kai Yan,Tiantian Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种名为Camassa-Holm-Zakharov-Kuznetsov（CH-ZK）方程的新的二维非线性色散波模型，该方程结合了Camassa-Holm方程的非线性结构与Zakharov-Kuznetsov方程的横向拉普拉斯色散。论文建立了该方程Cauchy问题在合适Sobolev空间中的局部适定性，推导了强解的破裂准则，构造了有限时间破裂的强解，证明了解的独特延拓性质，并研究了尖峰孤立波和平滑孤立波的存在性，同时根据波速大小获得了行波解的刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提出并系统研究一个新的二维非线性色散波模型——CH-ZK方程，该方程融合了Camassa-Holm方程（描述一维浅水波中的尖峰孤立波现象）与Zakharov-Kuznetsov方程（二维色散波方程）的结构优势，以探索二维情形下尖峰孤立波及相关的数学性质。

Method: 主要采用偏微分方程分析的方法：在合适Sobolev空间中通过半群理论或能量方法建立Cauchy问题的局部适定性；利用特征线方法或能量估计推导强解的破裂准则；通过构造适当的初值条件得到有限时间破裂的强解；运用乘积估计和连续延拓原理证明独特延拓性质；并通过行波解的常微分方程分析和变分方法研究尖峰与平滑孤立波的存在性及刚性定理。

Result: 成功建立了CH-ZK方程在合适Sobolev空间中的局部适定性；得到了强解的破裂准则并构造了有限时间破裂的强解；证明了CH-ZK方程解的独特延拓性质；证实了尖峰和平滑孤立波的存在性；根据波速大小获得了行波解的刚性定理。

Conclusion: 本文对CH-ZK方程的数学理论进行了系统研究，涵盖了适定性、破裂现象、独特延拓性质以及孤立波存在性与行波解的刚性分类等方面，为二维非线性色散波方程的理论分析提供了新的成果和工具，也为后续研究该方程的动力学行为奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to a new two-dimensional nonlinear dispersive wave model named as the Camassa-Holm-Zakharov-Kuznetsov (CH-ZK) equation which combines the nonlinear structure of the Camassa-Holm equation with the transverse Laplacian dispersion of the Zakharov-Kuznetsov equation. We first establish the local well-posedness of its Cauchy problem in a suitable Sobolev space and derive a blow-up criterion for strong solutions. Then the finite-time blow-up strong solutions for the CH-ZK equation have been constructed. Moreover, we prove a unique continuation property for the solutions to the CH-ZK equation. Finally, we investigate the existence of both peaked and smooth solitary waves, and obtain a rigidity theorem for the traveling wave solutions according to the magnitude of wave speed.

</details>


### [137] [Unstable Manifolds of Stratified Euler Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05370)
*Zhiwu Lin,Yanbo Wang,Chongchun Zeng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了不可压缩分层Euler方程在谱不稳定稳态附近的局部稳定与不稳定流形的存在性。在线性化方程具有指数二分且谱间隙相对背景稳态流动最大Lyapunov指数足够大的假设下，通过Lyapunov–Perron方法在无穷维体积保持Lagrangian映射流形上构造了稳定与不稳定流形，并讨论了在二维稳态流动中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可压缩分层Euler方程的稳态流动在谱意义下可能不稳定，但现有的非线性稳定性理论难以处理这类情形。作者旨在建立谱不稳定性与非线性动力学之间的桥梁，通过构造局部稳定/不稳定流形来理解稳态在非线性层面对扰动的响应结构，从而为分层流体的动力学行为提供更完整的理论刻画。

Method: 核心方法是将Euler方程重新表述为定义在体积保持Lagrangian映射的无穷维流形上的ODE，其中密度被视为冻结的Lagrangian参数，同时也是L^2度量中的权重。在此基础上，利用Lyapunov–Perron方法构造稳定与不稳定流形，其关键假设是线性化方程具有指数二分，且其谱间隙相对于背景稳态流动的最大Lyapunov指数足够大。

Result: 在d维一般域上证明了谱不稳定稳态的局部稳定与不稳定流形的存在性，并将结果应用于二维稳态流动的具体情形。

Conclusion: 该工作为分层Euler方程的非线性动力系统分析提供了新的工具，揭示了谱不稳定性与非线性流形结构之间的联系，并为进一步研究湍流转捩或长时间动力学行为奠定了基础。

Abstract: We consider a spectrally unstable steady state $(ρ_0,v_0)$ of the incompressible stratified Euler equations on a class of $d$-dimensional domains. Assuming that the linearized equation admits an exponential dichotomy with a reasonably large spectral gap relative to the maximal Lyapunov exponent of the background steady flow $v_0$, we construct the local stable and unstable manifolds of $(ρ_0,v_0)$. The proof is based on the Lyapunov--Perron method after reformulating the Euler equation as an ODE on the infinite-dimensional manifold of volume-preserving Lagrangian maps, with the density treated as a frozen Lagrangian parameter as well as the weight in the $L^2$ metric. We also discuss some applications to two-dimensional steady flows.

</details>


### [138] [Sharp $H^2$-regularity in dimensions $N\geq 5$ and beyond for two classes of elliptic problems with critical unbounded coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05383)
*Cristian Cazacu,Adelina Călina*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了N≥5维空间中两类带临界无界扰动的椭圆问题在H₀¹中弱解的H²正则性，并建立了关于参数λ的尖锐阈值。第一类问题(-Δv+λ(x·∇v)/|x|²=f)对所有λ<(N-2)/2都成立H²正则性；第二类问题(-Δv+λv/|x|²=f)在λ>-N(N-4)/4时成立H²正则性，而在区间(-(N-2)²/4, -N(N-4)/4]上失败。文中还发展了所需的尖锐二阶Hardy-Rellich型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Kim和Tsai仅在λ≤0时证明了第一类问题的H²正则性，而Kato扰动理论仅保证第二类问题在λ∈(-N(N-4)/4, N(N-4)/4)范围内的H²正则性。本文旨在完全确定这两类带临界奇异扰动的椭圆问题H²正则性成立的精确参数范围。

Method: 利用Hardy不等式确保问题在H₀¹中的适定性；针对两类含奇异项（x·∇v/|x|²和v/|x|²）的算子，发展尖锐的二阶Hardy-Rellich型不等式作为核心工具，从而建立H²正则性的充分和必要条件。

Result: (1) 对第一类问题，对任意λ<(N-2)/2且f∈L²(Ω)，任意v∈H₀¹(Ω)均属于H²(Ω)，完全推广了Kim-Tsai在λ≤0时的结果；(2) 对第二类问题，H²正则性在λ>-N(N-4)/4时成立，在λ∈(-(N-2)²/4, -N(N-4)/4]时失败，尖锐扩展了Kato扰动理论给出的范围。

Conclusion: 本文给出了两类含临界无界扰动椭圆问题H²正则性的精确参数阈值，其中第一类问题在所有适定参数范围内均保持H²正则性，而第二类问题存在一个临界断裂点λ=-N(N-4)/4，越过该阈值正则性失效。这揭示了不同奇异扰动的结构差异对正则性的本质影响，并提供了相应的最优Hardy-Rellich型不等式。

Abstract: We establish sharp parameter thresholds governing $H^2$ -regularity of weak solutions in $H_0^1$ for two classes of elliptic problems with critical unbounded perturbations, in dimensions $N\geq 5$. More precisely, we consider two distinct $λ$-parametric elliptic problems $ - Δv + λ\frac{x\cdot \nabla v}{|x|^{2}} =f$ and $-Δv + λ\frac{v}{|x|^2}=f$ posed in a bounded $C^2$-domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^N$ containing the origin $x=0$. We observe that the singular perturbations $\frac{x\cdot \nabla v}{|x|^{2}}$ and $\frac{v}{|x|^2}$ are homogeneous operators of order 2 consistent with the scaling of the Laplacian. In view of the Hardy inequality the problems are well-posed in $H_0^1(Ω)$ for $λ<\frac{N-2}{2}$ and $λ>-\frac{(N-2)^2}{4}$ respectively.
  The main results are as follows. For the first problem we show that any solution $v\in H_0^1(Ω)$ belongs to $H^2(Ω)$ for any $λ< \frac{N-2}{2}$ provided $f\in L^2(Ω)$. This fully extends the previous $H^2$ regularity properties obtained by Kim and Tsai in \cite{Kim-Tsai} for $λ\leq 0$. For the second problem we show that $H^2$ regularity holds for any $λ>- \frac{N(N-4)}{4}$ and fails for any $λ\in \left(-\frac{(N-2)^2}{4},-\frac{N(N-4)}{4}\right]$. This extends sharply the range of $λ\in \left(-\frac{N(N-4)}{4}, \frac{N(N-4)}{4}\right)$ obtained when applying the Kato perturbation theory in \cite{Kato}. In addition, we develop sharp second order Hardy-Rellich type inequalities for the involved elliptic operators which are essential in the above proofs.

</details>


### [139] [Time-periodic solutions of the Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann system with a general external force in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05637)
*Renjun Duan,Jinkai Ni*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维全空间中Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann（VPB）系统在给定时间周期非势外力下的时间周期问题。在全局Maxwellian附近，通过构建融合低频Besov框架与自洽电场控制的混合函数空间，证明了小解的全局存在性、稳定性以及时间周期解的存在与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPB系统在等离子体物理和气体动力学中具有重要应用，但外力为时间周期非势场时的周期解问题尚未被系统解决。现有方法将非线性Vlasov力视为一般源项处理，无法充分利用VPB系统内部的结构性消去关系，导致无法在低频区域建立封闭的先验估计。

Method: 本文将解空间构建为低频Besov框架（用于受迫Boltzmann方程）与自洽电场控制相结合的混合函数空间。核心创新在于利用Poisson方程和宏观平衡定律恢复VPB半群估计所需的结构消去，而非将非线性Vlasov力-∇_xφ·∇_vf + (1/2)(v·∇_xφ)f简单视为源项。结合高频能量估计与加权微观传播，获得封闭的全局适定性理论。对时间周期外力，采用Serrin方法构造唯一周期解并证明稳定性。

Result: 证明了VPB系统在时间周期非势外力下小解的全局存在性与渐近稳定性；对时间周期外力，通过Serrin方法得到具有相同周期的唯一时间周期解及其稳定性；作为直接推论，当外力与时间无关时，给出了稳态解的存在性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过充分利用VPB系统中Poisson方程与宏观平衡律提供的结构消去性质，克服了低频区域Vlasov力处理的困难，建立了时间周期问题的完整全局适定性理论，并统一覆盖了时间周期外力与时间独立外力两种情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the time-periodic problem for the Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann (VPB) system with a given time-periodic external force in the whole space $\mathbb{R}^3$. The force is allowed to be non-potential. Around the global Maxwellian, we prove the global existence of small solutions in a hybrid function space that combines the low-frequency Besov framework for the forced Boltzmann equation with a corresponding control of the self-consistent electric field. The main novelty lies in the treatment of the nonlinear Vlasov force $-\nabla_xφ\cdot \nabla_vf + \frac{1}{2}(v \cdot \nabla_xφ)f$ at low frequencies. Rather than treating it as a generic source term, we exploit the Poisson equation and macroscopic balance laws to recover the structural cancellation required for the VPB semi-group estimates, which combined with high-frequency energy estimates and weighted microscopic propagation, yields a closed global well-posedness theory. We further prove the asymptotic stability of small solutions driven by the same force. When the external force is time-periodic, Serrin's method yields a unique time-periodic solution with the same period, together with its stability. As a direct consequence, our result also gives the existence and stability of stationary solutions when the external force is time-independent.

</details>


### [140] [Truncations for fractional Laplacians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05433)
*Egor Ignatev,Alexander I. Nazarov,Pavel Nichitenko,Artur Tursunbaev*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明并广泛推广了A.I. Nazarov的猜想：在有界Lipschitz域Ω上，对于s∈(1,3/2)，谱分数阶Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子的二次型Q_s^SP[u]在映射u→|u|下严格递增，前提是u∈H̃^s(Ω)且在Ω中变号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nazarov提出了一个猜想，即谱分数阶Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子的二次型在取绝对值映射下具有严格单调递增的性质。该猜想对于理解分数阶Sobolev空间中变号函数的变分性质具有重要意义，本文旨在证明并推广这一猜想。

Method: 本文针对有界Lipschitz域Ω，考虑谱分数阶Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子的二次型Q_s^SP[u]，在s∈(1,3/2)的条件下，分析u→|u|映射下该二次型的严格单调性。严格性建立在函数u∈H̃^s(Ω)变号的前提上。

Result: 成功证明了Nazarov关于谱分数阶Dirichlet拉普拉斯二次型在绝对值映射下严格递增的猜想，并将其结果推广到更广泛的情形，参数范围覆盖s∈(1,3/2)。

Conclusion: 本文完整地证明并推广了Nazarov猜想，揭示了在s∈(1,3/2)范围内，谱分数阶Dirichlet拉普拉斯二次型对变号函数取绝对值后严格递增的性质，为分数阶Sobolev空间中的变分分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^n$ be a bounded Lipschitz domain. We prove and widely generalize a conjecture of A.\,I.~Nazarov \cite{Naz21}: for $s\in(1,\frac 32)$ the quadratic form $Q^{\rm SP}_s[u]$ of the spectral fractional Dirichlet Laplacian strictly increases under the map $u\mapsto|u|$ provided $u\in\tilde H^s(Ω)$ changes sign in $Ω$.

</details>


### [141] [Analysis of a coupled magneto-quasistatic--mechanical model of an eddy current brake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05606)
*Anna Fischer,Kaja Krhač,Timo Reis,Alexander Wierzba*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究涡流制动器的耦合磁准静态模型，该模型产生非线性无穷维微分代数系统，其中非线性源于机电反馈闭环而非材料非线性。作者引入有限耗散解概念，并证明了弱解的全局存在性与唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡流制动器是一个典型的机电耦合系统，其数学模型涉及磁场与旋转运动的相互作用，构成封闭的机电反馈回路。这样一个系统的数学分析（尤其是弱解的全局存在性和唯一性）此前尚未被充分研究。

Method: 将模型表述为角动量与磁场耦合的非线性无穷维微分代数系统；引入有限耗散（finite-dissipation）解概念来处理结构非线性；对初始数据（磁场与角动量）和输入（线圈电流及外部扭矩）给出弱解的数学框架。

Result: 证明了在给定初始数据（磁场、角动量）和输入（线圈电流、外部扭矩）条件下，该模型弱解的全局存在性与唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作为涡流制动器这一由结构反馈而非材料非线性驱动的耦合系统提供了严格的数学理论基础，确保其解的存在性和唯一性。

Abstract: We study a coupled magneto-quasistatic model for an eddy current brake. The model leads to a nonlinear infinite-dimensional differential-algebraic system in which the nonlinearity is structural rather than caused by nonlinear material laws. It is generated by the closed electromechanical feedback loop: the angular momentum determines the velocity of the conducting disk, the velocity and the magnetic field generate motional eddy currents, and these currents induce a Lorentz torque acting back on the rotor. We introduce a finite-dissipation solution concept and prove global existence and uniqueness of weak solutions for initial data given by the magnetic field and the angular momentum, and for inputs given by the supplied coil current and the externally applied torque.

</details>


### [142] [On generalised d'Alembert-type integral representations for damped wave equations on the quarter-plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06355)
*Andreas Chatziafratis,Claudio Giorgi,Alain Miranville,Federico Zullo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文首次将Fokas统一变换方法（联合变换方法）推广到半无限区间上的双曲-抛物型问题，严格构造并验证了受迫Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte方程（阻尼波动方程）在时空四分之一平面上的新闭式解，并建立了正则性、边界、渐近性质及适定性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte方程在连续介质力学、数学物理、生物学等领域有广泛建模应用，但其在半无限区间上的一般初边值问题缺乏严格的后验闭式解。Fokas统一变换方法此前仅适用于椭圆型偏微分方程及具有多项式色散关系的演化方程，尚未被推广到双曲-抛物型混合问题，这构成了方法论的空白。

Method: 作者将Fokas的单性变换方法（unified transform method）扩展到半无限区间上的双曲-抛物型问题。通过复分析中的Riemann-Hilbert型分析，构造了包含振荡积分项的显式积分表示解；同时处理了半无界域带来的解析挑战，包括振荡积分的适当解释，并通过在原点处的相容性条件（初始、边界、强迫数据之间）保证了全域光滑解的存在性。

Result: 建立了关于新解析公式的正则性、边界和渐近性质定理，以及所涉边值问题的适定性定理。揭示了保证全域光滑解存在的初始-边界-强迫数据相容性条件，并给出了具有直接数值基准测试效用的显式积分表示。

Conclusion: 所获得的闭式积分表示不仅为数值基准测试和多个学科方向（连续介质力学、数学物理、生物学、自然科学）的建模探索提供直接工具，也为非线性推广问题的适定性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We rigorously construct and verify a posteriori new closed-form solutions for the forced Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte equation (also broadly known as the damped wave equation, hyperbolic heat, and telegrapher's equation on lossy transmission lines) posed on the spatiotemporal quarter-plane with general initial and boundary data in classical function spaces. For this purpose, the modern complex-analytic unified transform method of Fokas (originally developed for elliptic PDE and evolution equations with polynomial dispersion relations) is here, for the first time, extended for analysis of hyperbolic-parabolic problems on the semi-infinite interval. Importantly, we then establish theorems which pertain to regularity, boundary and asymptotic properties of the new analytical formulae as well as to well-posedness of the addressed boundary-value problems. Notably, the nature and generality of problems considered, combined with the semi-unboundedness of the domain, induce substantial analytic challenges which demand delicate treatment, both in appropriately interpreting oscillatory integral terms of the solution formulae and in proving the proposed results. In this process, crucially, certain compatibility conditions, between initial, boundary and forcing data at the origin, are revealed, which guarantee the existence of a smooth solution across the whole domain of interest. Our explicit integral representations are of direct utility for numercal benchmarking purposes, for exploring connections with modelling in continuum mechanics, mathematical physics, biology and the natural sciences, and for the investigation of well-posedness for nonlinear counterparts too.

</details>


### [143] [On dynamic stability of energetically stable equilibria of the Navier-Stokes-Korteweg flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05654)
*Yoshikazu Giga,Naoto Kajiwara,Kazuyuki Tsuda*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界域或周期胞腔中Navier-Stokes-Korteweg方程的稳定性问题。在压力关于密度非单调（允许两相共存、存在非恒定平衡态）的条件下，利用简单Hilbert空间框架证明了：当空间维数不超过3时，能量稳定且非退化的孤立平衡态在等温Navier-Stokes-Korteweg流下是指数稳定的；对于非孤立情形，处于能量稳定平衡态附近的全局解会指数收敛到（可能是另一个）平衡态。证明不要求平衡态具有小性假设，采用Prüss-Wilke-Simonett（2013）的（广义）稳定性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes-Korteweg方程用于描述两相流（如液-气相变）的动力学行为。在实际物理场景中，压力关于密度可能非单调，从而允许非恒定平衡态的存在，这使得经典的稳定性分析面临困难。作者希望在尽量少的假设下（不要求平衡态小性）建立能量稳定平衡态的指数稳定性，填补现有理论空白。

Method: 该方法采用简单的Hilbert空间框架，利用J. Prüss, M. Wilke和G. Simonett（2013）提出的（广义）稳定性原理。该原理将指数稳定性问题归结为验证线性化算子的谱性质（如能谱间隙条件）以及非线性项满足适当的Lipschitz/高阶估计。通过将能量稳定性与算子的谱条件联系起来，避免了以往对平衡态小性的限制。对于非孤立平衡情形，通过全局解在能量稳定平衡态附近的收敛性分析扩展了结果。

Result: 在空间维数d≤3的条件下，得到了：(1) 能量稳定且非退化的孤立平衡态在等温Navier-Stokes-Korteweg流下是指数稳定的；(2) 对于非孤立情形，位于能量稳定平衡态附近的全局解以指数速率收敛到（可能是另一个）平衡态。所有结论均不要求对平衡态势加小性假设。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了两相Navier-Stokes-Korteweg方程能量稳定平衡态的指数稳定性理论，覆盖了孤立与非孤立两种情况，且不要求小性假设，为两相流的动力学稳定性分析提供了统一的Hilbert空间框架。

Abstract: We consider the Navier-Stokes-Korteweg equations in a bounded domain or a periodic cell. The pressure considered in this paper may not be monotone with respect to the density so that there exist non-constant equilibria allowing two-phases. Using a simple Hilbert space framework, we prove that if an isolated equilibrium is energetically stable and non- degenerate, it is exponentially stable under the isothermal Navier-Stokes-Korteweg flows when the space dimension is less than or equal to three. For non-isolated case, we prove that a global-in-time solution near an energetically stable equilibrium converges to possibly another equilibrium exponentially fast. No smallness assumptions on equilibria are imposed. For the proof we apply a (generalized) stability principle due to J. Prüss, M. Wilke and G. Simonett (2013).

</details>


### [144] [Periodic Solutions to the Steady Viscous Burgers Equation: A Constructive Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05677)
*Quansen Jiu,Yuhan Cao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带有周期外力的稳态粘性Burgers方程的周期问题，旨在通过直接Fourier级数方法构造关于粘度一致有界的周期解。作者首先以无粘Burgers方程的一个特解为起点，显式求解粘性Burgers方程的近似解；其次通过求解二阶常微分方程的初值问题来构造周期解，保证其对粘度一致有界。与Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser的结果相比，该方法提供了显式构造过程，并在粘度趋于零时获得更优（更高阶）的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造稳态粘性Burgers方程在周期外力下的周期解，并要求这些解关于粘度参数一致有界，同时提供比现有方法（Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser）更显式且收敛更快的结果。

Method: 采用直接Fourier级数方法。具体分为两步：(1) 从无粘Burgers方程的一个特殊解出发，显式求解粘性方程的近似解；(2) 通过求解一个二阶常微分方程的初值问题，构造周期问题的解，并证明其对粘度一致有界。

Result: 成功构造了粘性Burgers方程周期问题的显式周期解，该解关于粘度一致有界；与Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser的结果相比，收敛率在粘度趋于零时更高（达到更高阶精度）。

Conclusion: 该Fourier级数方法为粘性Burgers方程的周期解提供了显式且更高效的构造途径，在粘度消失极限下具有更优的收敛性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the periodic problem to steady viscous Burgers equation with an periodic and external force. Our main aim is to construct periodic solutions to this model which are uniformly bounded with respect to the viscosity via a direct Fourier series approach. Firstly, as an example, starting from a special solution of the non-viscous Burgers equation with a specific force, we solve the approximate solutions to the viscous Burgers equation in an explicit way. Secondly, we construct the solution to the periodic problem of the viscous Burgers equation with the external force by solving the initial value problem to a second ordinary differential equations, which is uniformly bounded with respect to the viscosity. Compared with Jauslin-Kreiss-Moser's result, our approach provides an explicit constructive procedure for the periodic solutions and yields a sharper convergence rate (up to higher order) when the viscosity vanishes.

</details>


### [145] [Local well-posedness of the boundary layer for a pseudo-plastic fluid by energy methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05693)
*Zhonger Wu,Zhong Tan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过能量方法，在Oleinik单调性假设下研究了伪塑性流体边界层的适定性。针对导数损失和粘性引起的困难，分别借用Masmoudi和Wong的抵消机制，并结合单调性假设与Faà di Bruno公式实现对粘性高阶导数的精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪塑性流体的边界层问题在数学上具有挑战性，主要面临导数损失和粘性带来的两大困难。研究其适定性是理解该流体动力学行为的基础，但现有方法难以直接适用，需要新的技术手段。

Method: 采用能量方法研究边界层适定性。对导数损失困难，借用Masmoudi和Wong（CPAM, 2015）提出的抵消机制；对粘性困难，将Oleinik单调性假设与Faà di Bruno公式相结合，对粘性函数的高阶导数获得精确控制。

Result: 在Oleinik单调性假设下，建立了伪塑性流体边界层的适定性结果，其中对粘性高阶导数的精确控制是本文的主要贡献。

Conclusion: 本文通过将抵消机制与基于单调性假设和Faà di Bruno公式的粘性高阶导数控制相结合，成功解决了伪塑性流体边界层适定性中的两大核心困难，为该类问题的后续研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the well-posedness of the boundary layer for a pseudo-plastic fluid by energy methods under Oleinik's monotonicity assumption. We need to address two main difficulties: derivative loss and the difficulty caused by viscosity. For the first difficulty, we borrow the cancellation mechanism proposed by Masmoudi and Wong [CPAM, 2015]. For the second difficulty, we combine the monotonicity assumption and Faà di Bruno formula to obtain precise control over the high-order derivatives of viscosity, which is the main contribution of this paper.

</details>


### [146] [Mixed local and nonlocal weighted singular quasilinear elliptic problem and its associated Sobolev-type inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05696)
*Prashanta Garain*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一类混合各向异性非局部奇异拟线性椭圆问题，其中涉及Muckenhoupt权重。作者利用单调逼近、加权Sobolev嵌入、紧致性和变分方法，在适当数据假设下建立了弱解的存在性和唯一性，并刻画了相关加权混合各向异性非局部Sobolev型不等式的最佳常数，证明其可达且归一化弱解是唯一极值。这些结果即使在p=2的混合加权Laplace情形下也是全新的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题中奇异非线性在原点附近爆破，且与各向异性、权重退化及非局部扩散相互作用，产生了显著的分析困难。作者旨在克服这些挑战，建立存在唯一性结果并刻画最佳常数。

Method: 结合单调逼近、加权Sobolev嵌入、紧致性论证和变分方法，在合适的数值假设下证明弱解的存在性和唯一性；同时利用变分方法刻画加权混合各向异性非局部Sobolev型不等式的最佳常数并证明其可达性。

Result: 在适当的数据假设下建立了弱解的存在性与唯一性；刻画了相关Sobolev型不等式的最佳常数，证明了该常数可被达到，且归一化弱解是唯一极值。这些结果在混合加权Laplace特殊情形(p=2)下亦为新结论。

Conclusion: 该工作成功解决了带Muckenhoupt权重的混合各向异性非局部奇异椭圆问题的存在唯一性及最佳常数问题，填补了相关领域的空白，即使退化到p=2的混合加权Laplace情形也具有新颖性。

Abstract: We consider a class of mixed anisotropic and nonlocal singular quasilinear elliptic problem associated with Muckenhoupt weights. The presence of the singular nonlinearity, which blows up near the origin along with its interaction with anisotropy, weighted degeneracy, and nonlocal diffusion creates significant analytical challenges. We employ monotone approximation, weighted Sobolev embeddings, compactness, and variational methods to establish the existence and uniqueness of weak solutions under suitable assumptions on the datum. Further, we characterize the best constant in an associated weighted mixed anisotropic and nonlocal Sobolev-type inequality, prove that it is attained, and show that the normalized weak solution is the unique extremal. These results are new even in the mixed weighted Laplace case \(p=2\).

</details>


### [147] [Positive Solutions for a One-Dimensional Minkowski-Curvature Equation with a Sign-Changing Nonlinearity under Mixed Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05711)
*Ao Xiao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维Minkowski曲率方程在混合边界条件和变号非线性项下正解的存在性。通过非负线性移位和Green核构造不变锥，结合主特征值比较和不动点指数方法获得锥的扩张与压缩，并利用全局辅助方程和首触论证证明解的斜率小于1，从而满足原方程，还给出了可直接验证的渐近判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Minkowski曲率方程在微分几何和广义相对论中具有重要应用，混合边界条件下的变号非线性问题尚未被充分研究，需要建立正解存在性理论。

Method: 采用非负线性移位将变号非线性项转化为可处理的框架；利用相关线性问题的Green核构造不变锥；通过主特征值比较和不动点指数方法证明锥在零点附近扩张、在无穷远处压缩；构造全局定义的辅助方程并使用首触（first-contact）论证确保解满足斜率小于1的约束条件。

Result: 成功建立了该Minkowski曲率方程正解的存在性，并导出了一个可直接验证的渐近判据。

Conclusion: 通过结合不变锥、不动点指数和首触论证等多种技术，解决了混合边界条件下变号非线性Minkowski曲率方程正解的存在性问题，并提供了实用的渐近判定条件。

Abstract: We establish the existence of a positive solution for a one-dimensional Minkowski-curvature equation with mixed boundary conditions and a sign-changing nonlinearity. A nonnegative linear shift and the Green kernel of the associated linear problem produce an invariant cone. Principal-eigenvalue comparisons and the fixed point index give cone expansion near zero and compression at infinity. A globally defined auxiliary equation and a first-contact argument show that the resulting solution has slope below one and hence solves the original equation. We also derive a directly verifiable asymptotic criterion.

</details>


### [148] [Prandtl--Batchelor and flux-expulsion selection for steady MHD flows in a disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05994)
*Chen Gao,Zhiwu Lin,Jianfeng Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了圆盘内稳态不可压缩MHD流的粘性-电阻双重消失极限，在非Alfvén条件下构造了解，证明了内部核心收敛到刚性旋转的理想MHD核心，并推导出新的MHD-Wood定律来描述核心旋转的选择机制，实现了Prandtl-Batchelor选择和磁通驱逐的完全耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在同时消失粘性和电阻率的极限下，MHD流如何从边界层过渡到理想核心，揭示速度场与磁场之间的主动耦合如何影响核心结构的选择（如Prandtl-Batchelor涡量和磁通驱逐），并推广经典运动学模型，允许磁场非弱地主动改变流动。

Method: 结合三个关键技术：一是针对周期性MHD边界层的非Alfvén强制性理论，用于处理边界层分析；二是速度场和磁场的全局匹配策略；三是针对磁边界条件调整的耦合稳定性估计。此外，还使用了一个独立的条件刚性论证，基于精确粘性恒等式和局部收敛性（无需内部渐近展开）来解释更广泛单涡族的核心结构。

Result: 在α≠0且|α|≠|β|的非Alfvén条件下，构造了收敛于刚性旋转理想MHD核心的解，该核心具有恒定涡量和面外电流密度。新的MHD-Wood定律表明：速度核心由动力-磁场耦合平衡决定，而磁核心由施加的平均环量固定。零环量导致完全内部磁驱逐，非零环量则留下均匀磁旋转，非轴对称模态被限制在薄边界层内。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Prandtl-Batchelor选择和磁通驱逐的完全耦合实现，与经典运动学模型不同，这里磁场主动改变流动且不需要很弱。结果建立了核心结构选择的严格数学框架，并通过条件刚性论证扩展到了更广泛的单涡族。

Abstract: We study the simultaneous vanishing-viscosity and vanishing-resistivity limit of steady incompressible MHD flows in a disk. The boundary velocity is a small nonaxisymmetric perturbation of a rigid rotation with mean angular speed \(α\), while the prescribed tangential magnetic trace has mean \(β\). Assuming \(α\neq0\) and the non-Alfvénic condition \(|α|\neq|β|\), we construct solutions converging on compact interior subdisks to a rigidly rotating ideal MHD core with constant vorticity and out-of-plane current density. A new MHD--Wood law determines the two core rotations from the boundary data: the velocity core is selected by a coupled kinetic--magnetic balance, whereas the magnetic core is fixed by the imposed mean circulation. Consequently, zero circulation gives complete interior magnetic expulsion, while nonzero circulation leaves a uniform magnetic rotation after the nonaxisymmetric modes are confined to a thin boundary layer. This provides a fully coupled realization of Prandtl--Batchelor selection and flux expulsion; unlike classical kinematic models, the magnetic field actively changes the flow and need not be weak. The proof combines a non-Alfvénic coercive theory for a periodic MHD boundary layer, global matching of the two fields, and a coupled stability estimate adapted to the magnetic boundary condition. A separate conditional rigidity argument, using exact viscous identities and local convergence but no interior asymptotic expansion, explains the same core structure for a broader single-eddy family.

</details>


### [149] [A note on generalized BV mean curvature flow with a critical forcing term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05760)
*Kiichi Tashiro*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明由Liu-Tonegawa(2024)得到的具有临界强制项的弱平均曲率流满足BV型面积变化公式，即该流不仅是Brakke流，也是Stuvard-Tonegawa(2024)提出的广义BV流。作者识别出Brakke流满足面积变化公式的最小条件，并以此推导了临界强制项下广义BV流灭绝时间的下界，同时概述了广义BV流紧性定理的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Liu-Tonegawa和Stuvard-Tonegawa两个关于弱平均曲率流的研究框架统一起来，证明带临界强制项的弱平均曲率流同时满足广义BV流的面积变化公式，从而在更广泛的框架下理解这类几何流的性质。

Method: 识别Brakke流满足面积变化公式所需的最小（最弱）条件，并证明满足这些条件的弱平均曲率流即为广义BV流。在此基础上利用面积变化公式推导灭绝时间的下界，并概述广义BV流紧性定理的证明思路。

Result: 证明了带临界强制项的弱平均曲率流满足BV型面积变化公式，从而既是Brakke流也是广义BV流；获得了临界强制项下广义BV流灭绝时间的下界估计，并给出了广义BV流紧性定理的证明概述。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了弱平均曲率流和广义BV流两个理论框架，为理解带临界强制项的奇异（弱）几何流提供了更完整的视角，并为后续研究灭绝时间和紧性等关键问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this note, we show that a weak mean curvature flow with critical forcing term obtained by Liu--Tonegawa (2024) satisfies the BV-type area change formula, that is, their flow is not only a Brakke flow but also a generalized BV flow, which is proposed by Stuvard--Tonegawa (2024). To establish this result, we identify minimal conditions under which a Brakke flow satisfies the area-change formula. As an application of our main theorem, we derive a lower bound for the extinction time of generalized BV flows with a critical forcing term. We also outline the proof of a compactness theorem for generalized BV flows.

</details>


### [150] [Optimization-Based Identification of Effective Coefficients for Wave Equations in Spatio-Temporal Metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05779)
*Christian Döding,Vishnu Raveendran,Barbara Verfürth*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于优化的方法，用于识别波动方程在非均匀介质中的有效系数，该方法在微观尺度趋近于零时收敛到均匀化系数，并给出了相应的数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空超材料（spatio-temporal metamaterials）等应用中，材料属性在空间和时间上均存在变化。尽管均匀化理论在微观尺度趋于零的渐近极限下提供了有效模型，但从波的传播观测中确定宏观参数仍然具有挑战性。现有优化型系数识别技术主要针对椭圆型多尺度问题，尚缺乏针对波动方程的同类方法。

Method: 引入一个优化基方法，通过最小化代价泛函来识别一个常数的有效系数。该方法适用于底层时空依赖系数未知、但解在空间和时间上的测量数据可用的场景。将优化型系数识别技术从椭圆型多尺度问题推广到波动方程。

Result: 证明在均匀化极限存在的前提下，识别出的系数随微观尺度趋于零而收敛至均匀化系数；同时建立了对应有效解向非均匀解的收敛性，包括L^2强收敛以及一阶空间和时间导数的弱收敛。数值实验验证了该方法对非周期时空非均匀介质的有效性，包括均匀化极限未知是否存在的场景。

Conclusion: 该方法为波动方程非均匀介质中的有效系数识别提供了理论保证和数值验证，可应用于非周期时空变化的介质，即使均匀化极限的存在性未知，方法仍表现出良好的性能。

Abstract: We study the identification of effective coefficients for wave equations in heterogeneous media. Such equations arise in the modeling of spatio-temporal metamaterials, where the underlying material properties exhibit variations in both space and time. While homogenization provides effective models in the asymptotic regime of vanishing microscopic scales, determining macroscopic parameters from observations of wave propagation remains challenging. We introduce an optimization-based method that identifies a constant effective coefficient by minimizing a cost functional. The approach is designed for situations in which the underlying space-time-dependent coefficient is unknown, while the solution is available in space and time by measurements. We extend optimization-based coefficient identification techniques from elliptic multiscale problems to wave equations and prove that, provided a homogenized limit exists, the identified coefficient converges to the homogenized coefficient as the microscopic scale tends to zero. Furthermore, we establish convergence of the corresponding effective solution towards the heterogeneous solution, including strong convergence in $L^2$ and weak convergence of first-order space and time derivatives. Numerical experiments further demonstrate the performance of the method for non-periodic space-time heterogeneous media, including cases for which a homogenized limit is not known to exist.

</details>


### [151] [Vanishing capillary limit for compressible Navier-Stokes-Korteweg system in a bounded interval with large initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05849)
*Jiaxin Ling,Huanyao Wen,Xinhua Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界区间上等熵可压缩Navier-Stokes-Korteweg系统的毛细数消失极限问题，通过新的三阶密度导数的一致耗散估计和修正子处理毛细项与边界效应带来的挑战，实现了任意大初始数据下全局L^∞范数的最优收敛速率，严格推导了等熵可压缩Navier-Stokes系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究等熵可压缩Navier-Stokes-Korteweg系统的毛细数消失极限，主要挑战来自毛细项和边界效应的处理，需要建立统一的耗散估计以克服这些困难。

Method: 推导了关于密度三阶导数的新的一致耗散估计，并引入适当的修正子来处理毛细项与边界效应，从而获得全局最优收敛速率。

Result: 在任意大初始数据下，获得了L^∞范数意义下全局时间的解的最优收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该工作为等熵可压缩Navier-Stokes系统在有界区间上从Navier-Stokes-Korteweg系统通过毛细消失极限提供了严格的数学推导。

Abstract: In this paper, we study vanishing capillary limit for isentropic compressible Navier-Stokes-Korteweg system in a bounded interval. The main challenges focus on the capillary term and the boundary effect. A new uniform dissipative estimate in terms of the third-order derivative of density and some correctors are derived to handle such difficulties. It leads to the optimal convergence rate of the solutions in $L^\infty$ norm globally in time with arbitrarily large initial data. This work provides a rigorous derivation of the isentropic compressible Navier-Stokes system in a bounded interval from the isentropic compressible Navier-Stokes-Korteweg system via vanishing capillary limit.

</details>


### [152] [Boundary layer analysis for the 2D chemotaxis-Navier-Stokes system with logarithmic sensitivity, Part I: Well-posedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05940)
*Hui Wang,Wendong Wang,Lingling Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是两部分工作的第一部分，研究二维半空间中趋化-纳维-斯托克斯系统在Navier滑移边界条件下的边界层收敛问题。作者对带有粘性系数ε>0的系统进行了精确渐近展开，获得了部分边界层剖面并建立了其适定性，同时克服了扩散项消失的困难，建立了超临界趋化欧拉方程（ε=0）解的局部适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究趋化-纳维-斯托克斯系统在二维半空间中的边界层收敛问题，特别是当粘性系数趋于零时，系统与趋化欧拉方程之间的极限关系。这一问题的难点在于对数奇异性和Navier滑移边界条件带来的数学挑战，以及扩散项消失后超临界趋化欧拉方程的适定性问题。

Method: 采用精确渐近展开方法对带粘性系数ε>0的趋化-纳维-斯托克斯系统进行分析，通过构造边界层剖面来捕捉边界附近的行为；对于ε=0的超临界趋化欧拉方程，通过克服扩散项消失的困难来建立局部适定性。

Result: 获得了趋化-纳维-斯托克斯系统的部分边界层剖面，建立了相应边界层剖面的适定性；同时建立了超临界趋化欧拉方程（ε=0）解的局部适定性。

Conclusion: 本文为二维半空间中趋化-纳维-斯托克斯系统在Navier滑移边界条件下的边界层收敛问题提供了理论框架，包括边界层剖面的适定性和极限方程的局部适定性，为后续第二部分工作奠定了基础。

Abstract: This is the first part of a two-part work concerning the boundary layer convergence for chemotaxis-Navier-Stokes system in a two-dimensional half-space. In this paper, we investigate the chemotacxis-Navier-Stokes system with the logarithmic singularity under Navier-slip boundary conditions. More precisely, we perform an exact asymptotic expansion for the chemotaxis Navier-Stokes system with viscous coefficient $\varepsilon>0$, and obtain partial boundary layer profiles, establishing the well-posedness of the corresponding boundary layer profiles. Specially, we also establish the local well-posedness of solutions to the supercritical chemotaxis Euler equation (with $\varepsilon=0$) by overcoming the difficulty from the disappearance of diffusion terms.

</details>


### [153] [Exponential growth and decay in the ideal induction equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05997)
*Víctor Navarro-Fernández*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在三维环面上构造了一个由三段交替分段仿射剪切组成的时间周期无散速度场。当剪切振幅足够大时，理想感应方程下所有非零无散L^p初值场都指数增长。证明通过建立时间一映射的一致锥条件，并结合bunching不等式排除非平凡无散场几乎处处位于稳定丛中。同时，对时间反演速度场（其时间一映射为原映射的逆），存在非平凡有界无散初值几乎处处取值于二维稳定丛，对应解在一切L^p中指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究快速动力学发电机机制，构造能实现无散场指数增长（快发电机）的明确速度场模型，并深入理解时间正向与反向的稳定/不稳定丛结构差异。

Method: 采用三段交替分段仿射剪切构造三维环面上的时间周期无散速度场；利用时间一映射的一致锥条件证明扩张性，结合bunching不等式排除稳定丛中的非平凡无散场；对时间反演情形分析二维稳定丛中的非平凡解。

Result: 证明了当剪切振幅足够大时，所有非零无散L^p初值在感应方程下指数增长；同时证明了时间反演速度场下存在非平凡有界无散初值使解在一切L^p中指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该工作构造了明确且解析简单的快发电机流场，揭示了时间对称性破缺下稳定丛与不稳定丛维度不一致的动力学机制，为理解磁流体中的快发电机过程提供了严格数学框架。

Abstract: We construct a divergence-free velocity field on the three-dimensional torus that is time-periodic and consists of three alternating piecewise-affine shears. For sufficiently large shear amplitude, every non-zero divergence-free initial field in $L^p$ grows exponentially under the ideal induction equation. The proof establishes a uniform cone condition for the time-one map, and combines it with a bunching inequality to rule out nontrivial divergence-free fields lying almost everywhere in the stable bundle. Additionally, we show that for the time-reversed velocity, whose time-one map is the inverse of the original one, there exist nontrivial, bounded, divergence-free initial configurations taking values almost everywhere in its two-dimensional stable bundle. The corresponding solution decays exponentially in every $L^p$.

</details>


### [154] [New decay estimates and Liouville type theorems for the 3D axisymmetric stationary Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06040)
*Wendong Wang,Guoxu Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维定常Navier–Stokes方程在柱坐标下的Liouville问题，基于径向变量r的衰减获得了两个新结果：(i) 利用新的柱几何点态Calderón–Zygmund估计，改进了Carrillo–Pan–Zhang的衰减估计；(ii) 建立新的Liouville定理方法，在无需轴对称假设的条件下，证明了D-解在速度或涡量满足特定幂次对数衰减条件时必为平凡解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维定常Navier–Stokes方程的Liouville问题（即D-解是否平凡）是公开难题，即使在轴对称情形下也未解决。已有研究（如Wang 2019、Zhao 2019）在轴对称框架下给出了一些判定准则，但缺乏非对称情形下的有效方法；此外，Carrillo–Pan–Zhang（2020）的衰减估计仍有改进空间。本文旨在突破轴对称限制并改进衰减估计。

Method: (i) 建立了一个适配柱坐标几何的新的点态Calderón–Zygmund估计，据此对速度梯度和涡量的径向衰减率进行精细化改进。(ii) 发展了一种新的Liouville定理证明途径，不依赖轴对称假设，仅通过柱面|x'|=r上的速度或涡量上界的衰减条件来判定D-解为平凡解。

Result: (i) 证明了|∇u_r|+|∇u_z|≲r^{-5/4}[\log(e+r)]^{5/4}，|ω_r|+|ω_z|≲r^{-9/8}[\log(e+r)]^{9/8}（r≫1），优于Carrillo–Pan–Zhang的估计。(ii) 在无任何对称性假设下，若sup_{|x'|=r,z∈R}|u|≤Cr^{-2/3}[\log(e+r)]^{-γ}或sup_{|x'|=r,z∈R}|ω|≤Cr^{-5/3}[\log(e+r)]^{-γ}（γ>1/3），则D-解必为平凡解。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入柱几何下的新型Calderón–Zygmund估计和新的Liouville论证策略，同时改进了衰减估计并大幅推广了轴对称Liouville准则至一般的无对称情形，为最终解决三维Navier–Stokes方程的Liouville问题提供了重要进展。

Abstract: The Liouville problem for the three-dimensional stationary Navier--Stokes equations remains open, even for axisymmetric \(D\)-solutions. In this paper, we obtain two results based on decay in the cylindrical radial variable \(r=|x'|\).
  (i). Using a new pointwise Calderón--Zygmund estimate adapted to cylindrical geometry, we improve the decay estimates of Carrillo--Pan--Zhang (2020, JFA) and prove
  \[
  |\nabla u_r|+|\nabla u_z|
  \lesssim r^{-5/4}[\log(\mathrm e+r)]^{5/4},
  \quad
  |ω_r|+|ω_z|
  \lesssim r^{-9/8}[\log(\mathrm e+r)]^{9/8}, \quad r\gg1.
  \]
  (ii). We develop a new approach to Liouville theorems that improves the axisymmetric criteria of Wang (2019, JDE) and Zhao (2019, Nonlinear Anal.). Without any symmetry assumption, we show that a \(D\)-solution is trivial if one of the following holds:
  \[ (\mathrm a).\,\sup_{|x'|=r,\, z\in\mathbb R}
  |u(x',z)|
  \leq
  Cr^{-2/3}[\log(\mathrm e+r)]^{-γ}; \quad (\mathrm b).\,
  \sup_{|x'|=r,\, z\in\mathbb R}
  |ω(x',z)|
  \leq
  Cr^{-5/3}[\log(\mathrm e+r)]^{-γ},
  \]
  for $r\geq1$, where $γ>1/3$.

</details>


### [155] [On advective nonlocal operators: multiplicity of principal eigenpairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06095)
*Arnaud Ducrot,Quentin Griette,Xing Liang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有周期性异质非局部扩散和平流项的算子的主特征值与特征函数的存在性和多重性问题。该算子具有预解正性但非预解紧性，经典Krein-Rutman理论无法直接适用。当平流系数保号时，主特征值唯一存在；当平流系数变号时，可能出现唯一解或连续统解，甚至包含奇异测度分量，并深入讨论了非线性KPP型方程的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期异质非局部扩散模型在生态学、种群动力学等领域有广泛应用，而带平流项的非局部算子由于不满足预解紧性，经典Krein-Rutman理论无法直接处理，因此需要发展新的数学工具来研究其主特征值问题。

Method: 将问题分为平流系数保号与变号两种情况分别处理。对于保号情形，利用预解正性建立主特征值的存在唯一性；对于变号情形，通过精细分析算子的谱结构，刻画特征值与特征向量的完整分类，包括允许特征向量为Radon测度的情形。

Result: 平流系数保号时，主特征值与归一化特征函数唯一存在。平流系数变号时，取决于方程系数，主特征问题可能具有唯一归一化解或解的连续统；在连续统边界处存在带奇异测度分量的主特征向量，且所有构造的特征值均嵌入连续谱中。此外发现KPP型非线性方程存在连续统的非常值平稳解，与经典局部扩散KPP方程行为不同。

Conclusion: 该研究完整刻画了带平流项的周期异质非局部扩散算子主特征值问题，揭示了平流方向性对谱结构的深刻影响，并展示了非局部扩散系统相较于局部扩散系统的本质差异，为非线性非局部扩散方程的研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the existence and multiplicity of principal eigenvalues and eigenfunctions for a periodically heterogeneous nonlocal dispersal model with advection. The operator we consider is resolvent-positive but not resolvent-compact; therefore, the classical Krein-Rutman theory cannot be applied directly. When the advection coefficient has a constant sign, we prove the existence and uniqueness of the principal eigenvalue and the corresponding normalized eigenfunction. In sharp contrast, when the advection does not have a constant sign, the problem is more involved and leads to surprising results. Depending on the coefficients of the equation, the principal eigenproblem can either have a unique normalized solution or a continuum of solutions, at the boundary of which there exists a principal eigenvector with a singular measure component. In the latter situation, all the constructed eigenvalues are embedded in the continuous spectrum of our operator. We completely characterize the eigenvalues associated with positive eigenvectors, even when the eigenvector is a Radon measure. Finally, we discuss an application to a nonlinear KPP-type equation with nonlocal dispersal, which possesses a continuum of nontrivial stationary solutions, a different behavior from the classical KPP equation with local diffusion.

</details>


### [156] [Vanishing viscosity limit to two interacting shocks from the same family for the compressible Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06173)
*Lin-An Li,Dehua Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程在两道同族激波相互作用下的粘性消失极限问题。由于同族激波碰撞会产生一道激波和一道稀疏波，且碰撞发生在与波强度成反比的更大时间尺度上，问题更具奇异性。作者采用碰撞前的反导数方法与碰撞后的加权相对熵方法（结合时间依赖平移），证明了对于足够小的波强度和粘性系数，存在一族全局光滑解收敛到Euler方程的熵解，并给出了显式收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同族两道激波相互作用的粘性消失极限问题比异族激波相互作用更为复杂和奇异：碰撞发生在更大时间尺度上（与波强度成反比），且碰撞后同时产生激波与稀疏波的复合波结构，这为粘性消失极限的验证带来了根本性困难，尤其是在碰撞点处的验证。

Method: 1) 碰撞前：采用反导数方法（anti-derivative method）精确定位两条粘性激波到碰撞点；由于均匀估计无法在粘性参数下闭合到碰撞时刻，引入精心构造的近似碰撞时间以获得所需的高阶能量界。2) 碰撞后：采用加权相对熵方法（weighted relative entropy method）结合时间依赖平移，处理激波与稀疏波共存的复合波结构。

Result: 对于足够小的波强度和粘性系数，证明存在一族Navier-Stokes方程的光滑全局解，收敛到Euler方程的熵解，并给出显式收敛率。

Conclusion: 该方法为同族激波相互作用的粘性消失极限问题提供了完整的理论框架，其技术有望推广到粘性消失理论中的其他相关问题。

Abstract: We investigate the vanishing viscosity limit for the one-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes equations in the regime of two interacting shock waves from the same characteristic family of the underlying Euler equations. Unlike the interaction of two shocks from distinct families, which produces two outgoing shocks in their respective original families, the collision of two same-family shocks generates an outgoing shock in the same family together with a rarefaction wave in the other family. This configuration is more singular because the collision occurs on a larger time scale that depends inversely on the wave strength, making it challenging to justify the vanishing viscosity limit not only in the processes before and after the shock collision but, more crucially, at the collision point. Furthermore, the emergence of both shock and rarefaction waves after the collision introduces an additional difficulty. To overcome these obstacles, we first employ the anti-derivative method before the collision, which allows us to fix the locations of both viscous shocks precisely up to the collision point. However, uniform estimates with respect to the viscosity cannot be closed up to the collision time; we therefore introduce a carefully constructed approximate collision time to obtain the required higher-order energy bounds. After the collision, we apply a weighted relative entropy method, combined with time-dependent shifts, to handle the composite wave structure arising from the coexistence of shock and rarefaction waves. Our main result establishes that, for suitably small wave strengths and viscosity coefficients, there exists a family of global smooth solutions to the Navier-Stokes equations that converge to the entropy solution of the Euler equations with an explicit convergence rate. The techniques are expected to be applicable to other related problems in vanishing viscosity theory.

</details>


### [157] [The energy of fractional Allen--Cahn layers in dimension one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06226)
*Alvaro Carballeira,Damien Galant,Javier Gómez-Serrano*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维分数阶Allen–Cahn层解Φ_s的能量E(s)，对s∈(1/2,1]给出了能量的严格单调递减性、连续性，以及在两个端点处的精确渐近展开式（上端点E(s)=2√2/3+κ₁(1-s)+o(1-s)，下端点E(s)=1/[π(s-1/2)]+O(1)）。严格递减性通过计算机辅助证明完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是对分数阶Allen–Cahn方程层解的能量提供完整的定性与定量刻画——包括连续性、严格递减性以及两个端点处的精确渐近行为，填补该区间内能量性质研究的空白。

Method: 结合多种技术手段：(1) Cabré–Sire的层构造方法；(2) Palatucci–Savin–Valdinoci的极小性定理；(3) 关于能量对s导数的恒等式；(4) 计算机辅助的强制性估计（在显式近似层上通过区间算术验证有限个不等式），从而在内部子区间上降为有限个可验证的不等式。

Result: (1) 能量在(1/2,1]上连续且严格递减；(2) 上端点s=1处：E(s)=2√2/3+κ₁(1-s)+o(1-s)，其中κ₁有显式表达式；(3) 下端点s→1/2⁺处：E(s)=1/[π(s-1/2)]+O(1)，能量发散到无穷。

Conclusion: 完成了分数阶Allen–Cahn层解能量的全面刻画，验证了能量随s从1/2到1递减的直觉，并给出了两端点的精确渐近公式；严格递减性的证明依赖于计算机辅助验证，展示了人工分析与区间算术相结合的有效性。

Abstract: We study the energy $\mathcal{E}(s) := E_{s}[Φ_s]$ of the one-dimensional fractional Allen--Cahn layer solution $Φ_s$, defined as the unique odd, increasing solution of $(-Δ)^{s} Φ_s = Φ_s - Φ_s^{3}$ with $Φ_s(\pm\infty)=\pm 1$ and $Φ_s(0)=0$, for $s\in(1/2,1]$. Our main results are a sharp qualitative and quantitative description of the energy $\mathcal{E}$ on this interval. We show that the energy is continuous and strictly decreasing, and we obtain explicit asymptotic expansions at both endpoints. At the upper endpoint we prove $\mathcal{E}(s) = \frac{2\sqrt{2}}{3} + κ_1 (1-s) + o(1-s)$ with an explicit formula for $κ_1$. At the lower endpoint we prove that the energy goes to infinity as $\mathcal{E}(s)=\frac{1}{π(s-1/2)}+O(1)$. The strict decrease is proved with computer assistance. On an interior subinterval it is reduced to finitely many inequalities verified by interval-arithmetic computations. The proofs combine the Cabré--Sire construction of the layer, the minimality theorem of Palatucci--Savin--Valdinoci, an identity for the $s$ derivative of the energy, and a computer-assisted coercivity estimate at explicit approximate layers.

</details>


### [158] [Well-posedness of stochastic time-nonlocal telegraph equations with Hölder diffusion coefficient: hereditary phase-space lifting and novel generalized coupling method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06237)
*Xi Huang,Li Peng,Juan Carlos Pozo,Yong Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对带(PC_ε)型核的随机时间非局部电报方程的初边值问题，提出了新的遗传相空间提升框架和广义耦合框架，首次证明了温和解在L_loc^2(R_+; H^δ)中的弱存在性和分布唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机时间非局部电报方程描述了小尺度系统在随机涨落下的高频信号传播，但此类方程的弱解存在性和唯一性此前尚未被证明，需要开发新的分析工具来突破技术障碍。

Method: 提出了两个核心分析框架：(1) 新的遗传相空间提升框架，用于处理时间非局部电报方程；(2) 泛化耦合框架，包含速度阻尼控制项的新构造，可处理低正则非线性项。正则性指标δ可任意接近min{1/2, ε/(2+ε)}，Hölder指数κ的下界由可积性指数ε定量决定。

Result: 首次证明了随机非局部电报方程在L_loc^2(R_+; H^δ)中温和解的弱存在性和分布唯一性。对于随机阻尼波动方程（将a替换为Dirac测度δ_0的情形），κ可改进为(3/5,1]中的任意值。广义耦合框架还可处理同时依赖位移和速度的低正则非线性项。

Conclusion: 本文建立的遗传相空间提升框架和广义耦合框架为随机时间非局部偏微分方程提供了通用的分析工具，拓展了随机非线性偏微分方程弱解理论的应用范围。

Abstract: We consider the initial-boundary value problem for the stochastic time-nonlocal telegraph equation with $(\mathcal{PC}_\varepsilon)$-type kernel $a$:
  \begin{align*}
  γ\partial_t \left( a \ast \partial_t (a \ast v)\right) =Δv-\partial_t (a \ast v)+ Ψ(v)+ Φ(v) \frac{\mathrm{d}W(t)}{\mathrm{d}t},
  \end{align*}
  where $W$ is a space-time Gaussian white noise, $Ψ$ satisfies a linear growth condition, and $Φ$ is Hölder continuous and uniformly nondegenerate. This model characterizes high-frequency signal propagation in small-scale systems under stochastic fluctuations. We develop a new hereditary phase-space lifting framework for time-nonlocal telegraph equations. In addition, we propose a novel generalized coupling framework, which features a new construction of the damping control term for the velocity. Based on these analytic tools, we prove the first results on weak existence and uniqueness in law for mild solutions, valued in $L_{loc}^2(\mathbb R_+; H^δ)$, to the stochastic nonlocal telegraph equation. The regularity index $δ$ can be arbitrarily close to $\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{\varepsilon}{2+\varepsilon} \}$ from below, and the admissible lower bound of the Hölder exponent $κ$ is quantitatively determined by the integrability exponent $\varepsilon$. For the corresponding IBVP of the stochastic damped wave equation, obtained by replacing $a$ with the Dirac measure $δ_0$, $κ$ can be improved to any value in $(\frac{3}{5}, 1]$. More significantly, the generalized coupling framework also handles low-regularity nonlinearities depending on both displacement and velocity.

</details>


### [159] [Nonconvex Sublevel Sets For The Planar Translating Mean Curvature Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06293)
*Jiahuan Li,Yilu Liu,Xi-Nan Ma,Guohuan Qiu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个有界均匀凸区域Ω⊂R²，使得平面平移平均曲率方程的零Dirichlet解的某个子水平集是非凸的，尽管该区域本身是凸的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移孤子作为平均曲率流II型奇点的模型出现，理解其解的几何性质（如子水平集的凸性）对研究平均曲率流奇点行为具有重要意义。此前人们可能倾向于认为在凸区域上方程的解具有凸的子水平集，本文通过反例揭示这一直观认识并不成立。

Method: 构造方法基于两个关键技术：(1) 修正的近临界死神轮廓（grim-reaper profile），用于调整解的局部行为；(2) 在长凸通道上构造显式屏障（barriers），用于控制解的全局行为。通过将这两种工具结合，在一个精心设计的凸区域上使得方程解的某个子水平集产生非凸性。

Result: 成功构造了一个光滑有界均匀凸域Ω⊂R²，使得零Dirichlet边值问题的平移平均曲率方程解的子水平集呈现非凸性，从而给出反例。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，即使区域本身是凸的，平移平均曲率方程解的子水平集也未必保持凸性，这对理解平移孤子的几何结构及其在平均曲率流奇点分析中的作用提供了新的认知。

Abstract: Translating solitons arise as models for type~II singularities of mean-convex mean curvature flow. We construct a smooth bounded uniformly convex domain \(\Om\Subset\R^2\) such that the zero-Dirichlet solution of the planar translating mean curvature equation has a nonconvex sublevel set. The construction is based on a corrected near-critical grim-reaper profile and explicit barriers on a long convex channel.

</details>


### [160] [Sub-critical well-posedness for the intermediate nonlinear Schrödinger equation on the line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06298)
*Andreia Chapouto,Justin Forlano,Thierry Laurens*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究中间非线性薛定谔方程(INLS)的适定性理论。主要贡献有二：其一，证明INLS在H^s(R)中对任意s>0是局部适定的，改进此前s>1/4的结果，覆盖完整的scaling-subcritical范围，同时无需手性假设即获得连续Calogero-Moser方程的局部适定性；其二，对可积模型，证明小L²范数初始数据在0<s<1/2范围内的全局适定性，并指出该结果可由流的L²等度连续性性质推广至去掉小数据限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在推进INLS方程的适定性理论，此前其研究仅证明了s>1/4的局部适定性，本文希望覆盖完整的scaling-subcritical范围。同时，对于可积模型，作者希望建立全局适定性结果，并探索去除小数据限制的可能性。所提出的方法不依赖完全可积性，具有更广泛的适用性。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1)对于局部适定性，采用规范变换、导出关于四个辅助变量的封闭系统，并利用非线性光滑化估计（非线性平滑估计），不依赖方程的可积结构；(2)对于全局适定性，利用先前工作中发现的Lax对构造一族新的守恒量，以此建立0<s<1/2时的全局适定性，并论证该结果在L²等度连续集合被流保持的条件下可推广至一般数据。

Result: (1) INLS在H^s(R)中对任意s>0局部适定，覆盖完整scaling-subcritical范围；连续Calogero-Moser方程在无手性假设下亦获得全范围局部适定性。(2) 可积模型中，小L²范数初始数据在0<s<1/2时全局适定。全局适定性结果与L²等度连续集合的流不变性相关联，若能建立先验等度连续性，则小数据限制可移除。

Conclusion: 本文在INLS方程的适定性问题上取得了关键进展，将局部适定性阈值从s>1/4推进至任意s>0，完整覆盖了scaling-subcritical范围；同时通过新型守恒量建立了可积模型的全局适定性框架。作者指出泛化方向：先验等度连续性结果将消除小数据限制，为后续研究指明路径。

Abstract: We continue our study of the well-posedness theory for the intermediate nonlinear Schrödinger equation (INLS). Firstly, we prove that INLS is locally well-posed in $H^s (\mathbb{R})$ for any $s>0$. This improves on our previous result of local well-posedness for any $s>\frac 14$, and covers the full scaling-subcritical range for INLS. In particular, we also obtain the local well-posedness for the continuum Calogero-Moser equation without chirality assumption in the full scaling-subcritical range. Our method relies on a gauge transformation, the derivation of a closed system for four auxiliary variables, and nonlinear smoothing estimates, but not on the completely integrable nature of these equations.
  Secondly, for the integrable models, we prove global well-posedness for $0<s<\frac12$ for initial data with small $L^2$-norm. Moreover, we show that our global well-posedness result applies whenever $L^2$-equicontinuous sets are preserved by the flow, and so the small-data restriction would be removed by an a-priori equicontinuity result. Our argument relies on a novel family of conserved quantities based upon the Lax pair we discovered in our prior work.

</details>


### [161] [A General Aubry-Mather Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06344)
*Nassif Ghoussoub*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是对一部专著《Skew Linear Entropies and Kantorovich Operators: A General Aubry-Mather Theory》前言、概述和目录的复现。该专著定义了一类名为Kantorovich算子的非线性算子，并为其发展了遍历理论，通过"斜线性熵"（一种广义最优质量传输）实现对偶理论，将Aubry-Mather理论从哈密顿动力学推广至任意Kantorovich算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kantorovich算子在分析学、概率论、动力系统、数理经济学和金融等众多领域普遍存在，但经典遍历理论（基于Markov算子、自由能转移或Hopf-Lax-Oleinik半群）不足以处理这类没有伴随算子的非线性算子。作者旨在发展一套能够覆盖这些广泛应用的统一遍历理论框架。

Method: 由于Kantorovich算子缺乏伴随算子，作者引入了一对概率分布（源与目标）上的凸泛函——"斜线性熵"来替代传统的对偶结构。斜线性熵是一种广义最优质量传输形式，基于此构造出完整遍历理论体系，为任意Kantorovich算子赋予极值测度、Mather常数、弱KAM解及Aubry集等概念。

Result: 该理论将Aubry-Mather理论从哈密顿动力学的原始背景扩展到任意Kantorovich算子，建立了包括极小测度、Mather常数、弱KAM解和Aubry集的完整遍历理论，并推广了Markov算子、自由能转移、Hopf-Lax-Oleinik半群等经典情形。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一部系统化专著的前言概述，首次将凝聚态物理和哈密顿动力学中的Aubry-Mather理论推广到一般Kantorovich算子的框架下，并建立了以斜线性熵为纽带的最优传输与遍历理论的深层联系。

Abstract: This paper reproduces the front matter --- preface, overview and table of contents --- of a monograph by the author, submitted for publication under the title {\it Skew Linear Entropies and Kantorovich Operators: A General Aubry-Mather Theory}. The book isolates a class of non-linear operators, which we call {\it Kantorovich operators}, that are ubiquitous in analysis, probability, dynamical systems, mathematical economics and finance. We develop aspects of their ergodic theory in a way that extends classical ones involving Markov operators, free-energy transfers, or the Hopf--Lax--Oleinik semi-group. Having no adjoint, the duality between such an operator and measures is carried instead via a convex functional on {\it pairs} of probability distributions --- a source and a target --- which we call a {\it skew-linear entropy}, and which is a general form of optimal mass transport. The extensive overview reproduced here describes the resulting ergodic theory, in which minimal measures, a Mather constant, weak KAM solutions and an Aubry set are attached to an arbitrary Kantorovich operator, extending Aubry--Mather theory well beyond its origins in Hamiltonian dynamics.

</details>


### [162] [Quasiconvexity for the Dacorogna--Marcellini Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06367)
*Giuseppe Bruno,Federico Pasqualotto*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了平面Dacorogna-Marcellini能量f_γ(A)=|A|^4-2γ|A|²det A在|γ|≤2/√3时恰好既是拟凸的也是秩一凸的，通过分量热流的单调性性质建立等价性，并推广到左右旋转不变的四次齐次多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dacorogna-Marcellini能量是变分法和非线性弹性理论中的重要反例对象，本文旨在精确刻画该能量拟凸性与秩一凸性等价的参数范围。

Method: 利用能量泛函沿分量热流的单调性性质，建立了拟凸性与秩一凸性的等价关系，并推广到一般左右旋转不变的四次齐次多项式。

Result: 证明了f_γ拟凸当且仅当秩一凸，即|γ|≤2/√3；进一步证明了在2×2矩阵上左右旋转不变的四次齐次多项式中，拟凸性与秩一凸性等价。

Conclusion: 给出了Dacorogna-Marcellini能量精确的拟凸性临界值，并通过热流方法证明了该类对称四次多项式下拟凸与秩一凸的等价性，深化了对非线性变分问题中凸性概念关系的理解。

Abstract: We prove that the planar Dacorogna--Marcellini energy $f_γ(A)=|A|^4-2γ|A|^2\det A$ is quasiconvex exactly when it is rank-one convex, i.e. if and only if $|γ|\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt3}$. The proof uses a monotonicity property of the energy functional along the componentwise heat flow. As a corollary of our method, we show that for homogeneous quartic polynomials on $2 \times 2$ matrices invariant by left and right rotation, quasiconvexity is equivalent to rank one convexity.

</details>


### [163] [Global weak solutions to the Cahn-Hilliard equation with degenerate mobility and singular diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06371)
*Monica Conti,Andrea Giorgini,Greta Ricchi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有退化迁移率和奇异扩散的Cahn-Hilliard方程（描述聚合物共混物中相分离动力学的Flory-Huggins-de Gennes模型）的初边值问题，在三维有界光滑凸域中证明了有限能量初值下合适全局弱解的存在性。关键创新在于不依赖经典熵方法，直接推导出解u和函数φ(u)=arcsin(u)的L^4(0,T;H^2(Ω))估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型描述聚合物共混物的相分离动力学，其数学模型包含退化迁移率和纯相处的奇异扩散，这使得经典的理论工具难以直接应用。已有的熵方法在推导高阶估计时存在局限性，需要探索新的分析途径来建立全局弱解的存在性理论。

Method: 针对带有退化迁移率和奇异扩散（与Flory-Huggins-de Gennes自由能相关）的Cahn-Hilliard方程初边值问题，在三维有界光滑凸域中，从有限能量的初始条件出发，通过一种不依赖经典熵方法的新分析途径，推导出解u及其变换函数φ(u)=arcsin(u)在L^4(0,T;H^2(Ω))空间中的正则性估计，并在此基础上证明全局弱解的存在性。

Result: 证明了在三维有界光滑凸域中，具有退化迁移率和奇异扩散的Cahn-Hilliard方程存在合适的全局弱解；建立了不依赖熵方法的L^4(0,T;H^2(Ω))先验估计，这对解u和函数φ(u)同时成立。

Conclusion: 本文为具有退化迁移率和奇异扩散的Cahn-Hilliard方程建立了全局弱解存在性理论，提出了一种替代经典熵方法的新分析框架，为后续研究该模型的更精细正则性、唯一性与长时间行为奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the initial-boundary value problem for the Cahn-Hilliard equation with degenerate mobility and singular diffusion at pure phases. This model describes the dynamics of phase separation in polymer blends with associated Flory-Huggins-de Gennes free energy. We prove the existence of suitable global weak solutions in three-dimensional bounded, smooth, and convex domains, assuming that the initial datum has finite energy. A key novelty of our analysis is the derivation of $L^4(0,T; H^2(Ω))$ estimates for both the solution $u$ and the function $φ(u)=\arcsin(u)$, obtained without relying on the classical entropy-based approach.

</details>
