### [164] [A variational framework for Bayesian inversion in moving boundary Darcy flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05317)
*Michael E. Causon,Marco A. Iglesias,Michael V. Tretyakov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于移动边界单相达西流（树脂传递模塑）中从压力观测恢复空间变化对数渗透率的无限维贝叶斯反演框架，核心贡献是逐观测representer公式，将参数到观测映射的灵敏度与移动边界状态和伴随系统相关联，从而得到约化的Levenberg-Marquardt方法用于高斯先验Cameron-Martin空间上的MAP估计，以及线性化MAP近似的显式有限秩协方差。1D中利用显式移动边界解建立后验适定性和MAP估计存在性；2D中引入极弱混合公式并结合形式化单向形状线性化。数值实验表明1D下LMAP近似与参考MCMC后验高度吻合，仅需O(10^1)次正演模拟（对比O(10^5-10^6)），2D下以显著更低计算成本获得与大型集成EKI相当的重建与不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树脂传递模塑（RTM）等应用中，从压力观测反演空间变化的渗透率场具有重要的实际意义，但移动边界涉及时变的物理域，使传统贝叶斯反演框架面临参数空间维度高、正演模拟成本高昂（每次需求解移动边界PDE）的挑战。作者旨在开发一种在无限维空间中高效且稳健的贝叶斯反演方法，使计算成本独立于离散化维度。

Method: 方法包含三个层面：(1) 提出逐观测representer公式，将参数到观测映射的灵敏度分解为移动边界状态系统与伴随系统的求解，进而在观测空间内实现约化Levenberg-Marquardt更新和有限秩LMAP协方差组装；(2) 1D中利用显式移动边界解建立后验适定性、MAP估计存在性，并推导闭式Fréchet导数与representers；(3) 2D中引入耦合压力、界面速度与域演化的极弱混合公式，通过形式化单向形状线性化推导线性化状态与伴随系统，各观测representers通过独立并行的伴随问题求解。

Result: 1D数值实验中，LMAP近似与参考MCMC后验紧密吻合，仅需约O(10^1)次正演模拟，而MCMC需要O(10^5-10^6)次。2D实验中，方法在显著更低计算成本下获得与大型集成EKI相当的重建结果和不确定性估计。方法在多种几何构型和实验配置下均无需重构反演方法即可直接应用。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个计算高效、与离散化维度解耦的移动边界达西流贝叶斯反演框架，兼具理论保证（后验适定性、MAP存在性）和实用性能（远超MCMC和大型EKI的效率），为RTM等移动边界工业过程提供了可扩展的不确定性量化工具。

Abstract: We develop an infinite-dimensional Bayesian framework for recovering spatially varying log-permeability from pressure observations in single-phase Darcy flow with a moving boundary, motivated by resin transfer moulding. The main contribution is an observation-wise representer formulation linking sensitivities of the parameter-to-observable map to the moving-boundary state and adjoint systems. This yields a reduced Levenberg--Marquardt method for maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation on the Cameron--Martin space of a Gaussian prior and an explicit finite-rank covariance for the linearised MAP (LMAP) approximation. Once the representers are computed, the LM update and covariance assembly reduce to linear algebra in observation space, independently of the discretisation dimension.
  In 1D, the explicit moving-boundary solution is used to establish posterior well-posedness and existence of MAP estimators, and to derive closed-form Fréchet derivatives and representers. In 2D, we introduce a very weak mixed formulation coupling pressure, interface velocity and domain evolution. A formal one-sided directional shape linearisation yields the corresponding linearised state and adjoint systems, with observation-wise representers computed through independent, parallel adjoint problems.
  Numerical experiments show that the 1D LMAP approximation closely matches a reference MCMC posterior while requiring \(\mathcal{O}(10^1)\) rather than \(\mathcal{O}(10^5\text{--}10^6)\) forward simulations. In 2D, the method produces reconstructions and uncertainty estimates comparable to ensemble Kalman inversion with large ensembles, at substantially lower computational cost. The workflow is demonstrated across several geometries and experimental configurations without reformulating the inversion methodology.

</details>


### [165] [Structured Tensor Approximation from Lateral Slice Sampling via Basis and Manifold Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05463)
*Jeongmin Chae,Usama Saleem,Selin Bac,Shaama Mallikarjun Sharada,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BMTA的张量近似算法，在仅观测少量侧切片的情况下，同时利用全局准基模型和局部流形引导插值模型来捕捉张量演化中的全局与局部结构，并基于低秩Tucker重构框架进行求解，提供了非渐近重构误差界的理论分析，并在量子化学和时空感知等真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化张量近似问题中，仅有少量侧面切片被观测，传统方法难以同时捕捉张量演化中的全局趋势与局部关系，需要一种既能利用全局平滑结构又能刻画局部流形特性的高效近似方法。

Method: BMTA算法融合两种信号模型：(i) 准基模型（quasi-basis model），捕捉沿物理轨迹的平滑全局变化；(ii) 流形引导插值模型（manifold-guided interpolation model），刻画张量切片间的局部关系。算法采用低秩Tucker重构框架来高效捕获这些先验，分别生成用于基函数估计的系数和一个张量优化问题。

Result: 提供了非渐近重构误差界的理论分析，该误差界刻画了采样复杂度、优化收敛性和模型失配的影响；数值实验在合成数据及真实数据集（包括量子化学和时空感知应用）上验证了算法的性能。

Conclusion: BMTA通过同时建模全局基与局部流形结构，在稀疏观测条件下实现了有效的结构化张量重构，并给出了坚实的理论保障，适用于量子化学和时空感知等实际场景。

Abstract: In this work, we consider a structured tensor approximation problem, where only a limited number of lateral slices are observed. The proposed algorithm , called Basis and Manifold prior Tensor Approximation (BMTA), exploits both global and local structures of the evolution of a global tensor. Specifically, BMTA integrates two signal models: (i) a quasi-basis model that captures smooth global variations along a physical trajectory, and (ii) a manifold-guided interpolation model that characterizes local relationships among tensor slices. A low-rank Tucker reconstruction framework is incorporated to efficiently capture the priors, resulting in coefficients for basis function estimation and a tensor optimization. In addition, we provide a theoretical analysis which establishes a non-asymptotic reconstruction error bound that characterizes the effects of sampling complexity, optimization convergence, and model mismatch. Numerical experiments are performed on both synthetic and real-world datasets, including quantum chemistry and spatiotemporal sensing applications.

</details>


### [166] [A priori error estimator for reduced-order models based on the higher-order Craig-Bampton method in dynamic substructuring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05625)
*Jaemin Kim,Seung-Hwan Boo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高阶Craig-Bampton (HCB)方法的先验误差估计框架，利用HCB方法嵌套Ritz子空间的结构特性，通过高阶解作为低阶解的参考来估计特征值误差，无需求解全阶问题。该框架包括广义CB误差估计器和基于HCB-2的HCB-1误差估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CB方法及其高阶扩展HCB方法在动态子结构中广泛应用，HCB在相同模型维度下精度更高，但至今没有针对HCB方法的先验误差估计器。评估降阶模型精度通常需要求解全阶问题，这违背了模型降阶的初衷。

Method: 利用HCB方法中嵌套Ritz子空间的结构特性，构建分层估计框架：每个高阶解作为前一层阶解的参考来估计误差。具体包括：(i) 基于Rayleigh商扰动分析推导广义CB误差估计器；(ii) 利用HCB-2特征解作为参考，提出新的HCB-1误差估计器。

Result: 在不同几何复杂度的数值算例中验证了两种误差估计器的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层框架成功为HCB方法提供了无需求解全阶问题的先验误差估计能力，填补了该方向的研究空白。

Abstract: The Craig-Bampton (CB) method is a widely used dynamic substructuring technique based on component mode synthesis (CMS). The higher-order Craig-Bampton (HCB) method augments the CB basis with residual modes from a Neumann series expansion of the residual flexibility matrix, where HCB-n retains terms up to the n-th order and is reduced back to the CB size via the System Equivalent Reduction Expansion Process (SEREP), achieving improved accuracy at the same model dimension. However, assessing the accuracy of a reduced model without solving the full-order problem remains a fundamental challenge: if the full-order solution is required to evaluate the error, the purpose of model reduction is defeated. Despite the demonstrated superiority of the HCB method, no a priori error estimator (one that predicts eigenvalue errors without solving the full-order eigenvalue problem) has been proposed for it. The present work addresses this gap with a hierarchical estimation framework that exploits the nested Ritz subspace structure of the HCB method, where each higher-order solution serves as a reference for estimating the error of the preceding order. The framework provides (i) a generalized CB error estimator derived from a Rayleigh quotient perturbation analysis, and (ii) a novel HCB-1 error estimator using the HCB-2 eigensolution as a reference. Numerical examples across models of varying geometric complexity validate both estimators.

</details>


### [167] [A Second-Order Monolithic Scheme for the Coupled Stokes--Biot Model Using Total Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05501)
*Talal Alshehri,Mingchao Cai,Xiaoqin Shen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对耦合的非稳态Stokes与准静态Biot系统，提出并分析了一种二阶、完全隐式、整体式（monolithic）时间离散格式。Biot子系统采用三场总压力公式（引入总压力作为额外未知量，连同固体位移和孔隙压力），该重构在近不可压缩区域增强了鲁棒性并抑制了体积锁定。所有变量均用BDF2格式进行时间离散，耦合问题整体求解，无需算子分裂。利用BDF2的G-稳定性恒等式建立了离散能量稳定性，所得估计对Lamé参数、Biot–Willis系数和存储系数具有鲁棒性。通过一致性–稳定性论证推导出时间上的二阶收敛与空间上的最优阶收敛。数值实验验证了理论结果，并展示了相应能量范数下的二阶时间精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决耦合的Stokes-Biot系统在近不可压缩条件下的数值稳定性问题，特别是需要一种鲁棒的二阶时间离散方案，能够同时处理体积锁定、参数敏感性以及整体耦合求解的稳定性与收敛性保证。

Method: 采用三场总压力公式重新表述Biot子系统（总压力、固体位移、孔隙压力作为独立未知量），所有变量统一使用BDF2时间离散，采用整体式（monolithic）求解方式而非算子分裂。利用BDF2的G-稳定性恒等式推导离散能量稳定性，并通过一致性–稳定性（consistency–stability）论证获得时间二阶和空间最优阶收敛。

Result: 建立了对Lamé参数、Biot–Willis系数和存储系数具有鲁棒性的离散能量稳定性估计；推导出时间方向二阶收敛和空间方向最优阶收敛的理论保证；数值实验证实了理论结果并在相应能量范数下展示了二阶时间精度。

Conclusion: 该二阶、完全隐式、整体式时间离散格式为Stokes-Biot耦合系统提供了一种鲁棒且高效的数值方案，兼顾了二阶精度、稳定性以及近不可压缩极端参数下的可靠性。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a second-order, fully implicit, monolithic time-discretization for the coupled time-dependent Stokes and quasi-static Biot system. The Biot subsystem is formulated in a three-field total-pressure formulation, in which the total pressure is introduced as an additional unknown together with the solid displacement and pore pressure. This reformulation improves robustness in nearly incompressible regimes and suppresses volumetric locking. All variables are discretized in time using the BDF2 scheme, and the coupled problem is solved monolithically without operator splitting. Discrete energy stability is established using the BDF2 $G$-stability identity. The resulting estimates exhibit robustness with respect to the Lamé parameter, Biot--Willis coefficient, and storage coefficient. A consistency--stability argument is then used to derive second-order convergence in time together with optimal-order convergence in space. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results and demonstrate second-order temporal accuracy in the corresponding energy norms.

</details>


### [168] [Splitting methods for Intermediate Long Wave and perturbed Benjamin--Ono models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05540)
*Yvonne Alama Bronsard,Clémentine Courtès,Benjamin Melinand*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Birkhoff坐标的显式分裂格式，用于数值求解Intermediate Long Wave (ILW)方程及其他拟线性PDE，在H^s范数下实现一阶收敛，仅需初始数据多一个导数即可，远优于传统分裂方法。该方案在深水极限下收敛到Benjamin-Ono (BO)方程的解，并在长时间模拟中不要求二次时间步长限制，最后应用于孤子分辨猜想的数值探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BO方程的Birkhoff坐标和显式解公式已带来理论与计算上的重大进展，但ILW方程等非可积或拟线性方程缺乏类似工具。传统分裂方法处理非线性色散方程时正则性要求高且受时间步长限制。作者旨在利用BO的显式结构，构造一种收敛性好、计算效率高、适用于更广类PDE的统一数值方案。

Method: 将BO的Birkhoff坐标及其显式公式作为基础框架，设计一种新型分裂方法。该方法通过扰动BO的显式公式来求解包括ILW方程在内的拟线性PDE类。利用Birkhoff坐标建立H^s范数下的一阶收敛性分析，并证明ILW方程在深水极限下收敛到BO解。数值实现采用分裂格式，不依赖二次时间步长条件。

Result: 在H^s范数（s≥0）下证明了一阶收敛，仅需初始数据u_0∈H^{s+1}_0，相比典型分裂方法对非线性色散方程的正则性要求显著降低。深水极限下ILW格式收敛到BO解。数值模拟表明该方法不需要限制性的二次时间步长条件即可在长时间尺度上近保能量，计算效率与精度显著优于经典分裂方法。作为应用，对ILW和KdV-BO方程进行了孤子分辨猜想的数值探索。

Conclusion: 该方案弥合了BO可积结构理论与一般拟线性PDE数值计算之间的鸿沟，提供了一种高效、精确且收敛性良好的长时间模拟工具。对孤子分辨猜想的数值证据也为相关领域提供了新的研究线索。

Abstract: Recently, novel integrability techniques, and most notably Birkhoff coordinates followed by an explicit formula, have been introduced to solve the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, leading to major theoretical and computational advances. We propose to perturb this explicit BO formula via a novel splitting method for numerically solving a class of PDEs, which includes the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation, as well as other quasilinear equations that are not necessarily integrable. Using the Birkhoff coordinates of BO we establish first-order convergence in the $H^s$-norm ($s \ge 0$) while requiring only one additional derivative on the initial data $u_0 \in H^{s+1}_{0}$, significantly improving upon the regularity requirements of typical splitting methods for nonlinear dispersive equations. Furthermore, we prove that in the deep-water limit, the scheme for the ILW equation converges to the BO solution. Computational advantages of these schemes are shown in simulations: unlike classical splitting methods, our approach does not require a restrictive quadratic time step condition to nearly preserve energy over long time scales, thus rendering efficient and accurate long-time simulations feasible. As an application, we numerically explore the soliton resolution conjecture for both the ILW and a KdV--BO equation.

</details>


### [169] [Minimization-based polynomial corrections for high-order curved boundaries on fixed and moving domains: assessment on finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05768)
*Mirco Ciallella,Walter Boscheri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新策略，在固定和移动弯曲域上施加高阶边界条件。通过将ROD方法重新表述为无需线性系统求逆的简单多项式修正，在Runge-Kutta间断Galerkin和ADER任意拉格朗日-欧拉有限体积框架中实现高阶精度，数值实验验证了2D和3D中高达五阶的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲域上实现高阶精度需要同时处理PDE离散误差和几何误差。传统的ROD方法通过局部线性系统求逆来施加边界条件，但成本随多项式阶数和网格细化而增长。作者希望简化ROD方法，消除线性系统求逆的开销，使其更广泛适用。

Method: 受一维分析启发，将ROD类型的边界处理方法重新表述为简单的多项式修正，无需任何线性系统求逆。将该策略分别集成到Runge-Kutta间断Galerkin框架和ADER任意拉格朗日-欧拉有限体积框架中，用于固定和移动域上的可压缩流动模拟。

Result: 在固定和移动域上，对Dirichlet边界条件和滑移壁边界条件进行了多种数值实验，在2D和3D中实现了高达五阶的收敛分析，验证了所提策略的广泛适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: ROD类型的边界条件施加策略可以被简化为无需线性系统求逆的纯多项式修正，这大幅降低了计算成本并简化了基于最小化的边界处理方法开发，同时保持高阶精度，可广泛应用于不同的数值框架和问题类型。

Abstract: In this work, we present two novel strategies to impose high-order boundary conditions on fixed and moving curved domains, approximated with piecewise affine triangulations. Achieving high-order accuracy on curved domains requires tackling both the PDE discretization error and the geometrical error simultaneously. While the former can be reduced by employing high-order numerical methods such as finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin, the latter demands either a high-order parametrization of the physical domain or a consistent approximation of the boundary conditions. Minimization-based approaches like the Reconstruction for Off-site Data (ROD) method allow one to skip the construction of high-order curvilinear meshes by defining high-order consistent boundary conditions on a computational boundary that does not match the physical one. The ROD approach mitigates the second-order geometrical error by retrieving a modified polynomial in each boundary cell, which enforces the boundary conditions exactly on the physical boundary. However, the standard ROD method requires the inversion of a local linear system, whose cost grows with the polynomial degree and mesh refinement. Inspired by a recent one-dimensional analysis, we show that ROD-type approaches can be recast as simple polynomial corrections, applicable without any linear system inversion. This greatly simplifies the development of minimization-based boundary treatments and reduces the associated computational cost. To prove the wide applicability of our strategy, we develop it within a Runge-Kutta discontinuous Galerkin framework and an ADER arbitrary-Lagrangian-Eulerian finite volume framework for compressible flows on fixed and moving domains. Several numerical experiments with Dirichlet and slip-wall boundary conditions are presented, with convergence analysis up to fifth order in both 2D and 3D.

</details>


### [170] [Divergence-free interpolation of tangential vector fields via matrix-valued kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05547)
*Zhengjie Sun,Lishuo Dong,Biao Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对单位球面上的切向向量场，提出并分析了一族无散度核插值方法。通过从欧氏空间中的标量径向核出发，构造切向值、表面无散度的矩阵核，避免了重复应用表面微分算子。该方法将无散度约束的几何强制与标量生成器的选择分离，并支持低正则化要求的变体。利用向量球谐函数导出显式核表示并刻画傅里叶乘子，还引入了逆拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米构造以保持底层标量带谐核的乘子。针对散乱节点插值，建立了插值矩阵最小特征值的下界，并推导了点态和索伯列夫误差估计（含超收敛性）。数值实验验证了理论收敛性与稳定性，非球面表面上的额外算例展示了该核公式的广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无散度核插值方法在处理球面切向向量场时，往往需要通过重复应用表面微分算子来构造核函数，这既增加了计算复杂度，也对正则性提出了较高要求。作者希望开发一种更灵活、构造更简洁的框架，将无散度约束的几何强制与标量核生成器的选取解耦，从而支持低正则化变体并扩大适用范围。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 从欧氏空间标量径向核出发，构造切向值、表面无散度的矩阵核，避免重复应用表面微分算子；(2) 将无散度约束的强制执行与标量生成器选择分离，支持低阶变体；(3) 利用向量球谐函数导出显式核表示并刻画傅里叶乘子；(4) 引入逆拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米构造保持标量带谐核的乘子；(5) 针对散乱节点插值，建立插值矩阵最小特征值下界，并推导点态与索伯列夫误差估计（含超收敛性）。

Result: 该文建立了完整的理论框架：给出了无散度矩阵核的显式表示与傅里叶乘子刻画，证明了插值矩阵最小特征值的下界，导出了点态和索伯列夫误差估计并在目标函数比原生空间更光滑时实现了超收敛。数值实验验证了理论结果，且在非球面表面上的额外实验表明该核公式具有广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无散度核插值方法在理论上兼具完备性和灵活性，在实践上具有良好的收敛性与稳定性，不仅适用于单位球面，还可推广到非球面表面，为球面切向向量场插值提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a family of divergence-free kernel interpolation methods for tangential vector fields on the unit sphere. Starting from scalar radial kernels in Euclidean space, we construct tangent-valued, surface divergence-free matrix kernels without repeatedly applying surface differential operators. The construction separates the geometric enforcement of the divergence-free constraint from the choice of scalar generator and admits lower-order variants with reduced regularity requirements compared with classical potential-based methods. Using vector spherical harmonics, we derive explicit kernel representations and characterize their Fourier multipliers. We also introduce an inverse Laplace-Beltrami construction that preserves the multipliers of the underlying scalar zonal kernel. For interpolation at scattered nodes, we establish a lower bound for the smallest eigenvalue of the interpolation matrix and derive pointwise and Sobolev error estimates, including superconvergence for targets smoother than the native space. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical convergence and stability results, while additional examples on nonspherical surfaces illustrate the applicability of the kernel formula beyond the sphere.

</details>


### [171] [Iterative Decoupling Methods for a Total-Pressure Formulation of Quasi-Static Electroporoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05566)
*Huipeng Gu,Mingchao Cai,Jingzhi Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对准静态电动孔隙弹性问题，通过引入总压力将其转化为五场系统以解决近不可压缩状态下的孔隙弹性锁死问题，提出了BDF2时间离散与混合有限元空间离散的全耦合单块格式，并开发了一种在电磁子问题与孔隙弹性子问题间交替迭代的解耦方法，证明了其在物理耦合条件下的几何收敛性，数值实验验证了理论预测与无锁死性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准静态电动孔隙弹性方程耦合了Maxwell方程与Biot孔隙弹性理论，但近不可压缩区域存在孔隙弹性锁死问题，导致标准数值方法在空间离散时收敛性退化甚至失效。为此需要引入特殊变量重新表述方程，并设计高效的数值求解策略。

Method: (1) 引入总压力变量，将电动孔隙弹性方程重构为五场系统以缓解锁死；(2) 推导单块弱形式及连续稳定性估计；(3) 采用BDF2时间离散和混合有限元空间离散构造全离散单块格式；(4) 提出交替迭代解耦方法，交替求解电磁子问题与孔隙弹性子问题，并证明其在物理耦合条件下以显式、网格无关的压缩因子几何收敛于单块解；(5) 提出代数等价的简化形式，电磁块每时间步仅求解一次，孔隙弹性块迭代求解并借助压力梯度反馈更新电场修正。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论收敛预测，并展示了该方法在近不可压缩区域的无锁死性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的五场重构、全离散单块格式与迭代解耦算法在保证理论收敛性的同时有效克服了锁死问题，且简化形式显著降低了计算成本，为电动孔隙弹性问题提供了可靠高效的数值求解框架。

Abstract: Quasi-static electroporoelasticity couples Maxwell's equations with Biot's poroelasticity through electrokinetic coupling between the electric field and the pressure gradient. By introducing the total pressure, the electroporoelasticity equations are reformulated as a five-field system to address poroelastic locking in the nearly incompressible regime. For the resulting five-field system, a monolithic weak formulation is derived, together with a continuous stability estimate. A second-order backward differentiation formula (BDF2) time discretization and a mixed finite- element spatial discretization are then introduced, yieling to a fully discrete monolithic scheme. Building on this scheme, we develop an iterative decoupling method that alternates between an electromagnetic subproblem and a poroelastic subproblem, and prove its geometric convergence to the monolithic solution with an explicit mesh-independent contraction factor under the physical coupling condition. An algebraically equivalent reduced form is also presented, in which the electromagnetic block is solved only once per time step, while the poroelastic block is solved iteratively with electric-field correction updates obtained from the pressure-gradient feedback. Numerical experiments verify the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the locking-free performance.

</details>


### [172] [Efficient higher-order multi-scale method and its convergence estimate for dynamic nonlinear hygro-thermo-mechanical coupling problems of heterogeneous structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05580)
*Yifei Ding,Hao Dong,Jiale Linghu,Yangshuai Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高阶多尺度（HOMS）计算框架，用于高效、高精度、低成本地模拟非均质结构中的非线性湿热-力（H-T-M）耦合问题。该方法结合多尺度渐近分析和Taylor展开构建含高阶修正项的渐近模型，推导了点式和积分式误差估计，并开发了离线/在线两阶段数值算法，通过2D和3D数值实验验证了其精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均质结构中的非线性湿热-力耦合问题（如温度/湿度依赖的材料属性、放热化学反应引起的内热源和湿度汇）在实际工程中广泛存在，但精确模拟这类问题通常计算代价高昂。现有方法难以兼顾高精度与低成本，因此需要发展一种能够高效求解该非线性耦合问题、同时具备严格理论保证的计算框架。

Method: 采用多尺度渐近方法结合Taylor级数展开，构造含高阶修正项的高精度渐近模型用于非线性H-T-M耦合问题；推导了多尺度渐近解在点式和积分两种意义上的严格误差估计；基于有限差分和有限元方法开发了含离线（off-line）与在线（on-line）两阶段的高效数值算法，并严格证明了算法收敛性。

Result: 通过二维和三维数值实验评估了HOMS方法的计算性能，结果表明该方法在数值精度和鲁棒性方面表现优异，同时计算开销较低，验证了所提出框架的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: HOMS框架为非线性湿热-力耦合问题提供了一种兼具高精度、高效率和严格理论保证的求解方案，其高阶修正项的必要性和优势通过理论误差估计得到验证，且两阶段算法的设计使其在实际工程应用中具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel higher-order multi-scale (HOMS) computational framework for efficient, high-accuracy, and low-cost simulation of nonlinear hygro-thermo-mechanical (H-T-M) coupling problems in heterogeneous structures. The inherent nonlinearity in the investigated model stems primarily from temperature- or moisture-dependent material properties, and this model also accounts for temperature-dependent internal heat source and moisture sink terms induced by exothermic, moisture-consuming chemical reactions (e.g., hydration). The main contributions of this work are as follows. First, a high-accuracy multi-scale asymptotic model incorporating higher-order correction terms is constructed for nonlinear H-T-M coupling problems in heterogeneous structures with highly spatial inhomogeneity, using the multi-scale asymptotic approach together with Taylor series expansions. Second, rigorous error estimates in both point-wise and integral senses are derived for the multi-scale asymptotic solutions, which theoretically demonstrate the necessity and superiority of the proposed HOMS method. Third, an efficient two-stage numerical algorithm with off-line and on-line stages is developed, based on finite difference and finite element methods, and its convergence is also proved rigorously. Finally, two- and three-dimensional numerical experiments are performed to assess the computational performance of the proposed HOMS approach, showing excellent numerical accuracy and robustness with low computational overhead.

</details>


### [173] [A Kernel Formula for Kinetic Fokker-Planck Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05770)
*Fuqun Han,Wuchen Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文推导了动力学Fokker-Planck方程的显式一步核公式，通过参考动力学和辅助权重构造，在加权相空间中建立了局部弱一致性和一阶有限时间弱收敛性，并给出了正则化Wasserstein型近端算子的变分解释及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学Fokker-Planck方程描述粒子在相空间中的演化，其数值求解和理论分析具有重要应用价值。已有的数值方法通常面临高维问题中的计算瓶颈，而构造显式核公式可以为确定性粒子方案提供高效且可分析的近似手段，从而在动力学采样等领域获得实用优势。

Method: 作者从一个具有显式转移核的参考动力学出发，通过精心选择的辅助权重来编码漂移扰动，从而构建出动力学Fokker-Planck方程的一步核公式。该公式提供了密度演化的显式近似。作者在加权相空间框架下证明了局部弱一致性及一阶有限时间弱收敛性（在适当正则性和矩假设下），并给出了该公式的正则化Wasserstein型近端算子变分解释，同时将构造推广到更广泛的一类抛物型方程。

Result: 建立了核公式的局部弱一致性和一阶有限时间弱收敛定理；给出了公式的变分解释；数值实验验证了核近似的准确性，并展示了其在动力学采样确定性粒子方案中的应用效果。

Conclusion: 该显式核公式为动力学Fokker-Planck方程提供了一种新的、理论上有保证的数值近似途径，不仅具有清晰的收敛性分析框架，还可推广至更广的抛物型方程类别，在动力学采样和粒子方法领域具有潜在广泛应用价值。

Abstract: We derive an explicit one-step kernel formula for the kinetic Fokker-Planck equation. The construction begins with a reference dynamics admitting an explicit transition kernel and uses a carefully chosen auxiliary weight to encode the drift perturbation. The resulting kernel formula provides an explicit approximation of the density evolution. In a weighted phase-space setting, we establish local weak consistency and first-order finite-time weak convergence under suitable regularity and moment assumptions. We also give a formal variational interpretation of the formula in terms of a regularized Wasserstein-type proximal operator and present analogous constructions for a broader class of parabolic equations. Numerical experiments illustrate the accuracy of the kernel approximation and its application to deterministic particle schemes for kinetic sampling.

</details>


### [174] [A neural operator view on U-Nets for inverse imaging problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05839)
*Alexander Auras,Martin Burger,Samira Kabri,Michael Moeller,Michael Schopf-Kuester*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于求解不适定成像逆问题的U-Net类神经算子架构在高分辨率极限下的行为，通过1D玩具示例和有限角度CT重建实验，对比了神经算子U-Net与传统U-Net在不同分辨率间的泛化能力，发现尽管U形神经算子架构设计上具有分辨率不变性，但经典U-Net对分辨率变化的鲁棒性超出预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在解决成像中的不适定逆问题上取得了显著经验成功，但很少有工作研究当离散化分辨率增加、条件不良问题趋近于真正不适定问题极限时这些网络的行为表现。作者希望填补这一空白，理解不同网络架构在分辨率变化下的表现。

Method: 首先回顾了U-Net类架构中常见的神经算子学习方法，讨论了各自优缺点；随后设计了一个1D玩具示例以提高可解释性；最后通过有限角度CT重建的大量数值实验，研究不同类型神经算子U-Net对初始粗略重建的改进效果，并考察网络在不同分辨率泛化的能力。

Result: 实验发现，虽然U形神经算子架构在理论上具有分辨率不变性，但经典U-Net架构在分辨率变化时的鲁棒性却比预期更好，表明经典架构在处理跨分辨率问题时同样具有竞争力甚至优势。

Conclusion: 神经算子U-Net虽然设计上具备分辨率不变性，但在实际问题中经典U-Net的鲁棒性表现出乎意料地好，这提示研究者不应简单认为神经算子方法在分辨率泛化上必然优于经典架构，需要结合具体问题进一步权衡。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown great empirical success in the solution of a wide variety of ill-posed inverse problems in imaging. Yet, very few works have studied their behavior in the limit that turns the discretized ill-conditioned problems into truly ill-posed ones, i.e., for an increasing resolution of the discretization. In this work, we review common approaches to neural operator learning in architectures that resemble a U-Net, one of the most common classical architectures for inverse imaging problems. We discuss advantages and drawbacks of the respective approaches, consider a 1D toy example for improved interpretability, and present extensive numerical experiments on how different types of neural operator U-Nets can improve a first (crude) limited angle CT-reconstruction. In particular, we study how well networks trained for a certain resolution of the discretization generalize to other resolutions. Our finding is that while U-shaped neural operator architectures are by design resolution-invariant, the classical U-Net architecture seems to be more robust with respect to resolution changes than expected.

</details>


### [175] [Rothe time discretization and weak solutions for a cutoff Westervelt system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05865)
*Marvin Fritz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Westervelt方程（一种非线性声学波动方程）的一阶截断形式的全隐式Rothe时间离散化方法。通过引入焓变量和原始迁移率变量，将每个非线性时间步转化为一致单调的椭圆问题，避免了高阶能量估计和逆不等式。作者利用Browder-Minty定理证明了Rothe格式的适定性，通过Alt-Luckhaus型论证建立紧致性并得到弱解的存在性，同时证明了弱强唯一性原理、条件性后验准则和一致性估计，并辅以数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Westervelt方程是非线性声学中的基本模型，其数值离散通常面临非线性项处理困难和时间步适定性挑战。现有方法往往需要高阶能量估计和逆不等式，这在实际应用中受限。作者希望开发一种全隐式Rothe方案，通过合适的变量变换使每个时间步的离散问题成为一致单调的椭圆问题，从而在避免苛刻技术假设的同时保证适定性和收敛性。

Method: 该方法采用以下关键技术：(1) 引入焓变量（enthalpy）和原始迁移率（primitive mobility）变量进行变换，将非线性时间步化为一致单调的椭圆问题；(2) 利用Browder-Minty定理证明每个时间步的适定性；(3) 推导离散能量不等式；(4) 采用Alt-Luckhaus型紧致性论证处理变换后的变量；(5) 通过一致性估计识别Rothe逼近在自然离散时间弱范数下的时间残差；(6) 数值实验验证方案稳定性。

Result: 文章取得以下主要结果：建立了截断一阶系统Rothe格式的适定性并证明弱解存在性；证明了截断问题的弱强唯一性原理；给出了一个条件性a posteriori准则以判断截断何时不活跃；对充分正则的受迫截断问题推导了一致性估计，为Rothe逼近的时间行为提供了严格一致性基础；数值实验表明该方案稳定、呈现近似一阶时间收敛行为，且在测试区域内截断沿离散轨迹保持不活跃。

Conclusion: 该工作为Westervelt方程提供了一个理论上完备、数值上稳定的一阶全隐式Rothe离散框架。通过变量变换将非线性问题转化为单调椭圆问题，不仅绕过了高阶能量估计的技术障碍，还确保了格式的适定性与收敛性。一致性估计为数值观察到的近似一阶时间行为提供了严格的理论解释，而弱强唯一性原理和截断不活跃准则进一步增强了方法的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: We study a fully implicit Rothe time discretization for a cutoff first-order formulation of the Westervelt equation. The key ingredients are the enthalpy variable and the primitive mobility variable, which turn each nonlinear time step into a uniformly monotone elliptic problem and avoid higher-order energy estimates and inverse inequalities. For every time step, the discrete problem reduces to a monotone elliptic equation, which yields well-posedness of the Rothe scheme by the Browder-Minty theorem. We derive a discrete energy inequality, establish compactness for the transformed variable by an Alt-Luckhaus type argument, and pass to the limit to obtain existence of weak solutions for the cutoff first-order system. We prove a weak-strong uniqueness principle for the cutoff problem and formulate a conditional a posteriori criterion under which the cutoff is inactive. For sufficiently regular solutions of the forced cutoff problem, we establish consistency estimates which identify the temporal residuals produced by the Rothe approximation in the natural discrete-in-time weak norms. These estimates provide a rigorous consistency basis for the observed temporal behavior. Finally, numerical experiments illustrate the stability of the scheme, its observed near first-order temporal behavior, and inactivity of the cutoff along the computed discrete trajectories in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [176] [Reduced Basis Method for Simulating Thermal Transients in Electric Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05904)
*Herbert Egger,Eva-Maria Haslhofer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于降基（RB）方法的瞬态热仿真模型，用于感应电机的热分析。该方法在保持有限元（FE）精度和自动构造能力的同时，计算效率可与传统集总参数热网络（LPTN）相媲美，并通过数值实验验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感应电机瞬态热仿真需要在精度和计算效率之间取得平衡：有限元（FE）模型精度高但计算成本大，集总参数热网络（LPTN）计算快但精度有限且需要大量标定。本文旨在探索降基（RB）方法作为第三种方案，兼顾FE精度与LPTN效率。

Method: 采用降基（RB）方法构建参数化偏微分方程的低维近似，对感应电机的瞬态热过程进行建模。将RB模型与FE模型及经测量数据标定的LPTN进行对比，评估精度、自动构造能力与计算效率。

Result: 数值结果表明，RB模型能够达到与FE模型相当的精度；其构造过程可完全自动化；在计算效率上与传统的LPTN相当。

Conclusion: 降基（RB）方法为感应电机瞬态热仿真提供了一种兼具高精度、自动化构造和高效计算的可行方案，有望成为传统FE和LPTN方法的有效替代或补充。

Abstract: Reduced Basis (RB) methods provide low-dimensional approximations of parametrized partial differential equations with controllable accuracy. We discuss the construction of RB approximations for transient thermal simulation of an induction motor and compare their performance to Finite Element (FE) models and Lumped Parameter Thermal Networks (LPTNs) after calibration to measurements. Numerical results demonstrate that RB models can achieve FE accuracy, allow for a fully automatic construction, and offer computational efficiency comparable to traditional LPTNs.

</details>


### [177] [A Thermodynamically Consistent Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes Model for Tumor Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06099)
*Chenyang Li,Ping Lin,Hui Yu,Haibiao Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于能量变分框架，构建了一个热力学一致的肿瘤生长相场模型，将Cahn-Hilliard方程（肿瘤演化和营养运输）与不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程耦合。提出基于多重标量辅助变量（MSAV）方法的一阶时间离散格式与压力修正策略，严格证明其无条件能量稳定性和质量守恒性，并建立了肿瘤相场变量、营养浓度和流体速度的最优一阶时间误差估计，数值实验验证了方法的有效性与收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤生长过程涉及肿瘤细胞演化、营养运输与流体流动之间的复杂非线性耦合，需要开发一个热力学一致且计算高效的数学模型和数值方法，以准确模拟肿瘤生长的动力学行为。

Method: 基于能量变分框架构建热力学一致的肿瘤生长相场模型，耦合Cahn-Hilliard方程与不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程；采用多重标量辅助变量（MSAV）方法结合压力修正策略设计一阶时间离散格式，以高效处理系统的非线性和耦合结构。

Result: 严格证明了所提格式的无条件能量稳定性与质量守恒性；建立了肿瘤相场变量、营养浓度和流体速度的最优一阶时间误差估计；数值实验验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性，并证实了理论收敛阶。

Conclusion: 该工作为肿瘤生长的数值模拟提供了一种理论完备、计算高效且严格分析的相场方法，所提出的MSAV格式在能量稳定性、质量守恒和收敛性方面均具有理论保证，适合用于肿瘤生长的长期数值模拟。

Abstract: This work develops a thermodynamically consistent phase-field model for tumor growth based on the energetic variational framework. The model couples the Cahn-Hilliard equations for tumor evolution and nutrient transport with the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations. A first-order time discretization scheme based on the Multiple Scalar Auxiliary Variables (MSAV) approach together with a pressure-correction strategy is proposed to efficiently handle the nonlinear and coupled structure of the system. The proposed scheme is rigorously proved to be unconditionally energy stable and mass conservative. Furthermore, optimal first-order temporal error estimates are established for the tumor phase-field variable, the nutrient concentration, and the fluid velocity. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method and verify the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [178] [ChromOps.jl: High-order simulation and discrete forward sensitivity analysis for chromatography models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06131)
*Kristian Meyer,Maksym Ratajczyk,Christopher Rackauckas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出ChromOps.jl，一个全可微的Julia色谱求解器，结合高阶空间离散化与离散前向灵敏度分析（DFSA），避免了手推梯度链式法则的负担，实现了高效的基于梯度的参数估计与优化。在六组分离子交换色谱问题上比较了FD-SBP与DG-SEM两种离散方法，FD-SBP在计算效率与实现简洁性上均具优势，且DFSA扩展成本约为每个额外参数1.4次前向求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机理色谱模型对工艺开发至关重要，但基于梯度的参数估计和优化面临两大障碍：计算成本高以及推导目标函数梯度耗时费力，亟需一种自动可微的求解方案。

Method: 开发了全可微的Julia色谱求解器ChromOps.jl，采用高阶空间离散化（有限差分求和-积SBP与间断Galerkin谱元法DG-SEM）结合离散前向灵敏度分析（DFSA），利用ForwardDiff.jl自动微分实现梯度传播，使原对偶求解共享同一代码路径。

Result: 两种空间离散化在人造问题（manufactured problems）上均达到理论收敛阶；FD-SBP在前向（原）与对偶（带伴随值）两类模拟中表现出更优的work-precision性能且实现更简单。DFSA计算成本约为tDFSA≈1.4(1+Np)tfwd，即在单个ForwardDiff.jl chunk中传播最多24个参数时，每个额外参数仅需约1.4次前向求解。

Conclusion: DFSA能在无需手工推导链式法则的情况下提供用户自定义目标的精确梯度，使基于梯度的参数估计与优化流程变得易于获得，为机理色谱建模提供了高效且便捷的工具。

Abstract: Mechanistic chromatography models are valuable for process development, but gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization can be hindered by computational cost and the effort of deriving objective-function gradients. To address this concern, a fully differentiable Julia chromatography solver, ChromOps.jl, is presented that combines high-order spatial discretization with discrete forward sensitivity analysis (DFSA). Two high-order spatial discretizations, finite difference summation-by-parts (FD-SBP) and the discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method (DG-SEM), are compared on a 6-component ion-exchange chromatography problem with steric mass action kinetics. Both attain their theoretical convergence rates on manufactured problems, while FD-SBP shows favourable work-precision performance for both forward (primal) and dual-valued simulations and is conceptually simpler to implement. DFSA scales as tDFSA approx 1.4(1+Np)tfwd for up to Np = 24 parameters propagated in a single ForwardDiff.jl chunk, i.e. about 1.4 forward solves per additional parameter. Because the primal and dual-valued solves share the same code path, DFSA provides accurate gradients of user-defined objectives without any hand-derived chain-rule code, enabling accessible gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization.

</details>


### [179] [Gradient Descent on Point Clouds and Applications in Learned Operator Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06267)
*Andreas Hauptmann,Yury Korolev,Matthew Thorpe*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究在未知流形（仅由点云隐式给出）上最小化能量的问题，提出一种梯度下降方案，该方案在估计流形的同时进行能量最小化，并在适当的稳定性和采样假设下收敛到局部极小值邻域，其大小随时间步长和采样误差趋零而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典欧几里得空间中的梯度下降方案在流形已知时可以自然地推广，但当流形未知（仅以点云形式隐式给出）时，需要同时估计流形和最小化能量，这是一个尚未解决的挑战。

Method: 定义了一种梯度下降方案，该方案能使迭代点保持在流形的邻域内，通过同时估计流形来实现能量最小化，并基于稳定性与采样假设给出收敛性分析。

Result: 在适当的稳定性和采样假设下，该方案收敛到局部极小值的一个邻域，且该邻域的大小随时间步长和采样误差趋近于零而消失。并以反问题中学习算子修正为例展示了方法的实际应用。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在未知流形上（由点云给定）进行能量最小化的梯度下降方法，具有收敛性保证，并可应用于逆问题中的算子修正学习等场景。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimising an energy over an unknown manifold that is given implicitly by a point cloud. For a known manifold one can define a gradient descent scheme analogously to the classical construction in Euclidean spaces. However, when the manifold is not known one has to simultaneously estimate the manifold whilst minimising the energy. We define a gradient descent scheme which remains in a neighbourhood of the manifold and, under suitable stability and sampling assumptions, converges to a neighbourhood of a local minimiser whose size vanishes as the time step and sampling errors vanish. As an example we show the application of the methodology to learning operator corrections in inverse problems.

</details>

### [180] [On the motivic cohomology of some singular rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05220)
*Tess Bouis*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用非A^1-不变母题上同调，结合积分p-adic Hodge理论，对若干奇异环（如Z/p^n、Z[x]/(x^e)以及紧Hausdorff空间上的连续函数环）的代数K-理论给出了母题层面的精细化结果，以替代标准K-理论中使用的迹方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代数K-理论计算依赖于迹方法（trace methods），而本文希望通过母题上同调提供更丰富的结构信息，实现对奇异环K-理论的母题精化，从而建立K-理论与母题上同调之间更深刻的联系。

Method: 使用非A^1-不变母题上同调作为工具，并以积分p-adic Hodge理论（integral p-adic Hodge theory）替代标准K-理论中的迹方法，对特定奇异环的K-理论已知结果进行母题层面的重构与精化。

Result: 成功证明了Z/p^n、Z[x]/(x^e)及紧Hausdorff空间上连续函数环的代数K-理论计算的母题精细化版本。

Conclusion: 积分p-adic Hodge理论可以有效地替代迹方法，在非A^1-不变母题上同调框架下为奇异环的K-理论提供母题精化，拓展了母题上同调在奇异环情形的适用性。

Abstract: Using non-$\mathbb{A}^1$-invariant motivic cohomology, we prove motivic refinements of certain known computations of the algebraic $K$-theory of singular rings, such as rings of the form $\mathbb{Z}/p^n$, $\mathbb{Z}[x]/(x^e)$, and $\mathscr{C}(X;\mathbb{C})$ for $X$ a compact Hausdorff space. These refinements are made possible by the use of integral $p$-adic Hodge theory, as a replacement for the standard use of trace methods in $K$-theory.

</details>


### [181] [Elliptic matroids and modular curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05299)
*Matthew Baker*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究秩3拟阵$T_n$（其基为$\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z}$上非零和的三元子集）与模曲线$X_1(n)$之间的深刻联系。作者对$n\geq 10$给出了纯代数且基于关联几何的证明，建立了$T_n$的$k$-实现类与$X_1(n)^\circ(k)$之间的自然双射；随后将域值对应升级为$\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$上的概形同构，利用形变理论论证在Artin局部环值点上验证同构。该结果使$X_1(n)^\circ$获得作为拟阵实现空间的自然$\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$模型，并刻画了素数阶情形与Mazur有理挠点定理的等价性。附录还将实现空间从$\mathbb{Z}$上的仿射概形提升为$\mathbb{F}_1^{\pm}$上的仿射带概形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borisov和Roulleau在复几何中发现了模曲线$X_1(n)$与某个秩3拟阵$T_n$的实现空间之间的关联。本文的动机是将这一结果推广到任意域并在数论/代数几何层面深化：一方面给出纯代数且不依赖复解析方法的证明；另一方面将域值对应升级为整体的概形同构，从而建立模曲线的算术模型，并揭示拟阵不可表示性与Mazur定理等经典数论结果之间的等价关系。

Method: 方法主要包括两个层面：(1) 对$n\geq 10$，通过纯代数与incidence-theoretic（关联几何）论证，建立$T_n$的$k$-实现类与$X_1(n)^\circ(k)$之间的自然双射；(2) 将域值对应提升为$\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$上的概形同构，关键技术是形变理论论证——通过在Artin局部环值点上验证同构来整体化。附录中进一步利用Baker-Jin-Lorscheid的带概形（band scheme）理论，将实现空间升级为$\mathbb{F}_1^{\pm}$上的仿射带概形。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对任意域$k$（$\mathrm{char}(k)\nmid n$），$n\geq 10$时$X_1(n)^\circ(k)$与$T_n$的$k$-实现类之间存在自然双射，将Borisov-Roulleau的复几何定理推广到一般域的情形；(2) 该对应可提升为$\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$上的概形同构，从而$X_1(n)^\circ$获得拟阵实现空间的自然模型；(3) 对素数$p\geq 11$，$T_p$在$\mathbb{Q}$上的不可表示性与Mazur关于椭圆曲线有理挠点的经典定理素数阶情形等价；(4) 实现空间可提升为$\mathbb{F}_1^{\pm}$上的仿射带概形。

Conclusion: 本文建立了拟阵理论与模曲线理论之间更深层的算术联系。通过将Borisov-Roulleau的复几何结果推广为$k$上的一般代数对应，并升级为$\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$上的概形同构，作者展示了拟阵实现空间作为模曲线算术模型的自然性与普适性。特别地，素数阶情形下拟阵不可表示性与Mazur定理的等价性揭示了组合结构与算术几何深层结构间令人惊讶的关联，而$\mathbb{F}_1^{\pm}$上的带概形推广则指向了更广阔的理论前景。

Abstract: For $n\geq 4$, let $T_n$ be the rank-3 matroid on $\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z}$ whose bases are the three-element non-zero-sum subsets. Let $X_1(n)^\circ$ denote the open subscheme of the modular curve $X_1(n)$ obtained by removing the cusps corresponding to reducible Néron polygons. For $n \geq 10$, we give a purely algebraic and incidence-theoretic proof that, for every field $k$ with $\mathrm{char}(k)$ not dividing $n$, there is a natural bijection between $X_1(n)^\circ(k)$ and rescaling classes of $k$-realizations of $T_n$. For $k = \mathbb{C}$, this recovers a theorem of Borisov and Roulleau.
  We then upgrade the field-valued correspondence to an isomorphism of schemes over $\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$. The main new ingredient is a deformation-theoretic argument which allows us to verify the isomorphism on points valued in Artinian local rings. As consequences, the modular curve $X_1(n)^\circ$ acquires a natural model over $\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$ as a matroid realization space, and, for primes $p \geq 11$, the non-representability of $T_p$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is equivalent to the prime-order case of Mazur's celebrated theorem on rational torsion points of elliptic curves.
  In an appendix, we explain how to upgrade the realization space of a matroid from an affine scheme over $\mathbb{Z}$ to an affine band scheme (in the sense of Baker-Jin-Lorscheid) over $\mathbb{F}_1^{\pm}$.

</details>


### [182] [The weak Markus--Yamabe conjecture fails in dimension 14](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05392)
*Álvaro Castañeda,Gerardo Honorato,Francisco Valenzuela-Henríquez*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱Markus-Yamabe猜想在所有维度n≥14时均不成立。通过建立链实现定理，将任何多项式Keller映射转化为具有特定谱性质的向量场，并利用Jacobian猜想的反例构造出度数为7、定义于R^14的Hurwitz向量场（含三个有理奇点），以及通过降秩齐次化得到的度数为3、定义于R^18的Hurwitz场。维度3≤n≤13仍为开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证弱Markus-Yamabe猜想的正确性——该猜想断言若多项式向量场在每一点的Jacobian矩阵谱均位于左半平面，则该向量场是全局吸引子（只有一个奇异点）。研究者试图找到该猜想的反例，以确定其在任意维度上的成立范围。

Method: 首先证明链实现定理：将任意多项式Keller映射F=I+H（R^n上，分量次数为d_1,...,d_n）显式构造为R^N上的多项式向量场（N=Σmax(d_i,2)-n），使得该向量场的Jacobian矩阵在所有点上谱为{-1}，且其奇异点与F的任意给定纤维一一对应。然后将该定理应用于Jacobian猜想的最近反例，得到R^14上的度数7 Hurwitz向量场。此外，通过所关联的三次稳定的降秩齐次化，独立地得到R^18上的度数3 Hurwitz场。

Result: 成功构造出弱Markus-Yamabe猜想的反例：R^14上的Hurwitz向量场（度数7，含三个有理奇异点，对应Jacobian猜想反例的纤维），以及R^18上的Hurwitz向量场（度数3）。由此在维度n≥14时证明弱Markus-Yamabe猜想不成立。3≤n≤13的维度情形仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 弱Markus-Yamabe猜想在维度n≥14时失败，这是通过链实现定理将Jacobian猜想的反例系统性地转化为反例得到的。文中同时提供了两个独立的维度（14和18）的反例构造，而维度3到13之间的成立性仍待解决。

Abstract: We show that the weak Markus--Yamabe conjecture fails in every dimension $n\geq14$. We first prove a chain realization theorem: every polynomial Keller map $F=\I+H$ of $\R^n$ with component degrees $d_1,\ldots,d_n$ yields an explicit polynomial vector field on $\R^N$, with $N=\sum_i\max(d_i,2)-n$, whose Jacobian matrix has spectrum $\{-1\}$ at every point and whose singularities are in bijection with any prescribed fiber of $F$. Applied to the recent counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture, this gives a Hurwitz vector field of degree seven on $\R^{14}$ with three rational singularities. A rank-reduced dehomogenization of the associated cubic stabilization gives, independently, a Hurwitz field of degree three on $\R^{18}$. The dimensions $3\leq n\leq13$ remain open.

</details>


### [183] [Elliptic curve counting in toric threefolds: virtual, enumerative, and tropical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05486)
*Sae Koyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究三维toric流形中椭圆曲线的枚举几何，引入称为"well-spaced counts"的整数不变量，通过well-spaced genus-one tropical曲线进行研究，并与logarithmic degeneration公式比较，建立了logarithmic virtual invariants与几何不变量之间的显式关系，得到Getzler--Pandharipande公式在$\mathbb{P}^3$情形的logarithmic类比。作为应用，证明了$\mathbb{P}^3$关于其toric边界的virtual logarithmic invariants在次数足够大时严格小于普通Gromov--Witten不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究toric三维流形中椭圆曲线的枚举几何，期望建立logarithmic virtual invariants与更几何的整数不变量（well-spaced counts）之间的显式联系，并推广Getzler--Pandharipande在$\mathbb{P}^3$上的经典公式，同时揭示logarithmic Gromov--Witten不变量与普通GW不变量之间的差异。

Method: 利用well-spaced genus-one tropical曲线在$\mathbb{R}^3$中研究计数问题，通过与logarithmic degeneration公式进行比较，推导出logarithmic virtual invariants与well-spaced counts之间的显式关系式。这一方法本质上是通过tropical化将问题转化为组合计数，再利用退化解技术建立代数侧的联系。

Result: 得到了$\mathbb{P}^3$情形下Getzler--Pandharipande公式的logarithmic类比；证明了对$\mathbb{P}^3$关于其toric边界的virtual logarithmic invariants，在次数充分大时严格小于普通Gromov--Witten不变量。文中还包含若干计算示例。

Conclusion: 本文建立了toric三维流形上椭圆曲线logarithmic virtual invariants与几何整数不变量之间的显式关系，推广了经典结果，并揭示了logarithmic GW不变量与普通GW不变量在较高次数下的系统性差异，为理解logarithmic enumerative geometry提供了新的定量视角。

Abstract: We study the enumerative geometry of elliptic curves in toric threefolds. We consider enumerative integer invariants, called well-spaced counts, which can be studied using well-spaced genus-one tropical curves in $\mathbb{R}^3$. By comparing this with the logarithmic degeneration formula, we obtain an explicit relationship between logarithmic virtual invariants and these geometric invariants. The result is a logarithmic analogue of a formula of Getzler--Pandharipande for elliptic curves in $\mathbb{P}^3$. As an application, we show that the virtual logarithmic invariants for $\mathbb{P}^3$ with respect to its toric boundary are strictly less than the ordinary Gromov--Witten invariants once the degree is sufficiently large. Several examples are included.

</details>


### [184] [On the Numerical Terao Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05662)
*Piotr Pokora*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明数值Terao猜想对仅含ADE奇点的偶数次圆锥-线排列成立，同时通过构造一个九次反例（包含七条直线和一个光滑圆锥，具相同弱组合数据）证明该猜想在允许普通四重点的拟齐次情形下不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究数值Terao猜想成立的边界条件，即在更一般的几何环境中检验该猜想是否仍然成立，并考察其最强形式是否有效。

Method: 作者先证明数值Terao猜想对偶数次、仅具有ADE奇点的圆锥-线排列成立；随后在允许普通四重点的拟齐次情形下，构造两个具有相同弱组合数据的九次排列（七条线和一条光滑圆锥），其中一个为自由排列（指数(4,4)），另一个为近自由排列（指数(3,6)）。

Result: 构造出两个具有相同弱组合数据 W(CL)=(7,1; 8A1+D4+4X9) 但自由性不同的排列：一个是自由排列，指数为(4,4)；另一个是近自由排列，指数为(3,6)。这为数值Terao猜想最强的已知表述提供了一个反例。

Conclusion: 数值Terao猜想在仅含ADE奇点的偶数次圆锥-线排列中成立，但在允许普通四重点的拟齐次圆锥-线排列中不成立，最强形式的数值Terao猜想被证伪。

Abstract: We prove that the Numerical Terao Conjecture holds for even-degree conic-line arrangements having only ADE singularities. We then show that the conjecture fails in the broader quasi-homogeneous setting once ordinary quadruple points are admitted, by constructing a degree-nine counterexample, each consisting of seven lines and one smooth conic, with the same weak combinatorics \[ W(\mathcal{CL}) = (7,1;\,8A_1+D_4+4X_9). \] We show that one curve is free with exponents $(4,4)$, whereas the other is nearly free with exponents $(3,6)$. This yields a counterexample to the strongest known formulation of the Numerical Terao Conjecture.

</details>


### [185] [Real Structures in the Moduli of Projective Models of K3-Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05941)
*Çisem Güneş Aktaş*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有指定简单奇点的K3曲面族中何时包含实代数曲面，发展了一个显式且统一的算法来检测等奇点层中的实代表，并应用于空间四次曲面和平面六次曲线的实可表示性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K3曲面族中实代数曲面的存在性是代数几何中的一个基础问题。在具有简单奇点的K3曲面族中，判断其等奇点层是否包含实曲面此前缺乏显式且统一的判定方法，尤其在空间四次曲面情形下没有完整的结果。

Method: 论文发展了一个显式且统一的算法，用于检测任意等奇点层中的实代表，该算法适用于所有极化（polarizations）。该算法被应用于空间四次曲面（spatial quartics）的实可表示性问题。

Result: 对于空间四次曲面，证明了除三种例外类型外，所有实等奇点层都包含实曲面，从而完成了该情形下实可表示性的研究。此外，该方法还重现了平面六次曲线中已知的唯一无实代表的简单六次曲线等奇点层。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个适用于所有极化的统一算法，成功解决了空间四次曲面实可表示性问题（除三种例外类型外全部存在实代表），并验证了该方法在平面六次曲线情形下与已知结果一致，说明该算法具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: This paper investigates when a complex family of K3-surfaces with prescribed simple singularities contains real algebraic surfaces. We develop an explicit and uniform algorithm for detecting real representatives in any equisingular stratum, valid for all polarizations. As a primary application, we resolve the problem for spatial quartics, showing that all real equisingular strata contain real surfaces except for three exceptional types, thereby completing the study of real representability in this case. Our method also revisits the analogous phenomenon in the realm of plane sextics, recovering the unique known real stratum of simple sextics without real representatives.

</details>


### [186] [About finite differential tropical basis for linear ODE's](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06018)
*Stefano Mereta,Alejandro Vargas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关于线性常微分方程热带化（tropicalization）的几个开放问题，主要目标是发展计算其经典解收敛半径的方法。研究的核心是刻画允许有限微分热带基（finite differential tropical basis）的方程类别，初步探索聚焦于二阶和三阶情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发计算线性ODE经典解收敛半径的方法，为此需要理解热带化方法中微分热带基的性质，并确定哪些方程类别支持有限微分热带基。

Method: 提出若干关于线性ODE热带化的开放问题，以有限微分热带基（源于Fink和Toghani, 2022）的存在性为核心，从二阶和三阶方程入手进行初步探索。

Result: 尚处于问题提出与初始探索阶段，主要贡献是系统性地列出开放问题，并对二阶、三阶情形进行了初步考察。

Conclusion: 该研究为热带几何方法应用于线性ODE分析（特别是收敛半径计算）奠定了基础，但成果目前主要停留在问题框架构建和低阶情形的初步探索层面。

Abstract: We formulate several open questions regarding the tropicalization of linear ODEs, aiming primarily to develop methods for calculating the radius of convergence of their classical solutions. To this aim it is of foremost importance to characterize the classes of equations that admit a finite differential tropical basis, as introduced in (Fink and Toghani, 2022). Our initial exploration examines the second- and third-order cases.

</details>


### [187] [Fundamental groups of the complements to reducible curves on smooth surfaces with restricted classes of components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06136)
*A. Libgober*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对光滑单连通射影曲面上线束的等奇异族进行分类，这些线束的对偶簇次数不超过6，且包含足够多由超平面截面或其不可约分量组成的成员。作为推论，描述了满足上述条件的曲线上补集的基本群，其基本群存在到秩大于6的自由群的本质满射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超平面截面及其不可约分量是研究射影曲面上曲线簇的奇异性和对偶簇基本性质的基础对象。通过对对偶簇次数较低（不超过6）的等奇异线束族进行分类，可以深入理解这类曲线的结构，并进一步揭示其补集基本群的代数性质。

Method: 采用分类学方法，利用对偶簇次数的上界约束来枚举和分类光滑单连通射影曲面上的等奇异线束族。通过对线束成员的结构分析（判断其不可约分量是否来自超平面截面），结合基本群理论中的本质满射技术，推导出补集基本群的结构结论。

Result: 完成了对偶簇次数≤6的等奇异线束族的完整分类；并证明了在此条件下，若曲面上的曲线补集基本群存在到秩>6的自由群的本质满射，则可明确描述这些基本群的结构。

Conclusion: 该分类结果建立了对偶簇次数约束与基本群代数性质之间的内在联系，为研究射影曲面上曲线补集的基本群提供了新的分类工具和结构性刻画。

Abstract: We classify equisingular families of pencils of curves on smooth simply connected projective surfaces whose dual varieties have degree at most 6, and which contain sufficiently large number of members which irreducible components are either hyperplane sections or irreducible components of hyperplane sections. As a consequence, we describe the fundamental groups of complements to curves on such surfaces whose irreducible components satisfy the above condition and whose fundamental groups admit an essential surjection onto a free group of rank greater than 6.

</details>


### [188] [Compactification Independence of the Irregular Hodge Filtration on Deligne-Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06234)
*Haoxu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于光滑分离的Deligne-Mumford栈上正则函数w的扭曲de Rham上同调滤过的紧致化不依赖性定理，并将该结果应用于轨道簇不奇Hodge数的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yu的工作利用紧致化定义了DM栈上扭曲de Rham上同调的滤过，并由Kontsevich格计算该滤过。但紧致化上的扩展函数w可能仅为有理函数，且紧致化的选择可能影响滤过的定义，因此需要证明滤过不依赖于紧致化的选择，以保证其为栈Landau-Ginzburg模型的内在不变量。

Method: 证明方法采用好的分辨率（good resolutions）和栈弱分解定理（stacky weak factorization），将紧致化之间的比较归结为两类初等操作：沿边界除子的爆破（blowups）和根（roots）。针对这两种操作分别建立Yu复形与Kontsevich复形的滤过比较定理，从而证明滤过不依赖紧致化。

Result: 证明了由紧致化定义的滤过与紧致化选择无关。该独立性的证明分两个层面：一是通过栈弱分解将任意两个紧致化之间的比较化为爆破与根操作的复合；二是分别证明沿边界除子的爆破和根操作下Yu复形与Kontsevich复形的滤过保持同构。此外，该结果按扇区（sector by sector）适用于Harder-Lee的轨道簇Clarke镜像对研究，表明由此得到的轨道簇滤过和不奇Hodge数仅依赖于栈Landau-Ginzburg模型本身。

Conclusion: 紧致化独立性定理确立了DM栈上Yu滤过（及Kontsevich格计算）作为栈Landau-Ginzburg模型内在不变量的地位，并为Harder-Lee框架中轨道簇不奇Hodge数的良定义性提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Let $(\mathscr U,w)$ be a smooth separated Deligne--Mumford stack of finite type over $\mathbb C$, with a regular function $w$. Following Yu, one can use a compactification to define a filtration on its twisted de Rham cohomology. The same compactification gives Kontsevich lattices that compute this filtration. The extension of $w$ on the compactification may be only rational. Only a local nondegeneracy condition near the polar divisor is imposed.
  We prove that the resulting filtration does not depend on the compactification. The proof uses good resolutions and stacky weak factorization. This reduces the comparison to blowups and roots along boundary divisors. Filtered comparison theorems for the Yu and Kontsevich complexes are established for both operations.
  Harder and Lee use orbifold irregular Hodge numbers in their study of stacky Clarke mirror pairs. Compactification independence applies sector by sector to their setting. Thus the resulting orbifold filtration and irregular Hodge numbers depend only on the stack Landau--Ginzburg model.

</details>


### [189] [Frobenius-orbit slicing and uniform elimination of positive-dimensional singular loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06273)
*Yutong Zhang,Yaoran Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对固定整拟射影子概形X⊂P^n_Z（在Z上光滑，相对维数r），给出了限制均匀d次形式到X_p的m阶主部喷流的零概形具有正维数的概率上界C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}，其中N_m=binom(r+m,m)，λ_m(d)=floor(m(d+1)/(m+1))。该结果解决了Poonen的算术Bertini猜想5.2，对c个独立超曲面还给出了Jacobian秩退化轨迹的概率界。证明采用滤过Q-adic分解、三角正规Taylor块和Jacobian枢轴图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在建立算术几何中Bertini型奇异轨迹的概率上界估计。经典Bertini定理保证一般超曲面的光滑性，但在算术场景下需要定量估计奇异轨迹出现的概率，特别需要解决Poonen在2004年提出的算术Bertini猜想5.2，并为此发展出统一的概率界框架。

Method: 证明方法包括：(1) 滤过的Q-adic分解将问题约化到p-adic局部分析；(2) 三角正规Taylor块用于控制喷流在约化点处的结构；(3) Jacobian枢轴图的均匀控制复杂度，用于对Jacobian秩退化事件进行概率计数。对于高阶（m阶）喷流，通过组合计数N_m和指数λ_m(d)=floor(m(d+1)/(m+1))来刻画概率衰减速率。

Result: 主要结果：单超曲面的m阶喷流零概形为正维的概率不超过C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}。特别地，m=1时得到Bertini奇异轨迹估计C(d+1)^{r+1}p^{-ceil(d/2)}。该结果解决了Poonen的算术Bertini猜想5.2；提高次数阈值后对任意固定A>0可得到p^{-A}的概率界。对c≤r个独立超曲面，Jacobian秩退化轨迹概率同时受CΣ_i(d_i+1)^{r+1}p^{-ceil(d_i/2)}和C'(d_min+1)^{r+1}p^{-ceil(d_min/2)}控制。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立统一的概率估计框架，完整解决了Poonen的算术Bertini猜想，并将结果推广到高阶喷流与多个超曲面的情形。所获得的概率界不仅验证了预期的最优指数衰减，还展示了方法在高阶与多重情形下的普适性，为算术几何中奇异轨迹的概率研究提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: Let $\cX\subseteq\Pj^n_{\Z}$ be a fixed integral quasiprojective subscheme, smooth over $\Z$ of relative dimension $r$. For each fixed $m\ge1$, we bound the probability that the $m$th principal-parts jet of the restriction of a uniform degree-$d$ form to $\cX_p$ has a positive-dimensional zero scheme. The bound is $C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}$, where $N_m=\binom{r+m}{m}$ and $λ_m(d)=\floor{m(d+1)/(m+1)}$. For $m=1$, this gives the Bertini singular-locus estimate $C(d+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d/2}}$. It settles Poonen's arithmetic Bertini Conjecture~5.2 and, after increasing the degree threshold, yields $p^{-A}$ for every fixed $A>0$. For $c\le r$ independent hypersurfaces, the probability of a positive-dimensional Jacobian rank-degeneracy locus is bounded both by $C\sum_i(d_i+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d_i/2}}$ and by $C'(d_{\min}+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d_{\min}/2}}$. The proof uses filtered $Q$-adic decompositions, triangular normal Taylor blocks, and Jacobian-pivot charts of uniformly controlled complexity.

</details>


### [190] [The graded Betti numbers are not tropical invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06284)
*Noah Laikin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明分次Betti数并非齐次理想热带化的不变量，也非值域上射影代数簇热带化的不变量。通过构造一族饱和理想，它们对应相同的热带理想，但其中一个成员的Betti表与其他成员不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带几何是代数几何的组合化工具，但热带化过程可能丢失某些代数几何精细不变量（如分次Betti数）。研究者希望探明热带化在何种程度上保留了原对象的同调信息，特别是分次Betti数是否在热带化下保持。

Method: 构造一族饱和理想，所有这些理想实现同一个热带理想，但其中某个成员的Betti表与其他成员不同，从而以具体反例证明分次Betti数不是热带化的不变量。

Result: 找到了一个具体的反例族：一族饱和理想共享同一个热带理想，但其中恰好一个成员的Betti表与其他成员不同，从而证明了分次Betti数不是热带理想的不变量。

Conclusion: 分次Betti数不能作为热带化（包括齐次理想的热带化和射影代数簇的热带化）的不变量；热带化在代数结构的精细同调信息方面存在信息损失。

Abstract: We show that the graded Betti numbers are not invariants of the tropicalisation of a homogeneous ideal, or the tropicalisation of a projective algebraic scheme over a valued field. We do this by producing a family of saturated ideals that all realise the same tropical ideal with one member having a distinct Betti table from the rest.

</details>


### [191] [Stability of kernel bundles on projective bundles over curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06365)
*Abel Castorena,George H. Hitching*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究射影丛X上的相对超平面丛的核丛稳定性。当底曲线C、射影丛X和生成子空间V在模空间中是一般的，且相对超平面丛充分丰富时，证明系统(O_X(1),V)的核丛是O_X(1)-稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核丛的稳定性是代数几何中的核心问题，与向量丛的模空间结构、线性系理论以及Brill-Noether理论密切相关。该结果推广了经典情形（射影空间或曲面上）中核丛稳定性的已知结论，为更高维射影丛上的线性系研究提供理论支撑。

Method: 采用模空间中的一般性论证方法，通过对底曲线C、射影丛X及生成子空间V施加一般性假设，结合充分丰富的条件，利用稳定性判据和模参数的分析来证明核丛的O_X(1)-稳定性。

Result: 证明了在一般模参数条件下，核丛是O_X(1)-稳定的。这一结果对亏格g≥3的任意光滑曲线上的射影丛均成立，且不依赖于射影丛的具体结构，只要求相对超平面丛充分丰富。

Conclusion: 该定理建立了射影丛上线性系统核丛稳定性的充分条件，对理解更高维代数簇上向量丛的模空间及Hilbert方案的结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a projective bundle over a smooth curve $C$ of genus $g \ge 3$, and consider the relative hyperplane bundle ${\mathcal O}_X (1) \to X$. Let $V \subseteq H^0 ( X , {\mathcal O}_X (1) )$ be a generating subspace. We prove that when $C$, $X$ and $V$ are general in moduli and ${\mathcal O}_X (1)$ is sufficiently ample, the kernel bundle of the system $({\mathcal O}_X (1) , V)$ is ${\mathcal O}_X (1)$-stable.

</details>
